12. (new - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) The crime is committed "by two or more persons" when at least two persons have participated in the actual execution.
13. (new - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) International protection is enjoyed by persons for whom such protection is provided in an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party.
14. (new - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000) "Taxes in large amounts" are those that exceed three thousand leva, and "taxes in particularly large amounts" are those that exceed twelve thousand leva.
15. (new - SG, issue 7 of 1999) "Foreign official" is one who performs:
a) service in an institution of a foreign state;
b) functions entrusted by a foreign state, including by a foreign state enterprise or organization;
c) (supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002) service or commission entrusted by an international organization, as well as service in an international parliamentary assembly or international court;
d) (new - SG, issue 74 of 2015) functions of an arbitrator entrusted in accordance with the law of a foreign state.
16. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
17. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
18. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
19. (new - SG, issue 21 of 2000, repealed - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
20. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) "Organized criminal group" is a structured permanent association of three or more persons with the purpose of committing in a coordinated manner in the country or abroad crimes for which a punishment of deprivation of liberty of more than three years is provided. The association is structured even without the presence of a formal distribution of functions among the participants, duration of participation or developed structure.
21. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Information system" is any separate device or set of interconnected or similar devices which, in execution of a specific program, provides or one of the elements of which provides automatic processing of data, as well as the computer data stored, processed, retrieved or transmitted by such device or group of devices for the purpose of operating with such data and using, protecting and maintaining them.
22. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer data" is any representation of facts, information or concepts in a form amenable to processing in information systems, including a program that is capable of causing a given information system to perform a specific function.
23. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Provider of computer-information services" is any legal or natural person that offers the possibility of communication through an information system or that processes or stores computer data for this communication service or for its users.
24. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, supplemented - SG, issue 84 of 2023) "Payment instrument" is a tangible or intangible means that allows, independently or in connection with another means, the transfer of money or monetary values.
25. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer network" is a set of interconnected information systems or facilities that enables the exchange of computer data.
26. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer program" is a series of machine instructions that are capable of causing an information system to perform specific functions.
27. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 101 of 2017) "Computer virus" is a computer program that spreads automatically and against the will or without the knowledge of persons using information systems and is intended to bring information systems or computer networks into states undesired by those using them or to produce undesired results.
28. (new - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 74 of 2015) "Pornographic material" is material prepared in any manner, indecent, unacceptable or incompatible with public morals, the content of which depicts real or simulated lewd act, copulation, sexual intercourse, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, as well as lascivious display of the sexual organs of a person.
29. (new - SG, issue 107 of 2014, in force from 01.01.2015) "Mandatory insurance contributions for state social insurance or for health insurance in large amounts" are those that exceed three thousand leva, and "mandatory insurance contributions for state social insurance or for health insurance in particularly large amounts" are those that exceed twelve thousand leva.
30. (new - SG, issue 74 of 2015) "Pornographic performance" is a live or real-time presentation before another of a lascivious display of the sexual organs of a person who has not reached 18 years of age, or of the participation of such person in a real or simulated lewd act, copulation, sexual intercourse, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism.
31. (new - SG, issue 16 of 2019, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) The crime is committed "in conditions of domestic violence" if it is committed through the exercise of physical, sexual or psychological violence, placing in economic dependence, forced restriction of personal life, personal freedom and personal rights and is carried out against an ascendant, descendant, spouse or former spouse, person from whom there is a child, person with whom one is or has been in actual marital cohabitation, or person with whom one lives or has lived in one household.
32. (new - SG, issue 84 of 2023) "Financial or other means for financing terrorism" are any assets, financial, economic resources, tangible or intangible benefit, movable or immovable property, regardless of the manner of acquisition, documents or instruments, including in electronic or digital form, proving ownership or interest, funds, bank credits, traveler's checks, bank checks, money transfers, shares, securities, bonds, bills of exchange or letters of credit, interest, dividends or other income or value accrued or generated from such funds, which can potentially be used to obtain funds, goods or services.
Special Provision
Art. 94. The provisions of the general part of this code shall also apply to crimes provided for in other laws.
SPECIAL PART
Chapter One.
CRIMES AGAINST THE REPUBLIC


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Раздел I

Amendment
Art. 95. (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever, with the purpose of overthrowing, undermining or weakening the power in the republic, participates in the commission of an attempt at a coup for the violent seizure of power in the center or locally, or in a rebellion, or in an armed uprising, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 96. (Amended - SG, issue 41 of 1985) (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever, with the purpose of undermining or weakening the power in the republic or creating difficulties for it, deprives of life a state or public figure, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.
(2) Whoever with the same purpose causes grievous bodily harm to such a person shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
(3) (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose under para. 1, by arson, explosion, flooding or other generally dangerous act, causes the death of one or more persons, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from fifteen to twenty years, by life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 97. (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose specified in the preceding article commits a generally dangerous crime under Art. 349 or 350 shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 97a. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985) (1) Whoever with the purpose under Art. 96 detains someone as a hostage, whose release he makes dependent on the fulfillment of a specific condition by the state, by a state or public organization or by a third person, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(2) When in the cases under the preceding paragraph the perpetrator threatens that if the condition set by him is not fulfilled, he will cause death or grievous or moderate bodily harm to the detained person, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.

### Раздел II

Treason and espionage
Art. 98. (1) Whoever incites a foreign state or public group abroad to war or other hostile action against the Republic shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who commits an act with the purpose of provoking war or other hostile action against the Republic.
Art. 99. (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever deprives of life a representative of a foreign state with the purpose of provoking war or international complications against the republic shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.
(2) For grievous bodily harm to such a person with the same purpose, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
Art. 100. (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) A Bulgarian citizen who, during a declared or commenced war, voluntarily enters the ranks of an enemy army or armed group or participates in a hostile action against the Republic, or in any form goes over to the side of the enemy, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on a Bulgarian citizen who in any manner assists a foreign state or public group abroad in conducting military or other hostile actions against the Republic.
Art. 101. (1) A Bulgarian citizen who leaves the country or refuses to return to the country with the purpose of placing himself in the service of a foreign state or a foreign organization, to serve it to the detriment of the republic, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(2) If the act is committed by a serviceman, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
Art. 102. (1) Whoever with the purpose of reducing the defense capability of the republic provokes rebellion or insubordination in the Bulgarian army or desertion from it, or with the same purpose disrupts the preparation or supply thereof, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
(2) (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) If serious consequences have resulted from the act or if it is committed in wartime, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 103. (Supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever, performing state service or a commission before a foreign government or international organization, intentionally conducts them to the detriment of the republic, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from ten to fifteen years, as well as by deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 9.
Art. 104. (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004) Whoever discloses or collects with the purpose of disclosing to a foreign state or a foreign organization information constituting a state secret shall be punished for espionage by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.
(2) If the perpetrator voluntarily discloses the committed crime to the authorities, he shall be punished under mitigating circumstances.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 99 of 1989, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004) Information constituting a state secret shall be determined by law.
Art. 105. (1) Whoever places himself in the service of a foreign state or a foreign organization to serve it as a spy, if he has not committed an act under the preceding article, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
(2) The perpetrator shall not be punished if he voluntarily discloses himself to the authorities.

### Раздел III

Sabotage and wrecking
Art. 106. (Supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever with the purpose of weakening the power or creating difficulties for it destroys or damages public buildings, structures, installations, facilities, means of transport or communication or other significant public property shall be punished for sabotage by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years, and in particularly serious cases - by deprivation of liberty of twenty years, by life imprisonment or by life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 107. Whoever with the purpose of weakening the power or creating difficulties for it disrupts or undermines industry, transport, agriculture, the monetary and credit system, other economic sectors or individual economic enterprises, by using state institutions, economic enterprises or public organizations, by obstructing their activity, or by failing to fulfill important economic tasks assigned to him, shall be punished for wrecking by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years, and in particularly serious cases - by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.

### Section IV

Other crimes
Art. 108. (Am. - SG, No. 41 of 1985, am. - SG, No. 99 of 1989) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) Whoever preaches fascist or other anti-democratic ideology or violent change of the social and state order established by the Constitution of the Republic of Bulgaria, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, suppl. - SG, No. 95 of 2016) Whoever in any way desecrates the coat of arms, flag or anthem of the Republic of Bulgaria, or the flag or anthem of the European Union, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by a fine of up to three thousand leva.
Art. 108a. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, suppl. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever, with the purpose of creating turmoil and fear in the population or threatening or compelling an authority, a public representative or a representative of a foreign state or an international organization to perform or omit something or to cause serious destabilization or destruction of fundamental political, constitutional, economic or social structures of the state or an international organization, commits a crime under Art. 115, Art. 116, para. 1, item 1, Art. 128, Art. 142, Art. 142a, para. 3, item 2, Art. 143, Art. 143a, Art. 144, para. 2, Art. 170, para. 3, Art. 216, para. 1 and 5, Art. 216a, para. 2 or 3, Art. 319b - 319g, Art. 326, Art. 330, Art. 333, Art. 334, Art. 336a, Art. 337, Art. 339, Art. 340, Art. 341a, Art. 341b, Art. 341v, Art. 344, Art. 347, para. 1, Art. 348, Art. 349, Art. 350, Art. 352, para. 1, 2 and 3, Art. 354, Art. 356e, Art. 356z, Art. 356k, Art. 356l and Art. 356m, shall be punished for terrorism by imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and when death has been caused - by imprisonment from fifteen to thirty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever in any way, directly or indirectly collects or provides financial or other means, knowing or assuming that they will be used wholly or partially:
1. (suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) for the commission or preparation for the commission of crimes under para. 1, 3, 4 and para. 6 - 8 or Art. 111;
2. by an organization or group that sets itself the goal of committing an act under para. 1 or 3;
3. (suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) by a person who participated in the commission or preparation of crimes under para. 1, 3, 4 and para. 6 - 8 or Art. 111,
(suppl. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from five thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever recruits or trains individuals or groups of people with the purpose of participating in the commission or preparation for a crime under para. 1 or under Art. 109, or in any way assisting another person or group of persons to commit such acts, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to ten years.
(4) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever trains with the purpose under para. 3, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to eight years.
(5) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The perpetrator under para. 4 shall not be punished if he voluntarily discloses himself to the authorities before a crime under para. 1 has been committed.
(6) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever crosses the border of the country with the purpose of participating in the commission or preparation for a crime under para. 1 - 4, under Art. 109, Art. 111, para. 1, or in any way assisting another person or group of persons to commit such acts, including against another state, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to ten years.
(7) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) The punishment under para. 6 shall also be imposed on a foreigner who, with the purpose under para. 6, illegally resides in the country.
(8) (New - SG, No. 84 of 2023) The punishment under para. 6 shall also be imposed on whoever organizes or facilitates travel for the purpose of terrorism of the persons under para. 6 or 7.
(9) (Previous para. 3 - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 74 of 2015, previous para. 8 - SG, No. 84 of 2023) The object of the crime under para. 2 shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 109. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 99 of 1989, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever forms or leads an organization or group that sets itself the goal of committing crimes under this chapter, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to twelve years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever is a member of such an organization or group, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to ten years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.
(3) (New - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) When the organization or group sets itself the goal of committing a crime under Art. 108a, the punishment is:
1. under para. 1 - imprisonment from ten to twenty years;
2. under para. 2 - imprisonment from two to ten years.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, previous para. 3 - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) A participant in the organization or group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities, discloses everything known to him about the organization or group and thereby substantially facilitates the detection and proof of crimes committed by it under this chapter, shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55.
(5) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) A participant in the organization or group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities and discloses the organization or group before another crime under this chapter has been committed by it or by him, shall not be punished.
Art. 110. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 99 of 1989, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, previous text of Art. 110 - SG, No. 74 of 2015, suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) For preparation for a crime under Art. 95, 96, 99, 106, 107, Art. 108a, para. 1, 6 - 8 and Art. 111, para. 1, the punishment is imprisonment for up to six years.
(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) A foreigner who on the territory of the country prepares to commit abroad a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to six years, but not more than the punishment provided in Art. 108a, para. 1.
Art. 111. (Rep. - SG, No. 99 of 1989, new - SG, No. 84 of 2023) (1) Whoever threatens to commit a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1 - 4 and 6 - 8 and this threat could cause reasonable apprehension for its realization, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to four years.
(2) If the act under para. 1 has been committed by a leader of an organization or group under Art. 109, para. 1, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) Whoever publicly incites the commission of a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1 - 4 and 6 - 8 through preaching before a multitude of people, through distribution of printed works or in another way, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to ten years, but with no heavier punishment than that provided for the crime itself.
Art. 112. (Rep. - SG, No. 99 of 1989)
Art. 112a. (New - SG, No. 41 of 1985, rep. - SG, No. 99 of 1989)
Art. 113. (Rep. - SG, No. 99 of 1989)
Supplementary provisions
Art. 114. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) For crimes under this chapter, the court may order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 - 10.
(2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) For crimes under Art. 95 - 107, Art. 108a and 109, the court may order confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
Chapter Two.
CRIMES AGAINST THE PERSON


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section I

Murder
Art. 115. Whoever intentionally kills another shall be punished for murder by imprisonment from ten to twenty years.
Art. 116. (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, supplemented - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) For murder:
1. of an official, of a representative of the public, as well as of a military person, including from an allied or friendly state or army, during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function or of a person enjoying international protection;
2. (amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) by an official, as well as by a representative of the public, by a police officer during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function;
3. of a father or of a mother, as well as of a natural son or of a natural daughter;
4. (supplemented - SG, issue 62 of 1997) of a pregnant woman, of a minor or of more than one person;
5. of a person who is in a helpless state;
6. by a method or with means dangerous to the life of many, in a particularly painful manner for the victim or with particular cruelty;
6a. (new - SG, issue 16 of 2019) committed in conditions of domestic violence;
7. with a mercenary purpose;
8. with the purpose of facilitating or concealing another crime;
8a. (new - SG, issue 84 of 2013) with the purpose of removing a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid from the victim;
9. committed with premeditation;
10. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002) committed by a person who acts on order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
11. (former item 10 - SG, issue 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 67 of 2023) committed with hooligan, racist, xenophobic or related to sexual orientation motives and
12. (amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998, former item 11, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004) constituting dangerous recidivism or committed by a person who has committed another intentional murder under the previous or present article, for which no sentence has been imposed,
(supplemented - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) the punishment is imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
(2) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, supplemented - SG, issue 43 of 2005, in force from 01.09.2005, amended - SG, issue 64 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 80 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, supplemented - SG, issue 61 of 2013) For murder of a judge, prosecutor, investigator, police officer, investigating police officer, state bailiff, private bailiff and assistant private bailiff, as well as of a customs officer, of a revenue officer, of an officer of the Executive Forest Agency or of an officer of the Ministry of Environment and Water exercising control activity or of a medical specialist, of a teacher (educator) during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function the punishment is imprisonment from twenty to thirty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 117. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Preparation for murder is punished by imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) The same punishment is also imposed on one who incites another to murder.
Art. 118. (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) For murder committed in a state of strong irritation which has been provoked by the victim with violence, with grave insult or slander or with another unlawful act from which serious consequences have occurred or could have occurred for the perpetrator or his relatives, the punishment is: in the cases of Art. 115 - imprisonment from one to eight years, and in the cases of Art. 116, para. 1, items 1 - 6 - imprisonment from three to ten years.
Art. 119. For murder committed when exceeding the limits of necessary defense, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 120. For murder committed by a mother upon her newborn during childbirth or immediately after it, the punishment is imprisonment up to three years.
Art. 121. (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) For murder of a just-born newborn with a monstrous appearance the guilty parent is punished by imprisonment up to one year or by probation.
Art. 122. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever causes another's death by negligence shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If the death has been caused with a firearm or with a strongly acting poisonous substance or if death of two or more persons has been caused, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years.
Art. 123. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever causes another's death due to ignorance or negligent performance of an occupation or of another legally regulated activity constituting a source of increased danger shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever by negligence causes another's death through actions which fall within an occupation or activity under the previous paragraph which he has no right to exercise shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If in the cases under the previous paragraphs the perpetrator has been in a state of intoxication or if death of more than one person has been caused, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, and in particularly serious cases - imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(4) If the perpetrator after the act has done everything depending on him to save the victim, the punishment is: under para. 1 and 2 - imprisonment up to three years; under para. 3 - imprisonment up to five years, and in particularly serious cases - imprisonment from three to ten years.
Art. 124. (1) Whoever causes another's death by negligence as a consequence of intentionally inflicted bodily injury shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years for serious bodily injury, from two to eight years for medium bodily injury and up to five years for light bodily injury.
(2) (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, amended - SG, issue 89 of 1986) If the act under the previous paragraph has been committed in a state of strong irritation which has been provoked by the victim with violence, with grave insult or slander or with another unlawful act from which serious consequences have occurred or could have occurred for the perpetrator or his relatives, the punishment is: for serious bodily injury - imprisonment up to five years; for medium bodily injury - imprisonment up to three years; for light bodily injury - imprisonment up to two years.
(3) (New para. 3 - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) If the bodily injury from which death has resulted is dangerous recidivism, the punishment is: for serious bodily injury - imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and for medium bodily injury - imprisonment from three to ten years.
(4) (New - SG, issue 89 of 1986) When the act under para. 1 or 3 has been committed when exceeding the limits of necessary defense, the punishment is: for serious bodily injury - imprisonment up to five years, for medium bodily injury - imprisonment up to four years, and for light bodily injury - imprisonment up to two years.
Art. 125. A mother who by negligence causes the death of her unborn or just-born newborn is not punished.
Art. 126. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever with the consent of a pregnant woman kills the fetus and outside an accredited medical institution or in violation of the approved medical standards and rules of good medical practice shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) If the perpetrator does not have higher medical education or has killed the fetus of two or more women, the punishment is imprisonment up to eight years.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) If the act under the previous paragraphs has been committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
(4) The pregnant woman does not bear criminal liability under the previous paragraphs, including for incitement and aiding.
(5) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) If the killing of the fetus has been committed without the consent of the pregnant woman, the punishment is imprisonment from three to eight years.
(6) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) If in the last case the death of the pregnant woman has resulted, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twelve years.
Art. 127. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever in any manner assists or incites another to suicide and such or only an attempt follows shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) For the same crime committed against a minor or against a person whom the perpetrator knows to be incapable of directing his actions or not to understand the nature or significance of what has been done, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
(3) Whoever through cruel treatment or systematic humiliation of the dignity of a person in material or other dependence on him brings him to suicide or to an attempt at suicide, having allowed this, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(4) If the act under the previous paragraph has been committed by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment up to three years.

### Section II

Bodily Injury
Art. 128. (1) Whoever causes another person grievous bodily injury shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for three to ten years.
(2) Bodily injury is grievous if it has caused: prolonged disturbance of consciousness; permanent blindness in one or both eyes; permanent deafness; loss of speech; reproductive incapacity; disfigurement causing permanent disturbance of speech or of a sensory organ; loss of one kidney, the spleen or a lobe of the lung; loss or mutilation of a leg or arm; permanent general disturbance of health dangerous to life.
Art. 129. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever causes another person medium bodily injury shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years.
(2) Bodily injury is medium if it has caused: permanent weakening of sight or hearing; permanent impediment of speech, of movement of the limbs, of the neck or throat, of the functions of the sexual organs without causing reproductive incapacity; fracture of the jaw or knocking out of teeth without which chewing or speaking is impeded; disfigurement of the face or other parts of the body; permanent disturbance of health not dangerous to life, or disturbance of health temporarily dangerous to life; injuries penetrating the cranial, thoracic and abdominal cavity.
Art. 130. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever causes another person a disturbance of health, outside the cases of Art. 128 and 129, shall be punished for light bodily injury by deprivation of liberty for up to two years or by probation.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, corr. - SG, No. 31 of 1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) For light bodily injury consisting in causing pain or suffering without disturbance of health, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to six months or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(3) If in the cases of the preceding paragraphs the injured person has immediately returned to the perpetrator the same kind of bodily injury, the court may release both from punishment.
Art. 131. (1) For causing bodily injury:
1. (suppl. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) to an official, to a representative of the public, as well as to a military person, including from an allied or friendly state or army, during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function; or to a person enjoying international protection;
2. (am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) by an official, by a representative of the public, by a police officer during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function;
3. to a mother or to a father;
4. (suppl. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) to a pregnant woman, to a minor or to more than one person;
5. (am. - SG, No. 95 of 1975) in a manner particularly tormenting for the injured person;
5a. (new - SG, No. 16 of 2019) in conditions of domestic violence;
6. (new - SG, No. 95 of 1975) by a person who has intentionally committed another grievous or medium bodily injury under Art. 128 and 129 or under this article, for which no sentence has been imposed;
7. (new - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) repeatedly, if the bodily injury is grievous or medium;
8. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
8a. (new - SG, No. 84 of 2013) with the purpose of removing a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid from the injured person;
9. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) in a manner or by means dangerous to the life of many or with particular cruelty;
10. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) with a mercenary purpose;
11. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) with the purpose of facilitating or concealing another crime;
12. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) from hooligan, racist, xenophobic or related to sexual orientation motives.
(New expression - SG, No. 95 of 1975, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years for grievous bodily injury; from two to ten years for medium bodily injury; up to three years for light bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 1 and up to one year or probation under Art. 130, para. 2;
(2) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, suppl. - SG, No. 43 of 2005, in force from 01.09.2005, am. - SG, No. 64 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 80 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, No. 61 of 2013) For causing bodily injury to a judge, prosecutor, investigator, police officer, investigating police officer, state bailiff, private bailiff and assistant private bailiff, as well as to a customs officer, to a revenue officer, to an officer of the Executive Forest Agency or to an officer of the Ministry of Environment and Water exercising control activity or to a medical specialist, to a teacher (educator) during or on the occasion of the performance of his service or function the punishment is deprivation of liberty:
1. from five to fifteen years for grievous bodily injury;
2. from three to ten years for medium bodily injury;
3. from one to five years for light bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 1;
4. up to three years for light bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 2.
Art. 131a. (Previous para. 2 of Art. 131 - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) In cases of dangerous recidivism, the punishment is: for grievous bodily injury deprivation of liberty from eight to fifteen years, and for medium bodily injury deprivation of liberty from five to twelve years.
Art. 132. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) For bodily injury, except in the cases of Art. 131a, caused to another person in a state of strong irritation provoked by the injured person by violence, by grave insult, by slander or by another unlawful act from which grave consequences have occurred or could have occurred for the perpetrator or his relatives, the punishment is:
1. deprivation of liberty for up to three years for grievous bodily injury;
2. deprivation of liberty for up to one year for medium bodily injury;
3. (am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) deprivation of liberty of three months or probation for up to six months for light bodily injury under Art. 130, para. 1;
4. (am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) probation for up to six months or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva under Art. 130, para. 2.
(2) The punishments under the preceding paragraph shall also be imposed in cases of causing bodily injury when exceeding the limits of necessary defense.
Art. 133. (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever causes another person by negligence grievous or medium bodily injury shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by probation.
Art. 134. (1) Whoever causes another person grievous or medium bodily injury due to ignorance or due to negligent performance of an occupation or other legally regulated activity constituting a source of increased danger shall be punished:
1. by deprivation of liberty for up to three years for grievous bodily injury and
2. (am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) by deprivation of liberty for up to two years or by probation for medium bodily injury.
(2) (Am. and suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever by negligence causes another person grievous or medium bodily injury through actions falling within an occupation or activity under the preceding paragraph which he has no right to exercise shall be punished for grievous bodily injury by deprivation of liberty for up to five years, and for medium bodily injury - by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) If in the cases of the preceding paragraphs the perpetrator was in a state of intoxication or if injury has been caused to more than one person, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to six years for grievous bodily injury and deprivation of liberty for up to five years for medium bodily injury.
(4) If the perpetrator after the act has done everything in his power to provide assistance to the injured person, this shall be taken into account as a mitigating circumstance in determining the punishment.
Art. 135. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever, knowing that he suffers from a venereal disease, infects another person with the same disease shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years and by a fine of up to two hundred leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) If in the case of the preceding paragraph the infected persons are minors under 16 years of age or more than two persons are infected, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to five years or a fine of up to five hundred leva.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever, knowing that he suffers from a venereal disease, infects by negligence another person with the same disease shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by a fine of up to two hundred leva.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever through sexual intercourse or in another manner places another person in danger of being infected with a venereal disease shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six months or by a fine of up to two hundred leva.
(5) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) A person suffering from a venereal disease who refuses to be treated or evades regular mandatory treatment shall be punished by a fine of up to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
(6) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed repeatedly, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to six months.

### Section III

Endangerment
Art. 136. (1) (Previous text of Art. 136 - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever violates rules established for the protection of occupational safety and thereby endangers the life or health of workers shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or by probation, as well as by public reprimand.
(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) When by an act under the preceding paragraph the life or health of workers is endangered through negligence, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation.
Art. 137. Whoever exposes a person deprived of the ability to protect himself due to infancy, old age, illness or generally due to his helplessness, in such a way that his life may be in danger, and being aware of this, does not come to his aid, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
Art. 138. (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever knowingly does not render assistance to a person for whom he is obliged to care and who is in danger for his life and has no ability to protect himself due to infancy, old age, illness or generally due to his helplessness, in cases where he was able to render assistance, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by probation.
Art. 139. (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever in case of immediate danger to the life of another does not come to his aid, which he was able to give without danger to himself or to another, shall be punished by probation for up to six months or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 140. (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) A driver of a vehicle who, after a traffic accident in which he participated, does not render the necessary assistance to the injured person, which he was able to give without danger to himself or to another, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by probation.
Art. 141. (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) A person who practices a medical occupation, if after being summoned, does not come to the aid of a sick person or a woman in childbirth without valid reason, shall be punished by probation or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) If it was known to the guilty person that the sick person or the woman in childbirth was in a dangerous condition, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation.
(3) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever, being obliged to render assistance to a sick person, does not render such assistance without valid reasons, shall be punished by probation for up to six months or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

### Section IV

Abduction and unlawful deprivation of liberty (Title amended - SG, No. 50 of 1995)
Art. 142. (New - SG, No. 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever abducts another shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(2) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) The punishment is deprivation of liberty from seven to fifteen years, when:
1. the perpetrator was armed;
2. the act was committed by two or more persons;
3. (amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) the abducted person is a pregnant woman or has not reached 18 years of age;
4. the abducted person enjoys international protection;
5. the act was committed against two or more persons;
5а. (new - SG, No. 16 of 2019) the act was committed in conditions of domestic violence;
6. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) the act was committed by a person engaged in security activity, by an employee in an organization that performs security or insurance activity, by a person who acts on behalf of such an organization or pretends to act on such behalf, by a person from the staff of the Ministry of Internal Affairs or a person who pretends to be such;
7. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002) the abduction is for profit or with the purpose that the person be taken outside the borders of the country;
8. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) the act was committed by a person who acts on behalf of or in execution of a decision of an organization or group under Art. 321a or an organized criminal group;
9. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) the act was committed on racist, xenophobic or related to sexual orientation motives.
(3) (Amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) The punishment is deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment, as well as confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person, when:
1. the act was committed repeatedly or constitutes dangerous recidivism;
2. significant harmful consequences resulted from the act;
3. particular cruelty was shown towards the abducted person;
4. the act was committed in a particularly painful or dangerous to the health of the abducted manner;
5. the release of the abducted person was made dependent on the fulfillment of a certain condition by a third party.
(4) (Repealed - SG, No. 26 of 2010)
(5) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2010) For preparation, incitement or association for the purpose of committing a crime under this article, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to six years.
(6) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2010) In the cases under para. 1 - 5, the perpetrator shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55, if he voluntarily surrenders to the authorities, discloses everything known to him about the crimes committed, and thereby substantially facilitates the detection and proof of the crimes.
Art. 142а. (Previous Art. 142 - SG, No. 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully deprives someone of liberty shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years.
(2) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010) If the act was committed by an official or by a representative of the public in violation of his service or function, or by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.
(3) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 101 of 2017, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 and 2 was committed:
1. against a pregnant woman, infant or minor;
2. against a person enjoying international protection;
3. on racist, xenophobic or related to sexual orientation motives,
the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(4) (New - SG, No. 16 of 2019) If the act under para. 1 was committed in conditions of domestic violence, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(5) (Previous para. 3, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 16 of 2019) If the act under the preceding paragraphs was committed in a manner painful or dangerous to the health of the victim, or if the deprivation of liberty lasted more than two days, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to twelve years.
(6) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 50 of 1995, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 5 - SG, No. 16 of 2019) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever knowingly places or detains a healthy person in a health institution for the mentally ill.
(7) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 50 of 1995, previous para. 6 - SG, No. 16 of 2019)

### Section V

Coercion
Art. 143. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, previous text of Art. 143 - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever compels another to perform, omit or endure something, contrary to his will, by using force, threat or abusing his power, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years.
(2) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997) If the act is committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(3) (New - SG, issue 16 of 2019, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 is committed:
1. in conditions of domestic violence;
2. with racist or xenophobic motives,
the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(4) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, supplemented - SG, issue 43 of 2005, effective from 01.09.2005, amended - SG, issue 64 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 80 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, previous para. 3 - SG, issue 16 of 2019) When coercion is exercised against a judge, prosecutor, investigator, police officer, investigating police officer, state bailiff, private bailiff and assistant private bailiff, as well as against a customs officer, a revenue authority, an employee of the Executive Forest Agency or an employee of the Ministry of Environment and Water exercising control activity during or in connection with the performance of his service or function, the punishment shall be:
1. in the cases under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty from two to eight years;
2. in the cases under para. 2 - deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
Art. 143a. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985) (1) Whoever detains someone as a hostage, whose release he makes dependent on the fulfillment of a specific condition by the state, by a state or public organization or by a third person, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
(2) When in the cases under the previous paragraph the perpetrator threatens that if the condition set by him is not fulfilled, he will cause death or serious or medium bodily injury to the detained person, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to ten years.
(3) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997) If the act under the previous paragraphs is committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, the punishment shall be:
1. under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty from two to ten years;
2. under para. 2 - deprivation of liberty from five to twelve years.
Art. 144. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever threatens another with a crime against his person or property or against the person or property of his relatives and this threat could arouse reasonable fear for its realization, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) For a threat against an official or a representative of the public during or in connection with the performance of his service or function, or against a person enjoying international protection, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010, supplemented - SG, issue 16 of 2019, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to six years, when:
1. the perpetrator has threatened with murder;
2. the act is committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8;
3. the act is committed in conditions of domestic violence;
4. the act is committed with racist or xenophobic motives.
Art. 144a. (New - SG, issue 16 of 2019) (1) Whoever systematically follows another and this could arouse reasonable fear for his life or health, or for the life or health of his relatives, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation.
(2) Following under para. 1 is any conduct with a threatening character against a specific person, which may be expressed in pursuing the other person, showing the other person that he is being observed, entering into unwanted communication with him through all possible means of communication.
(3) (Supplemented - SG, issue 67 of 2023) If the act is committed in conditions of domestic violence or with racist or xenophobic motives, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
Art. 144b. (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) (1) Whoever through force, threat or in another way tortures another, in order for him or another person to give information or confession, in order to be punished for an act which he or another has committed or is suspected of having committed, shall be punished for torture by deprivation of liberty from one to six years, if he is not subject to a more severe punishment.
(2) When the act is committed by an official during or in connection with the performance of his service, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.

### Section VI

Disclosure of another's secret
Art. 145. (1) (Previous text of Art. 145, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever unlawfully discloses another's secret, dangerous to someone's good name, which has been entrusted to him or has become known to him in connection with his occupation, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever discloses the secret of adoption with the intention to cause harmful consequences to the adopted person, to the adopter or to their family, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six months or by probation, and when serious consequences have resulted from the act - by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.
Art. 145a. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (1) Whoever uses information collected through the use of special intelligence means, outside its intended purpose for the protection of national security or for the purposes of criminal proceedings, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years and a fine of up to five hundred leva.
(2) When the act is committed by an official who has acquired the information or it has become known to him within the scope of his service, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(3) In the cases under the previous paragraph the court may order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section VII

Insult and Defamation
Art. 146. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) Whoever says or does something degrading to the honor or dignity of another in his presence shall be punished for insult with a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva. In this case the court may also impose a penalty of public reprimand.
(2) If the insulted person has immediately responded with an insult, the court may release both from punishment.
Art. 147. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000) Whoever discloses a shameful circumstance about another or attributes a crime to him shall be punished for defamation with a fine from three thousand to seven thousand leva and with public reprimand.
(2) The perpetrator shall not be punished if the truthfulness of the disclosed circumstances or of the attributed crimes is proven.
Art. 148. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) For insult:
1. inflicted publicly;
2. (amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) disseminated through mass media or by other means;
3. (repealed - SG, No. 67 of 2023)
4. (amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) by an official or by a representative of the public in or on the occasion of the performance of his office or function;
5. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) inflicted on racist or xenophobic grounds,
(amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) the penalty is a fine from five hundred to ten thousand leva and public reprimand.
(2) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) For defamation committed under the conditions of the preceding paragraph, as well as for defamation from which serious consequences have resulted, the penalty is a fine from one thousand leva to fifteen thousand leva and public reprimand.
(3) In the cases of para. 1, item 1, para. 2 of Art. 146 may find application.
Art. 148a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 21 of 2000, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever discloses orally, through mass media or by other means data, circumstances or allegations about another, based on unlawfully acquired information from the archives of the Ministry of Internal Affairs, shall be punished with a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.

### Section VIII

Debauchery
Art. 149. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever commits an act with the purpose of arousing or satisfying sexual desire without intercourse in relation to a person who has not attained 14 years of age shall be punished for indecent assault with imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) For indecent assault the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years, when committed:
1. through the use of force or threat;
2. by rendering the victim in a helpless state;
3. by taking advantage of the helpless state of the victim;
4. by taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision;
5. in relation to a person who engages in prostitution.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) When the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
(4) (New - SG, No. 107 of 1996) For indecent assault the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years:
1. if committed by two or more persons;
2. (rep. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, new - SG, No. 74 of 2015) if committed in relation to a person who does not understand the nature or significance of what is being done.
3. (rep. - SG, No. 62 of 1997)
4. (rep. - SG, No. 62 of 1997)
(5) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) For indecent assault the punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years:
1. if indecent assault is committed against two or more minor persons;
2. if serious bodily injury is caused or an attempt at suicide has followed;
3. if it constitutes dangerous recidivism;
4. (new - SG, No. 38 of 2007) if it constitutes a particularly serious case.
Art. 150. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 107 of 1996, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, previous text of Art. 150, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever commits an act with the purpose of arousing or satisfying sexual desire without intercourse in relation to a person who has attained 14 years of age, through the use of force or threat, through taking advantage of his or her helpless state or by rendering him or her in such a state or through taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on the person who commits the act under para. 1 in relation to a minor person who engages in prostitution.
(3) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 2, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed in relation to a person who does not understand the nature or significance of what is being done, or the act constitutes a particularly serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
Art. 151. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever has intercourse with a person who has not attained 14 years of age, insofar as the act does not constitute a crime under Art. 152, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to six years.
(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed:
1. through taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision;
2. in relation to a person who has not attained 14 years of age who engages in prostitution;
3. by two or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 2 - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed with a minor person through taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years.
(4) (Previous para. 2 - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 3 - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever has intercourse with a person who has attained 14 years of age who does not understand the nature or significance of what is being done shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 152. (1) Whoever has intercourse with a person of the female sex:
1. deprived of the possibility of self-defense, and without his or her consent;
2. by compelling him or her to this through force or threat;
3. by rendering him or her in a helpless state, shall be punished for rape with imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) For rape the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years:
1. (am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if the raped person has not attained eighteen years of age;
2. if she is a descendant relative;
3. (new - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) if committed repeatedly.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) For rape the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years:
1. if committed by two or more persons;
2. if medium bodily injury is caused;
3. if an attempt at suicide has followed;
4. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if committed with the purpose of involving in subsequent depraved acts or prostitution;
5. (previous item 4 - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if it constitutes dangerous recidivism.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) For rape the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years:
1. if the raped person has not attained fourteen years of age;
2. if serious bodily injury is caused;
3. if suicide has followed;
4. if it constitutes a particularly serious case.
Art. 153. (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever has intercourse with another person by compelling him or her to this through taking advantage of his or her official or material dependence on him or her shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
Art. 154. Intercourse between ascendants and descendants, between brothers and sisters and between adopters and adoptees shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
Art. 154a. (1) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous text of Art. 154a, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever commits indecent acts or intercourse with a minor person who engages in prostitution shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 is committed repeatedly or by two or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years.
Art. 155. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever inclines another person to prostitution or procures for indecent acts or intercourse shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever systematically provides premises to different persons for sexual intercourse or for indecent acts shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years and with a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) When the acts under para. 1 and 2 are committed with a mercenary purpose, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
(4) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever inclines or compels another person to use narcotic substances or their analogues with the purpose of prostitution, intercourse, indecent acts or committing sexual intercourse or acts of sexual satisfaction with a person of the same sex shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years and with a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(5) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) When the act under para. 1 - 4 is committed:
1. by a person who acts on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
2. in relation to a person who has not attained 18 years of age, or insane;
3. in relation to two or more persons;
4. repeatedly;
5. under the conditions of dangerous recidivism,
the punishment in the cases under para. 1 and 2 is imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva, in the cases under para. 3 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty-five thousand leva, and in the cases under para. 4 - imprisonment from ten to twenty years and a fine from one hundred thousand to three hundred thousand leva.
(6) (Previous para. 3 - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, rep. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
(7) (Previous para. 4 - SG, No. 62 of 1997, previous para. 5 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, rep. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005)
Art. 155a. (New - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever through information or communication technology or by another means provides or collects information about a person who has not attained 18 years of age, in order to establish contact with him or her for committing an indecent act, intercourse, sexual intercourse, prostitution, for creating pornographic material or for participation in a pornographic performance, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years and with a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on the person who through information or communication technology or by another means establishes contact with a person who has not attained 14 years of age, with the purpose of committing an indecent act, intercourse, sexual intercourse, for creating pornographic material or for participation in a pornographic performance.
Art. 155b. (1) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous text of Art. 155b, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever inclines a person who has not attained 14 years of age to observe actual, virtual or simulated sexual intercourse between persons of the same or different sex or lascivious display of human sexual organs, sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.
(2) (New - SG, No. 53 of 2022, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever inclines a person who has not attained 14 years of age to participate in actual, virtual or simulated sexual intercourse between persons of the same or different sex or lascivious display of human sexual organs, sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to ten years.
(3) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, previous para. 2 - SG, No. 53 of 2022) When the act under para. 1 is committed:
1. through the use of force or threat;
2. through taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision;
3. by two or more persons who have conspired in advance;
4. (am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
Art. 155c. (1) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, previous text of Art. 155c, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever through the use of force or threat, or through taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision inclines a minor person to observe actual, virtual or simulated indecent act, intercourse, sexual intercourse, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, as well as lascivious display of human sexual organs, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to seven years.
(2) (New - SG, No. 53 of 2022, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever through the use of force or threat, or through taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision inclines a minor person to participate in actual, virtual or simulated indecent act, intercourse, sexual intercourse, including sodomy, masturbation, sexual sadism or masochism, as well as for lascivious display of human sexual organs, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.
Art. 156. (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever abducts another person with the purpose that he or she be provided for depraved acts shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years and with a fine up to ten thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) The punishment is imprisonment from five to twelve years, when:
1. the abducted person has not attained 18 years of age;
2. the abducted person is provided for depraved acts, or
3. the abduction is with the purpose that the person be provided for depraved acts outside the borders of the country.
(3) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) The punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva, when:
1. the act is committed by a person who acts on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
2. the abducted person is provided for depraved acts outside the borders of the country;
3. the act constitutes dangerous recidivism.
Art. 157. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) (1) Whoever commits sexual intercourse or acts of sexual satisfaction with a person of the same sex, by using for this force or threat or taking advantage of a position of dependence or supervision, as well as with a person deprived of the possibility of self-defense, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 1 is committed in relation to a minor person who engages in prostitution, the punishment is imprisonment from five to ten years.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 1 is committed in relation to a person who has not attained 14 years of age, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twelve years.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever commits sexual intercourse or acts of sexual satisfaction with a person of the same sex who has not attained 14 years of age shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.
(5) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 4 is committed in relation to a person who has not attained 14 years of age who engages in prostitution, the punishment is from three to ten years.
(6) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever commits sexual intercourse or acts of sexual satisfaction with a person of the same sex who has attained 14 years of age who does not understand the nature or significance of what is being done shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.
(7) When the act under para. 1 - 6 constitutes a particularly serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from five to twenty years.
Art. 158. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, rep. - SG, No. 74 of 2015)
Art. 158a. (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever by any means recruits, assists or uses a person who has not attained 18 years of age, or a group of such persons to participate in a pornographic performance, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever compels a person who has not attained 18 years of age, or a group of such persons to participate in a pornographic performance, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 1 or 2 is committed in relation to a person who has not attained 14 years of age, the punishment is imprisonment from three to eight years.
(4) When property benefit is obtained from the act, the punishment is:
1. (am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) in the cases under para. 1 or 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva;
2. in the cases under para. 3 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(5) (Am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever observes a pornographic performance in which a person who has not attained 18 years of age participates shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 158b. (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) For a crime under Art. 149 - 157 or Art. 158a the court may also impose deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6 or 7.
Art. 159. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) Whoever creates, displays, presents, broadcasts, offers, sells, gives for rent or in another way distributes pornographic material shall be punished with imprisonment up to one year and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 38 of 2007, suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever distributes through information or communication technology or by another similar means pornographic material shall be punished with imprisonment up to two years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(3) (Previous para. 2, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever displays, presents, offers, sells, gives for rent or in another way distributes pornographic material to a person who has not attained 16 years of age shall be punished with imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, previous para. 3, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) For the act under para. 1 - 3 the punishment is imprisonment from three to six years and a fine up to ten thousand leva, when:
1. for the creation of the pornographic material a person who has not attained 18 years of age, or a person who appears as such, is used;
2. for the creation of the pornographic material a person who does not understand the nature or significance of what is being done is used;
3. committed by two or more persons;
