4. committed repeatedly.
(5) (Previous para. 4, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 1 - 4 is committed on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine up to twenty thousand leva, and the court may also order confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator.
(6) (Previous para. 5, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever holds or procures for himself or herself or for another through information or communication technology or by another means pornographic material, for the creation of which a person who has not attained 18 years of age, or a person who appears as such, is used, shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years and with a fine up to ten thousand leva.
(7) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The punishment under para. 6 shall also be imposed on the person who through information or communication technology knowingly gains access to pornographic material, for the creation of which a person who has not attained 18 years of age, or a person who appears as such, is used.
(8) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) In the cases under para. 1 - 7 the court may also impose deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6 or 7.
(9) (Previous para. 6 - SG, No. 38 of 2007, previous para. 7 - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The object of the crime shall be forfeited in favor of the state, and if missing or alienated, its equivalent shall be awarded.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section IX

Trafficking in persons (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002)
Art. 159a. (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 84 of 2013) Whoever recruits, transports, harbours or receives individuals or groups of people with the purpose of being used for depraved acts, for forced labour or for begging, for removal of a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid or to be kept in forced subjection regardless of their consent, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from three thousand to twelve thousand leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) When the act under para. 1 has been committed:
1. against a person who has not attained eighteen years of age;
2. by using coercion or by misleading the person;
3. by abduction or unlawful deprivation of liberty;
4. by using a state of dependence;
5. by abuse of power;
6. by promising, giving or receiving benefits;
7. (new - SG, issue 84 of 2013) by an official in or on the occasion of the performance of his service,
the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) When the act under para. 1 has been committed against a pregnant woman with the purpose of selling the child, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to fifteen years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva.
Art. 159b. (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) Whoever recruits, transports, harbours or receives individuals or groups of people and transports them across the border of the country with the purpose under Art. 159a, para. 1, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) (Suppl. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) If the act under para. 1 has been committed under the conditions of Art. 159a, para. 2 and 3, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twelve years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva.
Art. 159v. (New - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 84 of 2013) Whoever uses a person who has suffered from trafficking in persons for depraved acts, for forced labour or for begging, for removal of a bodily organ, tissue, cell or bodily fluid or to be kept in forced subjection regardless of his consent, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
Art. 159g. (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002, former Art. 159v, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) When the act under Art. 159a - 159v constitutes dangerous recidivism or has been committed by order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva, and the court may also order confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator.
Supplementary provision
Art. 160. (1) (Former text of Art. 160 - SG, issue 54 of 1978, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) For the crimes under Art. 116, para. 1, point 2, 123, 126, Art. 131, para. 1, point 2, 134, Art. 142, para. 2 and 3, the court may order deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6 or 7.
(2) (New - SG, issue 54 of 1978, repealed - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982)
Special provision
Art. 161. (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, issue 21 of 2000, former text of Art. 161 - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, issue 16 of 2019) For light bodily injury under Art. 130 and Art. 131, para. 1, points 3 - 5, for light and medium bodily injury under Art. 132, for the crimes under Art. 144, para. 1, Art. 145, 146 - 148a, as well as for bodily injury under Art. 132, 133 and 134, inflicted on an ascendant, descendant, spouse, brother or sister, criminal prosecution shall be initiated upon complaint of the injured party.
(2) (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 16 of 2019) For bodily injury under Art. 129, inflicted on an ascendant, descendant, spouse, brother or sister, as well as for crimes under Art. 133, Art. 135, para. 1, 3 and 4, Art. 139 - 141 and Art. 144a, para. 1, criminal prosecution of general character shall be initiated upon complaint of the injured party to the prosecutor's office and may not be terminated at his request.
Chapter Three.
CRIMES AGAINST THE RIGHTS OF CITIZENS

### Section I

Crimes against the equality of citizens (Title amend. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011)
Art. 162. (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever by word, print or other means of mass information, through electronic information systems or in another way preaches or incites discrimination, violence or hatred based on race, colour, origin, nationality or ethnic affiliation or sexual orientation, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to four years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, as well as by public reprimand.
(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. and suppl. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever uses violence against another or damages his property because of his race, colour, origin, nationality, ethnic affiliation, religion, political convictions or sexual orientation, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to four years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, as well as by public reprimand.
(3) (Amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) Whoever forms or leads an organization or group which sets as its purpose the commission of acts under para. 1 and 2 or systematically permits the commission of such acts, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva, as well as by public reprimand.
(4) Whoever is a member of such an organization or group shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years and by public reprimand.
(5) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, repealed - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005)
Art. 163. (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Persons who participate in a crowd gathered for an attack on groups of the population, individual citizens or their property in connection with their race, colour, origin, nationality, ethnic affiliation or sexual orientation, shall be punished:
1. the instigators and ringleaders - by imprisonment up to five years;
2. (amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) all others - by imprisonment up to one year or by probation.
(2) If the crowd or some of the participants are armed, the punishment shall be:
1. for the instigators and ringleaders - imprisonment from one to six years;
2. for all others - imprisonment up to three years.
(3) If an attack has been committed and from it has resulted grievous bodily injury or death, the instigators and ringleaders shall be punished by imprisonment from three to fifteen years, and all others - by imprisonment up to five years, when they are not subject to a more severe punishment.

### Section II

Crimes against Religions
Art. 164. (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) (1) (Supplemented - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever preaches or incites discrimination, violence or hatred on religious grounds through speech, print or other mass media, through electronic information systems or in any other way, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to four years or probation, as well as a fine of five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) (Supplemented - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever desecrates, destroys or damages a religious temple, house of prayer, shrine or building adjacent thereto, their symbols, graves or tombstones, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation, as well as a fine of three thousand to ten thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment shall be imprisonment of up to five years or probation, as well as a fine of five thousand to ten thousand leva, when the act under para. 2 is committed with racist or xenophobic motives.
Art. 165. (1) Whoever by force or threat prevents citizens from freely professing their faith or performing their religious rites and services, which do not violate the laws of the country, public order and good morals, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever in the same manner compels another to participate in religious rites and services.
(3) For the acts under Art. 163, committed against groups of the population, individual citizens or their property in connection with their religious affiliation, the punishments provided therein shall apply.
Art. 166. (Supplemented - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) Whoever forms a political organization on a religious basis or whoever through speech, print, action or in any other way uses the church or religion for propaganda against the state authority or its measures, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years, unless subject to a more severe punishment.

### Section III

Crimes against the political rights of citizens (Title amended - SG, No. 1 of 1991)
Art. 167. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 1 of 1991, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, previous text of Art. 167 - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever by violence, fraud, threat or other unlawful means prevents someone from exercising their right to vote or from being elected or from voting in a referendum or from participating in a petition for holding a referendum, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever offers or gives to another person a property benefit with the purpose of inducing them to exercise their right to vote in favor of a specific candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a specific way shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, No. 85 of 2007, effective 23.10.2007, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amended - SG, No. 17 of 2013, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever organizes the offering or giving to another person of a property benefit with the purpose of inducing them to exercise their right to vote in favor of a specific candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a specific way shall be punished by imprisonment from one to seven years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty-five thousand leva.
(4) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014) The penalty under para. 3 shall also be imposed on a person who provides the property benefit to the persons under para. 2 and 3, to be offered or given to another person with the purpose of inducing them to exercise their right to vote in favor of a specific candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a specific way.
(5) (New - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) When the act under para. 1 - 4 is committed by an official in or on the occasion of performing their official duties, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two to seven years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(6) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous para. 5, amended - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014) In the cases under para. 2, 3, 4 and 5 the court shall also impose a penalty of deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(7) (New - SG, No. 19 of 2008, previous para. 4, supplemented - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous para. 6 - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014) The perpetrator under para. 2 shall not be punished if they voluntarily report to a competent authority a crime committed under para. 3 and 4.
Art. 167a. (1) (New - SG, No. 85 of 2007, effective 23.10.2007, previous text of Art. 167a - SG, No. 19 of 2008, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014, supplemented - SG, No. 88 of 2020) Whoever with the purpose of exercising their right to vote in favor of a specific candidate, political party or coalition or to vote in a referendum in a specific way requests or receives a property benefit, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 19 of 2008, amended - SG, No. 27 of 2009) The perpetrator shall not be punished if they voluntarily report to a competent authority a crime committed under Art. 167, para. 2, 3 or 4.
Art. 168. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, previous text of Art. 168 - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014) Whoever exercises the right to vote or votes in a referendum without having such right, shall be punished by probation up to six months or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014) Whoever exercises their right to vote two or more times in one election, or votes two or more times in one referendum shall be punished by probation and a fine from five hundred to two thousand leva.
Art. 168a. (New - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) Whoever in violation of the established rules prints voting ballots, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(2) The penalty under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever unlawfully possesses or distributes voting ballots.
Art. 169. (Amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, supplemented - SG, No. 19 of 2014, effective 05.03.2014, amended - SG, No. 74 of 2015) An official, as well as a person from the composition of an electoral commission, who violates the secrecy of voting or in any way falsifies a result from elections, or a referendum shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years.
Art. 169a. (New - SG, No. 1 of 1991, amended No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever by violence, threat or other unlawful means compels someone contrary to their convictions or will to participate in or leave a political party, organization, movement or coalition with a political purpose, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 169b. (New - SG, No. 1 of 1991, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever by violence, threat or other unlawful means prevents someone from exercising their constitutional political rights, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 169c. (New - SG, No. 1 of 1991) When the act under Art. 169a and 169b is committed by an official in or on the occasion of performing their official duties, the penalty shall be imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 169d. (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009) (1) Whoever forms or leads a group which sets as its purpose the commission of crimes under this section, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years.
(2) Whoever is a member of such a group, shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years.
(3) A participant in the group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities, discloses everything known to them about the group, and thereby substantially facilitates the detection and proof of crimes committed by it, shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55.
(4) A participant in the group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities and discloses the group before another crime under this section has been committed by it or by them shall not be punished.

### Section IV

Violation of the inviolability of a dwelling, premises or vehicle (Title amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982)
Art. 170. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever enters another's dwelling by using force, threat, deception, dexterity, abuse of power or special technical means for this purpose shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or by probation of up to six months.
(2) (Amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed at night or by an armed person, or by two or more persons, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years.
(3) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) If the acts under the preceding paragraphs are directed against the dwelling, vehicle or official premises of a person enjoying international protection, the punishment shall be: under para. 1 - imprisonment from one to five years, and under para. 2 - imprisonment from two to eight years.
(4) (Former para. 3, amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever unlawfully remains in another's dwelling despite an express request to leave it shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year.

### Section V

Violation of the inviolability of correspondence
Art. 171. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) Whoever unlawfully:
1. opens, falsifies, conceals or destroys another's letter, telegram, sealed papers, package or other similar items;
2. (amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) takes another's letter or telegram, even if open, with the purpose of learning their content or with the same purpose delivers another's letter or telegram to someone else;
3. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) learns of a message sent electronically not addressed to him or her, or diverts such a message from its addressee,
shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva
(2) (Amended - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) If the act is committed by an official who has taken advantage of his or her official position, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(3) (Supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, No. 101 of 2017) Whoever, through the use of special technical means, unlawfully gains access to or learns of a message not addressed to him or her, transmitted by telephone, telegraph, through a computer network or by another telecommunications means, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years.
(4) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed when the subject of the act is computer data sent within one or between multiple information systems, including electromagnetic emissions from an information system.
(5) (New - SG, No. 38 of 2007, former para. 4, supplemented - SG, No. 101 of 2017) When the act under para. 3 and 4 is committed with a mercenary purpose or significant damage is caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.
Art. 171a. (New - SG, No. 26 of 2010) (1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, No. 24 of 2015, in force from 31.03.2015) Whoever unlawfully acquires, stores, discloses or disseminates data such as are collected, processed, stored or used in accordance with the Electronic Communications Act shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation.
(2) When the act under para. 1 is committed with a mercenary purpose, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years.

### Section VI

Crimes against the labour rights of citizens
Art. 172. (Amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 1 of 1991) (1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amended and supplemented - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amended - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever knowingly prevents someone from taking up employment or compels him or her to leave employment on account of his or her nationality or ethnic affiliation, race, skin colour, religion, social origin, sexual orientation, membership or non-membership in a trade union or other organisation, political party, organisation, movement or coalition with a political purpose or on account of his or her or his or her relatives' political or other convictions shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or by a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(2) An official who fails to execute an order or a decision that has entered into force for the reinstatement of an improperly dismissed worker or employee shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years.

### Section VII

Crimes against intellectual property (Title amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995)
Art. 172a. (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, issue 53 of 2022) Whoever records, reproduces, distributes, broadcasts or transmits, or otherwise uses another's object of copyright or related right, or copies thereof, without the consent required by law of the holder of the respective right, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) The penalty under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever builds or maintains an information system or provides an information society service for the purpose of committing crimes under para. 1 and obtaining material benefit.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, issue 53 of 2022, former para. 2, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever, without the consent required by law, holds material carriers containing another's object of copyright or related right, where the monetary equivalent of one or more of the objects exceeds 70 minimum wages and the objects exceed fifty in number, or holds a matrix for reproducing such carriers, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to six years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva.
(4) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, issue 53 of 2022, former para. 3, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 - 3 has been committed repeatedly or has caused significant harmful consequences, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from three thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
(5) (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, former para. 4, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 3 is on a particularly large scale, the penalty shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(6) (Former para. 4 - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, former para. 5 - SG, issue 67 of 2023) For minor cases the perpetrator shall be punished by administrative procedure under the Copyright and Related Rights Act.
(7) (Former para. 5, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, former para. 6, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) When the object of the crime belongs to the perpetrator, it shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 172b. (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) (1) (Amended and supplemented - SG, issue 53 of 2022) Whoever, without the consent of the holder of the exclusive right, uses in commercial activity a trademark, invention, utility model, industrial design, plant variety or animal breed, object of that exclusive right, or uses a geographical indication or its imitation without legal basis, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 has been committed repeatedly or has caused significant harmful consequences, the penalty shall be imprisonment from five to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) When the object of the crime belongs to the perpetrator, it shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 173. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 53 of 2022) Whoever publishes or uses under his own name or under a pseudonym another's work of science, literature or art or a significant part of such a work, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, as well as public censure.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 81 of 1999, effective from 14.12.1999) The same penalty shall be imposed on whoever submits for registration or registers in his own name another's invention, utility model or industrial design.
(3) (New - SG, issue 53 of 2022) When the act under para. 1 has been committed on the internet or has caused significant harmful consequences, the penalty shall be imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.
Art. 174. (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 81 of 1999, effective from 14.12.1999, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever, by abusing his official position, includes himself as a co-author of an invention, utility model or industrial design or of a work of science, literature or art, without having participated in the creative work of its creation, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, as well as public censure.

### Section VIII

Crimes against the freedom of assemblies, rallies and demonstrations
Art. 174a. (New - SG, issue 10 of 1990) (1) Whoever by violence, fraud, threat or other unlawful means disperses or prevents the holding of an assembly, rally or demonstration permissible under the Assemblies, Rallies and Demonstrations Act, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years.
(2) An organizer who, in violation of Art. 12, para. 3, and 13, para. 1 of the Assemblies, Rallies and Demonstrations Act, holds prohibited or continues to hold terminated assembly, rally or demonstration, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year.

### Section IX

Crimes against the right of workers and employees to associate in trade union organizations and unions (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023)

### Section IX

Art. 174b. (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) (1) Whoever by violence, threat or other unlawful means prevents someone from exercising his right to trade union association, by forcing him contrary to his convictions or will to be a member or to renounce membership in a trade union organization, or prevents him from creating such, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(2) Whoever by violence, threat or other unlawful means prevents someone from exercising rights arising from his exercised right to trade union association, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or a fine of up to three thousand leva.
(3) If the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed repeatedly, the penalty shall be imprisonment of up to five years or a fine of up to ten thousand leva.
Special provision
Art. 175. (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 53 of 2022, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 67 of 2023) For crimes under Art. 170, para. 1 and 4, Art. 171, para. 1, Art. 172, para. 2, Art. 173, para. 1 and 2 and Art. 174b criminal prosecution shall be initiated upon complaint of the victim.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 19 of 2008) For crimes under Art. 172, para. 1 and Art. 174 criminal prosecution of general nature shall be initiated upon complaint of the victim to the prosecutor's office and may not be terminated at his request.
Chapter Four.
CRIMES AGAINST MARRIAGE, FAMILY AND YOUTH


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Раздел I

Crimes against marriage and the family
Art. 176. (1) Whoever, upon entering into marriage, knowingly conceals from the civil status official a legal impediment to the marriage shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) A civil status official who performs a marriage ceremony knowing that there is a legal impediment to the conclusion of the marriage shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(3) (Repealed - SG, issue 51 of 2000)
Art. 177. (Amended - SG, issue 16 of 2019) (1) Whoever compels another to enter into marriage by using force, threat or abuse of power shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who abducts another with the purpose of compelling them to enter into marriage.
(3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who, with the purpose of compelling another to enter into marriage, induces them by deception to cross into the territory of another state.
(4) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who in any manner induces a person who was unable to understand the nature and significance of what was done to enter into marriage or to cross into the territory of another state with the purpose of compelling them to enter into marriage.
(5) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to eight years when the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed:
1. by a parent, other relative or guardian;
2. against a minor;
3. against two or more persons;
4. for mercenary purposes.
Art. 178. (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) A parent or other relative who receives a bride price in order to permit his daughter or female relative to enter into marriage shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, as well as by public reprimand.
(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever gives or whoever mediates in the giving or receiving of such a bride price.
Art. 179. (1) Whoever, in the presence of a lawful marriage, concludes another shall be punished for bigamy by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever concludes a marriage with a person whom they know to be in a lawful marriage.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) If the first marriage is declared invalid or is terminated on another ground, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation.
Art. 180. (Repealed - SG, issue 1 of 1991)
Art. 181. (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005) Whoever violates their obligation toward a spouse, ascendant or descendant unable to care for themselves, and thereby places them in a position of serious difficulty, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by probation, as well as by public reprimand.
Art. 182. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) A parent or guardian who leaves a person under parental care or guardianship without supervision and sufficient care and thereby creates a danger to their physical, mental or moral development shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years, as well as by public reprimand.
(2) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amended - SG, issue 16 of 2019) A parent or other relative who fails to comply with or in any manner obstructs the execution of a court decision concerning the exercise of parental rights or concerning personal contacts with a child shall be punished by probation and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva, and in particularly serious cases - by deprivation of liberty for up to six months and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) The perpetrator shall not be punished if, after a warning from the competent authority, they comply with the decision or remove the obstacles to its execution. This provision shall not apply repeatedly.
Art. 182a. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever, for the purpose of material gain, induces a parent by gift, promise, threat or abuse of official position to abandon their child or to give consent for its adoption shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years and a fine of up to two thousand leva.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever induces a minor to give consent for their adoption when the law requires it.
(3) Whoever, for the purpose of unlawful material gain, mediates between a person or family wishing to adopt a child and a parent wishing to abandon their child, or a woman agreeing to carry a child in her womb for the purpose of handing it over for adoption, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years and a fine of up to three thousand leva.
(4) If the act under para. 3 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years and a fine of up to four thousand leva.
Art. 182b. (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) (1) A female person who gives consent for the sale of her child in the country or abroad shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a pregnant woman who gives consent for the sale of her child before its birth.
Art. 183. (Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, supplemented - SG, issue 47 of 2009, effective 01.10.2009) Whoever, having been sentenced to maintain their spouse, ascendant, descendant, brother or sister, knowingly fails to fulfil their obligation in the amount of two or more monthly instalments shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by probation.
(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever deliberately places themselves in a position of inability to provide maintenance, whether by transferring their property, by failing to exercise their rights or in another manner.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) The perpetrator shall not be punished if, before the pronouncement of the sentence by the first instance, they fulfil their obligation and no other harmful consequences for the injured party have occurred. This provision shall not apply repeatedly.
(4) (Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective 01.01.2004) If the act under para. 1 and 2 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation, as well as public reprimand.
Art. 184. (1) Whoever intentionally exchanges, conceals or substitutes a small child or in another manner conceals or changes the civil status of someone shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) If this is done for mercenary purposes, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 185. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully takes or detains another's child under fourteen years of age and does not immediately notify the authorities or return it to the parents or guardian shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If this has been done by violence, threat or fraud or with the intention to use the child for mercenary or immoral purposes, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.
Art. 186. (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever takes in an abandoned or lost child under seven years of age and does not immediately report this to the authorities, to the parents or guardian shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by probation.

### Раздел II

Crimes against youth
Чл. 187. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) Whoever tortures a minor or juvenile person who is under their care or whose upbringing has been entrusted to them, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or by probation, as well as by public reprimand.
Чл. 188. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 74 от 2015 г.) (1) Whoever induces a person who has not reached 18 years of age to commit a crime through coercion or through the use of a position of dependence or supervision shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever induces a person who has not reached 18 years of age to prostitution through coercion or through the use of a position of dependence or supervision.
(3) When the act under para. 1 or 2 has resulted in harmful consequences for the physical, mental or moral development of the victim, when the act does not constitute a more serious crime, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and public reprimand.
(4) When the act under para. 2 has been committed against a person who has not reached 14 years of age, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to ten years.
(5) In the cases under para. 1 - 4, the court may also impose deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6 or 7.
Чл. 189. (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) Whoever systematically uses a person who is under their care for begging shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) If the perpetrator is a parent or guardian of the victim, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to two years or probation, as well as public reprimand.
Чл. 190. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 16 от 2019 г.) (1) Whoever forces another to live as a spouse with someone by using force, threat or abuse of power shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who abducts another with the purpose of forcing them to live as a spouse with someone.
(3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who, with the purpose of forcing another to live as a spouse with them or with another person, induces them through deception to cross into the territory of another state.
(4) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who in any way induces a person who was not able to understand the nature and significance of what was done to live as a spouse with another.
(5) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to eight years when the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed:
1. by a parent, other relative or guardian;
2. against a juvenile person;
3. against two or more persons;
4. with a mercenary purpose.
Чл. 191. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) An adult person who, without having entered into marriage, lives as a spouse with a female person who has not reached 16 years of age shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or by probation, as well as by public reprimand.
(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) An adult person who induces or facilitates a juvenile male person and a female person who has not reached 16 years of age to live as spouses without having entered into marriage shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or by probation.
(3) (Предишна ал. 2 - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г.) If the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed against a person who has not reached 14 years of age, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to five years.
(4) (Предишна ал. 3, изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 16 от 2019 г.)
Чл. 192. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) A parent or other relative who receives a ransom to allow their daughter or female relative who has not reached 16 years of age to live as a spouse with another shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or by a fine of up to three thousand leva.
(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever gives or intermediates in the giving of such a ransom.
Чл. 192а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) Whoever without proper authorization employs a person who has not reached 18 years of age shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six months and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) If the act under para. 1 has been committed against a person who has not reached 16 years of age, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year and a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva.
(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) If the act under para. 1 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva, and under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from three thousand to eight thousand leva.
Чл. 193. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 89 от 1986 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г.) Whoever intoxicates with alcoholic beverages a person who has not reached 18 years of age or an insane person shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six months or by a fine of up to five hundred leva.
(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 103 от 2004 г., в сила от 01.01.2005 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 27 от 2009 г.) Whoever sells an alcoholic beverage to a person who has not reached 18 years of age or to an insane person for personal consumption shall be punished by a fine of up to one thousand leva and probation, and if they do this systematically - by imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to three thousand leva.
Special provision
Чл. 193а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., отм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г., нов - ДВ, бр. 16 от 2019 г.) For a crime under Art. 182, para. 2, criminal prosecution shall be initiated upon complaint by the victim.
Глава пета.
CRIMES AGAINST PROPERTY (Загл. изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г.)


This excerpt sits under: | Конституция | Правилници |

### Section I

Theft
Art. 194. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) Whoever takes another's movable property from the possession of another without his consent with the intention to unlawfully appropriate it shall be punished for theft by deprivation of liberty for up to eight years.
(2) Theft exists also when part of the property belongs to the perpetrator.
(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) In minor cases the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 195. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) For theft the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to ten years:
1. if committed during a fire, flood, shipwreck, disaster, war or other public calamity;
2. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) if the stolen property is not under constant supervision;
3. if committed by destroying, damaging or undermining barriers solidly made for the protection of persons or property;
4. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) if a motor vehicle, technical means or special method was used to commit the theft;
5. if committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission, when it does not constitute a minor case;
6. if the theft was committed by an official who took advantage of his official position;
7. in non-minor cases, if committed repeatedly;
8. (new - SG, No. 10 of 1993) if the theft was committed from the grave of a deceased person;
9. (new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) if the theft was committed by a person under Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8;
10. (new - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) if the subject of the theft is explosives, pyrotechnic articles, weapons or ammunition for firearms;
11. (new - SG, No. 101 of 2017) if the theft is for the purpose of obtaining means for committing a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1, 2, 6 or 7;
12. (new - SG, No. 7 of 2019) if the subject of the theft is a motor vehicle.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) For theft in large amounts the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years, and the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the perpetrator's property.
(3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 102 of 2006, amend. - SG, No. 102 of 2008, amend. - SG, No. 93 of 2009, in force from 25.12.2009, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, No. 79 of 2015, in force from 01.11.2015) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed for theft of explosives, firearms or ammunition for firearms from the structural units of the Ministry of Internal Affairs, from the Ministry of Defense, from the Bulgarian Army and from the structures subordinate to the Minister of Defense, from the State Agency "State Reserve and Wartime Stocks", from the State Agency "National Security", from the General Directorate "Protection" and General Directorate "Execution of Punishments" at the Ministry of Justice, from the State Agency "Intelligence" and the National Protection Service.
(4) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, former para. 3 - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) In minor cases under points 2 and 6 of para. 1 the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(5) (Former para. 4, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. - SG, No. 7 of 2019) For preparation for theft under para. 1, points 3, 4 and 12 the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to three years or probation.
Art. 196. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) For theft which constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is:
1. in the cases of Art. 194, para. 1 - deprivation of liberty from two to ten years;
2. (suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) in the cases of Art. 195, para. 1 and 2 - deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years, and in the cases under Art. 195, para. 3 - deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) The court may order confiscation of up to one half of the perpetrator's property.
Art. 196a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) For theft in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years and confiscation of all or part of the perpetrator's property.
Art. 197. If by the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the stolen property is returned or replaced, the punishment is:
1. in the cases of Art. 194, para. 1 - deprivation of liberty for up to five years;
2. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) in the cases of Art. 194, para. 3 and 195, para. 4 - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva;
3. (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) in the cases of Art. 195, para. 1, points 2-6 - deprivation of liberty for up to eight years;
4. (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) in the cases of Art. 195, para. 2 in connection with Art. 194 or with Art. 195, para. 1, points 2 - 6 - deprivation of liberty for up to eight years;
5. (new - SG, No. 89 of 1986) in the cases of Art. 196a - deprivation of liberty from eight to twenty years.
Art. 197a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993)


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Rules of Procedure |

### Section II

Robbery
Art. 198. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) Whoever takes another's movable property from the possession of another with the intention of unlawfully appropriating it by using force or threat shall be punished for robbery by imprisonment from three to ten years.
(2) Threat means threatening with such an immediate act that exposes to serious danger the life, health, honor or property of the threatened person or of any other person present.
(3) Robbery also includes any theft in which the person caught at the scene of the crime uses force or threat to retain possession of the stolen property.
(4) Robbery also exists when, in order to take the property, the person has been rendered unconscious or placed in a defenseless state.
Art. 199. (1) (Amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993) For robbery of property:
1. in large amounts;
2. committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance to commit thefts or robberies;
3. accompanied by serious or medium bodily injury;
4. (amended - SG, No. 92 of 2002) constituting dangerous recidivism;
5. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group,
(amended - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and the court may also order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.
(2) (Amended - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amended - SG, No. 10 of 1993, supplemented - SG, No. 50 of 1995) For robbery of movable property:
1. accompanied by serious or medium bodily injury from which death has resulted;
2. accompanied by murder or attempted murder;
3. (Amended - SG, No. 153 of 1998) in especially large amounts, if the perpetrator was armed, the punishment is imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation. The court may order confiscation of all or part of the property of the guilty person.
Art. 200. For preparation for robbery under Art. 198 the punishment is imprisonment up to two years, and under Art. 199 - imprisonment up to three years.

### Section III

Misappropriations
Art. 201. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, suppl. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, suppl. from SG, No. 50 of 1995 was declared unconstitutional by Decision of the Constitutional Court No. 19 of 1995 - SG, No. 97 of 1995, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, previous text of Art. 201 - SG, No. 83 of 2019) An official who misappropriates another's money, things or other valuables handed over in that capacity or entrusted to him to keep or manage, shall be punished for official misappropriation with imprisonment of up to eight years, and the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(2) (New - SG, No. 83 of 2019) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed when the act has been committed by a foreign official.
Art. 202. (1) For official misappropriation the punishment is imprisonment from one to ten years:
1. if another crime has also been committed to facilitate it, for which the law does not provide for a more severe punishment;
2. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) if it has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance.
(2) For official misappropriation the punishment is imprisonment from three to fifteen years:
1. (amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if it is in large amounts,
2. (amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if it constitutes dangerous recidivism or
3. (new - SG, No. 92 of 2002) if the misappropriated funds are from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state.
(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) In the cases of the preceding paragraphs the court shall deprive the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. The court may also order confiscation under para. 1 of up to one half, and under para. 2 - of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
Art. 203. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) For official misappropriation in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004) The court shall order confiscation of all or part of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 204. In minor cases of official misappropriation the punishment is:
a) (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) under Art. 201 - imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
б) (amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) under Art. 202, para. 1 - imprisonment of up to two years or probation.
Art. 205. (1) If the misappropriated money, things or valuables are deposited or replaced before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court, the punishment is:
1. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) in the cases of Art. 201 - imprisonment of up to five years;
2. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) in the cases of Art. 202, para. 1 - imprisonment from one to seven years;
3. in the cases of Art. 202, para. 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years;
4. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) in the cases of Art. 203 - imprisonment from eight to twenty years;
5. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) in the cases of Art. 204, letter "a" - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva;
6. (amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) in the cases of Art. 204, letter "б" - imprisonment of up to six months or probation.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In the cases of points 2, 3 and 4 of the preceding paragraph the court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and in the cases under point 3 may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person, and in the cases under point 4 shall order confiscation of part or all of the property.
Art. 206. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully misappropriates another's movable thing which he possesses or keeps, shall be punished for embezzlement with imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Embezzlement also exists when part of the thing belongs to the perpetrator, as well as when the thing belongs to the perpetrator but is encumbered with a pledge and the perpetrator disposes of it unlawfully without preserving the rights of the pledgee creditor, or when the perpetrator pledges another's thing, thereby hindering the satisfaction of the creditor.
(3) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) If the embezzlement is in large amounts or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, and the court shall deprive the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7 and may order confiscation of part or all of his property.
(4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) For embezzlement in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and the court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7 and confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
(5) (Previous para. 3, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) In minor cases the punishment is imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(6) (Previous para. 4, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) If the misappropriated property is deposited or replaced before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court, the punishment is:
1. under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to three years;
2. under para. 3 - imprisonment from two to eight years;
3. under para. 4 - imprisonment from three to twelve years;
4. (amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) under para. 5 - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(7) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In the cases of point 2 of the preceding paragraph the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and in the cases of point 3 shall order confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 207. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, corr. - SG, No. 31 of 1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever finds another's movable thing and within one week does not report it to the owner, to the authorities or to the person who lost it, shall be punished with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on whoever unlawfully misappropriates another's thing which has come into his possession accidentally or by mistake.
Art. 208. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) Whoever discovers a treasure and within seven days does not report it to the authorities, shall be punished with probation or with a fine from five hundred to one thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) Whoever, while searching, discovers a treasure and within two weeks does not report it to the authorities, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or with a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(3) (Previous para. 2, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) If the treasure is in especially large amounts, the punishment is: under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to two years or a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, and under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to five years or a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(4) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) When the treasure contains a cultural value or the act under para. 1 - 3 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment is: under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to two years and a fine of up to five thousand leva, under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, and under para. 3 - imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(5) (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) When the act under para. 2 - 4 has been committed on commission or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(6) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, previous para. 4 - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) The object of the crime shall be forfeited to the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.

### Section IV

Fraud
Art. 209. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a property benefit, induces or maintains in someone an error and thereby causes him or another property damage, shall be punished for fraud with deprivation of liberty from one to six years.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, with the same purpose, exploits the error, inexperience or lack of information of someone and thereby causes him or another property damage, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to five years.
(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) In minor cases under the preceding paragraphs, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to one year or probation.
Art. 210. (1) For fraud the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to eight years:
1. if the perpetrator has represented himself as an official or as a person acting on behalf of the authorities;
2. (amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) if it has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission;
3. (suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) if the fraud has been committed by an official or by an agent within the scope of his office or authority or by a person who directly carries out transactions with cash currency by occupation;
4. if it has been committed repeatedly in non-minor cases;
5. (new - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) if the damage caused is in large amounts.
(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) In the cases of points 4 and 5, the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.
Art. 211. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) When the fraud under Art. 209, para. 1 and 2 and under 210 is in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to ten years. The court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.
Art. 212. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, through the use of a document with false content or of a false or forged document, obtains without legal basis another's movable or immovable property with the intention of appropriating it, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.
(2) (Amend. and suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on the one who, through the preparation of a document with false content or of a false or forged document, knowingly gave the opportunity to another natural or legal person to obtain without legal basis such property.
(3) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) When the property is from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by them to the Bulgarian state, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(4) (Previous para. 3 - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the property under the preceding paragraphs is in large amounts or the act constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years.
(5) (Previous para. 4 - SG, No. 92 of 2002) For documentary fraud in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years.
(6) (Previous para. 5 - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) In minor cases under paragraphs 1 and 2, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to two years or probation.
(7) (Previous para. 6 - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) In the cases of para. 1, the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and in the cases of para. 3, 4 and 5 it orders confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprives him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 212a. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a benefit, induces or maintains an error in someone by entering, modifying, deleting or erasing computer data or using another's electronic signature and thereby causes him or another damage, shall be punished for computer fraud with deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine up to six thousand leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) The same punishment shall be imposed on the one who, without having the right, enters, modifies, deletes or erases computer data in order to obtain something that is not due to him.
Art. 212b. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, previous Art. 212a - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) If the property obtained under Art. 212 is returned or replaced before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court, the punishment is:
1. under para. 1 and 2 - deprivation of liberty up to five years;
2. under para. 3 - deprivation of liberty from two to eight years;
3. (suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) under para. 4 and 5 - deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years;
4. (amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) under para. 6 - probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In the cases under point 2 of the preceding paragraph, the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7, and under point 3 it orders confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprives him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 213. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever destroys, damages or annihilates with fraudulent purpose his own insured property, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty up to three years and with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.

### Раздел V

Extortion
Чл. 213а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) (1) Whoever, with the purpose of compelling another to dispose of a thing or of his right or to assume a pecuniary obligation, threatens him with violence, disclosure of disgraceful circumstances, damage to property or other unlawful act with serious consequences for him or his relatives, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(2) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years and a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva, when the act is:
1. accompanied by a threat of murder or grievous bodily harm;
2. accompanied by causing light bodily injury;
3. accompanied by seizure, destruction or damage to property;
4. committed by two or more persons;
5. committed by a person under чл. 142, ал. 2, точки 6 и 8;
6. committed by an armed person;
7. committed repeatedly in non-trivial cases.
(3) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, and the court may order confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property, when:
1. medium or grievous bodily injury has been caused, if a more severe punishment is not provided for the crime committed;
2. significant pecuniary damage has been caused;
3. the act has been committed by an organization or group or on the order of a person, organization or group;
4. the act is accompanied by an explosion or arson;
5. the act is committed by or with the participation of an official;
6. the act is committed against an official in connection with his service;
7. the act constitutes dangerous recidivism.
(4) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г.) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from fifteen to twenty years or life imprisonment, or life imprisonment without commutation, and the court may order confiscation of part or all of the perpetrator's property, when the act is:
1. accompanied by grievous or medium bodily injury from which death has resulted;
2. accompanied by murder or attempted murder.
Чл. 214. (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 50 от 1995 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a pecuniary benefit, compels someone by force or threat to do, omit or suffer something contrary to his will and thereby causes pecuniary damage to him or to another, shall be punished for extortion by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva, and the court may impose confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property.
(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 50 от 1995 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) For extortion under the conditions of чл. 213а, ал. 2, 3 и 4 the punishment shall be:
1. по ал. 2 - deprivation of liberty from two to ten years and a fine from four thousand to six thousand leva, and the court may order confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property;
2. по ал. 3 - deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years, a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva and confiscation of up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property;
3. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 153 от 1998 г.) по ал. 4 - deprivation of liberty from fifteen to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation and confiscation of not less than 1/2 of the perpetrator's property.
(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 50 от 1995 г.) For extortion the punishment shall be from five to fifteen years deprivation of liberty and a fine of up to five hundred leva, and the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the guilty person's property, if:
1. it is accompanied by grievous or medium bodily injury;
2. the act constitutes dangerous recidivism.
Чл. 214а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г.) For preparation for a crime under чл. 213а и 214 the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to three years.

### Раздел VI

Receiving stolen goods
Чл. 215. (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., доп. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) Whoever, with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a pecuniary benefit, conceals, acquires or assists in the alienation of another's movable things, which he knows or assumes to have been acquired by another through a crime or through another socially dangerous act, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years, but with a punishment not more severe than that provided for the crime itself.
(2) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 95 от 1975 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2010 г.) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, if the receiving of stolen goods:
1. is on a large scale;
2. is with things placed under a special regime;
3. is carried out as a trade;
4. has been committed repeatedly or constitutes dangerous recidivism.

### Раздел VII

Destruction and damage
Чл. 216. (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г.) Whoever unlawfully destroys or damages another's movable or immovable thing shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to five years.
(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) Whoever destroys, demolishes or damages his own mortgaged or pledged property shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to five years and a fine of up to two thousand leva.
(3) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 101 от 2017 г.) Whoever, by effecting unauthorized access to an information system of significance for an enterprise, institution, legal or natural person, and thereby destroys or damages another's property, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva.
(4) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 28 от 1982 г., в сила от 01.07.1982 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., предишна ал. 2, изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) In minor cases the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of up to six months or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(5) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 62 от 1997 г., предишна ал. 3 - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г., изм. и доп. - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г., изм. - ДВ, бр. 67 от 2023 г.) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of up to ten years, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under чл. 37, ал. 1, т. 6 и 7, when:
1. the act is connected with destruction or damage to elements of the telecommunications network;
2. the act has caused significant damage or other serious consequences have occurred;
3. the act has been committed by a person under чл. 142, ал. 2, т. 6 и 8;
4. the act has been committed on racist, xenophobic or sexual orientation-related motives.
(6) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 10 от 1993 г., предишна ал. 4, изм. - ДВ, бр. 92 от 2002 г.) If the act under ал. 1, 2, 3 и 5 has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of up to two years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Чл. 216а. (Нов - ДВ, бр. 26 от 2004 г.) (1) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 33 от 2011 г., в сила от 27.05.2011 г.) Whoever, by himself or through another, unlawfully violates the integrity of facilities or objects of an electricity transmission or electricity distribution network, of a gas transmission or heat transmission system, of a system for transmission of liquid fuels, or of a water supply or sewerage system, thereby creating conditions for diversion of electrical energy, natural gas, liquid fuels, thermal energy or water, or discharge of waste water, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of up to five years and a fine of up to twenty thousand leva.
