(2) (Нова - ДВ, бр. 84 от 2023 г.) The same punishment shall also be imposed on one who, by himself or through another, causes disruptions or interruption of the supply of water, electricity or other basic natural resources, from which danger to human life may arise.
(3) (Изм. - ДВ, бр. 33 от 2011 г., в сила от 27.05.2011 г., предишна ал. 2, доп. - ДВ, бр. 84 от 2023 г.) If the act under ал. 1 или 2 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to ten years and a fine of up to thirty thousand leva.


This excerpt sits under: | Конституция | Правилници |

### Section VIII

Breach of trust
Art. 217. (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever knowingly damages another's property entrusted to him to manage or keep shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years or by a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on a representative or agent who knowingly acts against the lawful interests of the represented person.
(3) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on anyone who disposes of a seized or pledged thing left with him for safekeeping.
(4) (Previous para. 3 - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the above acts result in significant or irrecoverable damages, the punishment shall be imprisonment for up to five years and a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva.
Special provision
Art. 218. (Rep. - SG, No. 10 of 1993)
Supplementary provisions
(Title am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982)
Art. 218a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1979, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, rep. - No. 10 of 1993)
Art. 218b. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) For acts under Art. 194, para. 3, Art. 195, para. 4, Art. 204, letter "a", Art. 206, para. 1 and 5 and Art. 207 and for receiving stolen property in connection with them, when the value of the object is up to the amount of two minimum wages for the country established as of the date of commission of the act, the punishment shall be a fine of one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure, if the object of the crime has been restored or replaced.
(2) The provision of the preceding paragraph shall not apply when:
1. (am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 21 of 2000) the perpetrator has committed two or more acts within one year, the total value of the object of which exceeds one hundred and fifty leva;
2. the perpetrator has been convicted of such a crime, as well as if an administrative penalty has been imposed on him for such an act and one year has not elapsed since its commission;
3. (am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) the object of the act is weapons, ammunition for firearms, explosives, poisonous or narcotic substances, pyrotechnic products, combat and other special equipment.
Art. 218c. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Criminal prosecution shall be initiated upon complaint of the injured party in the following cases:
1. for crimes under Art. 216, para. 4 and 6 and Art. 217, para. 1 and 2, when the object of the crime is private property;
2. for theft, misappropriation, fraud and extortion, when the object of the crime is private property, if the injured party is the spouse, ascendant, descendant or relative in the collateral line up to the second degree of the perpetrator or lives with him in one household, or is the guardian or trustee of the perpetrator.
Chapter Six.
CRIMES AGAINST THE ECONOMY

### Section I

General Economic Crimes
Art. 219. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) An official who does not exercise sufficient care for the management, administration, operation or preservation of the property entrusted to him or for the work assigned to him and from this results significant damage, destruction or waste of property or other significant harm to the enterprise or to the economy, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever, despite his obligations, has not exercised sufficient control over the work of persons to whom the management, disposal or accounting of public property has been entrusted, and from this significant harm to the enterprise or to the economy has resulted, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) If the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed intentionally and does not contain the elements of a more serious crime, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years, and the court may order deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) For a crime under the preceding paragraphs in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment shall be under para. 1 and 2 - deprivation of liberty from three to ten years, and under para. 3 - deprivation of liberty from three to twelve years, and in these cases the court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
Art. 220. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) An official who knowingly concludes an unfavorable transaction and from this significant harm results for the economy or for the institution, enterprise or organization which he represents, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years, and the court may order deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(2) (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) In especially serious cases under the preceding paragraph, the punishment shall be from three to ten years deprivation of liberty, and the court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
(3) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, repeal. - SG, No. 101 of 2001)
Art. 221. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1988, repeal. - SG, No. 1 of 1991)
Art. 221a. (New - SG, No. 27 of 1973, corr. No. 31 of 1982, amend. - SG, No. 86 of 1991, corr., No. 90 of 1991) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever orders or in violation of his obligations permits the taking and use for construction and for other non-agricultural needs of unexpropriated or not transferred according to the established procedure agricultural land or pastures, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever continues, orders or in violation of his obligations permits the continuation of construction or other non-agricultural use on land under the preceding paragraph, after the construction and other use have been stopped by the competent authorities according to the established procedure, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 221b. (New - SG, No. 44 of 1984, repeal. - SG, No. 1 of 1991)
Art. 222. (Repeal. - SG, No. 1 of 1991)
Art. 223. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever gives false data regarding the quantity, quality or type of what has been produced or of the work performed with the aim of obtaining a property benefit which is not due, if this does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or by probation.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever for what has been produced by him or for the work performed receives remuneration which is not due to him, knowing that it has been determined on the basis of such false data, if this does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by probation or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(3) What has been acquired by the perpetrator as a result of the crimes under the preceding paragraphs shall be returned to the respective organization.
Art. 224. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever receives a gift or other property benefit, in order to give or for having given to a foreign state, foreign organization or company, or to a foreign citizen information from which significant harm to the economy has resulted or may result, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed also on the person who has given the gift or the property benefit.
(3) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 225. (Amend. - SG, No. 26 of 1973, amend. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 81 of 1990, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever sells goods at a price above the determined one or before it has been determined or approved according to the established procedure or receives for a service remuneration greater than legally permissible, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly and is a not insignificant case or if the amount received in excess is significant, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from six months to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 1973, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, repeal. - SG, No. 81 of 1990, new - SG, No. 81 of 1990) Whoever, after having been punished for a violation under Art. 3 of the Law on Combating Speculation, commits the same violation before one year has elapsed from the entry into force of the penal decree, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(4) (Repeal., former para. 5 - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever knowingly compiles false information or presents false data for determining the price of goods or services and from this harm has resulted or could have resulted for a state institution or enterprise, for a public organization or for citizens, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by probation.
(5) (Repeal., former para. 7 - SG, No. 81 of 1990, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In cases where under the preceding paragraphs the court determines a punishment of deprivation of liberty, the court may also order deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6 or 7.
(6) (Repeal., former para. 5 - SG, No. 89 of 1986, repeal. - SG, No. 81 of 1990, new - SG, No. 28 of 2020, in force from 24.03.2020) When the act under para. 1 has been committed during a declared state of emergency under Art. 84, item 12 of the Constitution of the Republic of Bulgaria, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to three years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
Art. 225a. (Repeal. - SG, No. 1 of 1991)
Art. 225b. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever for work performed or a service rendered receives a property benefit which is not due to him, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly or the benefit is in large amounts, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) In minor cases under para. 1, the punishment shall be a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
(4) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 225c. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) Whoever, while performing work for a legal entity or sole trader, requests or accepts a gift or any benefit which is not due to him, or accepts an offer or promise for a gift or benefit, in order to perform or not to perform an act in violation of his obligations in carrying out commercial activity, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years or a fine of up to twenty thousand leva.
(2) Whoever in carrying out commercial activity offers, promises or gives a gift or any benefit to a person who performs work in a legal entity or for a sole trader, in order to perform or not to perform an act in violation of his obligations, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or a fine of up to fifteen thousand leva.
(3) The punishments under the preceding paragraphs shall be imposed also when with the consent of the person under para. 1 the gift or benefit has been offered, promised or given to another.
(4) (Amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever acts as an intermediary for the commission of any of the acts under the preceding paragraphs, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(5) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 226. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever, by using a state, cooperative or other public organization, carries out private economic activity in violation of the established regulations and in this way obtains significant unlawful income, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, former para. 3 - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever carries out private economic activity by disguising it in the form of a state, cooperative or other public organization, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(3) (Former para. 2, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) An official of the respective state, cooperative or other public organization who permits the commission of the crime under the preceding paragraphs, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or by probation.
(4) (New - SG, No. 26 of 1973, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Upon repeated commission of the crime under the preceding paragraphs or when the unlawful income is in large amounts, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
(5) (Former para. 4 amend. - SG, No. 26 of 1973, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) If the unlawful income under the preceding paragraphs is in especially large amounts and the case is especially serious, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to twelve years.
Art. 227. (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, No. 81 of 1999, in force from 14.12.1999, repeal. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, new - SG, No. 19 of 2012) (1) Whoever employs simultaneously five or more illegally staying on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria foreigners, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to four years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall be imposed also on a person who employs an illegally staying on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria foreigner, whom the perpetrator knows to be a victim of trafficking in human beings.
(3) Whoever employs an illegally staying on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria foreigner who has not reached 18 years of age, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine from three thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(4) Whoever systematically employs one or more illegally staying on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria foreigners, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(5) The punishment under para. 4 shall be imposed also on a person who employs an illegally staying on the territory of the Republic of Bulgaria foreigner under working conditions which substantially differ from the working conditions of legally employed persons and violate human dignity.
(6) In minor cases under para. 2 - 4, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation, as well as a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva.
Art. 227a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, repeal. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section I "a"

Crimes against creditors (New - SG, issue 107 of 1996)
Art. 227b. (New - SG, issue 107 of 1996) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 85 of 1998, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) A merchant who becomes insolvent and within 30 days from the cessation of payments does not declare this before the court shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or by a fine of up to five thousand leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) The persons who manage and represent the commercial company or cooperative shall be punished by the penalty under para. 1 if within 30 days from the cessation of payments they have not requested the court to open insolvency proceedings.
(3) The procurator who has not fulfilled his obligation under Art. 626, para. 3 of the Commerce Act shall be punished by the penalty under para. 1.
(4) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, issue 59 of 2006, in force from 01.01.2007, suppl. - SG, issue 102 of 2015, in force from 01.01.2016) The persons who, having been obliged to notify the Bulgarian National Bank of the insolvency of a bank, according to the Credit Institutions Act, as well as the persons who, having been obliged to notify the Financial Supervision Commission of the insolvency of an insurer or reinsurer, according to the Insurance Code, have not done so, shall be punished by the penalty under para. 1.
Art. 227c. (New - SG, issue 107 of 1996) (1) A merchant who, after the opening of insolvency proceedings:
1. conceals, destroys, damages or alienates gratuitously money, things, securities or other valuables which may serve to satisfy his creditors;
2. alienates money, things, securities or other valuables which may serve to satisfy his creditors, when what is given significantly exceeds what is received and is done in contradiction to the normal conduct of business activity;
3. forgives or conceals a claim of his;
4. acknowledges or assumes in any manner or satisfies a non-existent obligation;
5. takes a loan, knowing that he cannot repay it;
6. provides on credit goods, money, things, securities or other valuables belonging to him in a manner contrary to the normal conduct of business activity;
7. unlawfully satisfies only one or several creditors or secures them to the detriment of the other creditors;
8. destroys, conceals or falsifies his commercial books or documents or keeps them in violation of the law in a manner which makes it difficult to establish the assets and liabilities of his enterprise or activity, if significant damages have resulted from the enumerated acts, shall be punished for intentional bankruptcy by imprisonment of up to three years.
(2) When an act under para. 1 has caused damages in especially large amounts, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to fifteen years. The court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 227d. (New - SG, issue 107 of 1996) The persons who manage and represent the commercial company or the cooperative shall be punished by the penalties under Art. 227c if they commit or allow the commission of the acts specified in the same article, and in the cases under para. 1 the court may also order a fine of up to 500 leva, and under para. 2 - confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
Art. 227e. (New - SG, issue 107 of 1996) (1) A merchant who:
1. has not conducted his commercial affairs with the care of a good merchant or has participated in obviously risky transactions which do not fall within the scope of his ordinary activity;
2. has made personal, family or other expenses obviously inappropriate and unrelated to the activity and inconsistent with his property status;
3. has failed to prepare or has prepared an incorrect annual accounting report and balance sheet, when he was obliged to do so,
due to which he has been declared insolvent and damages to the creditors have resulted from this, shall be punished for negligent bankruptcy by imprisonment of up to two years, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(2) A merchant who has been declared insolvent without having fulfilled his obligations under a preceding rehabilitation plan shall be punished by the penalties under para. 1.
(3) The persons who manage and represent the commercial company or the cooperative shall be punished by the penalties under para. 1 if they commit or allow the commission of the acts specified in the same paragraph.
(4) The persons under para. 1-3 shall not be punished if before the pronouncement of the judgment by the first instance they satisfy their creditors. This provision shall not apply repeatedly.
Art. 227f. (New - SG, issue 107 of 1996) (1) A merchant who has obligations to another merchant for whom insolvency proceedings have been opened, knowing this and not fulfilling his obligation within the agreed or customary term, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or by a fine of up to 200 leva.
(2) A merchant who, with the approval, knowledge or in the interest of his creditor, conceals wholly or partially an obligation to him and thereby causes damage to his creditor, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years and a fine of up to 300 leva.
(3) Whoever, knowing that insolvency proceedings have been opened for a merchant, conceals or destroys with his consent his things which belong or would belong to the insolvency estate, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years and a fine of up to 300 leva.

### Section II

Crimes in Individual Economic Sectors
Art. 228. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) (1) Whoever, as a manager or control body, orders or permits the production of poor-quality, non-standard or incomplete industrial products or products that do not meet the established requirements for quality, type or marks, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or by probation.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever, in violation of their official duties, marks as standard or does not mark goods that do not meet the relevant requirements, when this is mandatory, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by probation.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) When the products or goods under the preceding paragraphs are not in significant quantities or of significant value, the punishment under para. 1 shall be a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and under para. 2 - a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
Art. 229. (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever, while receiving agricultural products on behalf of a procurement or commercial organization, deceives the supplier regarding the quality or quantity of those products, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or by probation, as well as by public reprimand.
Art. 230. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever violates an ordinance issued against the spread or occurrence of an infectious disease in domestic animals shall be punished by probation for up to six months or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) If infection has resulted from this, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation.
(3) If the infectious disease is widely spread, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(4) In accordance with the differences in the preceding paragraphs, whoever violates an ordinance issued for combating diseases and pests in plants shall also be punished.
Art. 231. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever puts on sale industrial or agricultural goods in significant quantities or of significant value, not meeting the requirements specified in para. 1 of Art. 228, without having expressly declared these defects, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years, by a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva and by deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) In minor cases under the preceding paragraph, the punishment shall be a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
Art. 232. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) A seller who deceives a buyer in weighing or measuring goods or who uses false measures and weights shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years or by probation.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on:
a. whoever deceives a buyer by mixing foreign substances or deteriorating the quality of the goods in another way;
b. whoever deceives a client regarding the quality of goods, materials or services.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) When damage up to one hundred leva has been caused by one or more acts under the preceding paragraphs, the punishment shall be a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed after an administrative penalty has been imposed on the person under the same paragraph by a decree that has entered into force, and one year has not elapsed since the commission of the first violation, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(5) (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In cases where under the preceding paragraphs the court imposes a punishment of deprivation of liberty, the court may also order deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6 or 7.
Art. 233. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 81 of 1990, rep. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, new - SG, No. 102 of 1995) (1) (Am. and suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) Whoever, without the relevant license, registration or permit, exports, imports, transfers, transports, transits, carries out brokerage activities with weapons or with products or technologies with dual use, as well as when such activities are carried out in violation of prohibitions, restrictions or sanctions imposed by the Security Council of the United Nations, by the Organization for Security and Co-operation in Europe or by the European Union, indicated in an act of the Council of Ministers or arising from an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years and by a fine of up to two hundred thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) For particularly serious cases under para. 1, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from 3 to 8 years and a fine of up to five hundred thousand leva.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) In minor cases under para. 1, the punishment shall be a fine of up to twenty thousand leva.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007) The weapons or products or technologies with dual use, subject of the crime, shall be confiscated in favor of the state regardless of whose property they are, and if they are missing or have been alienated, their equivalent value, determined according to the foreign trade contract, shall be awarded.
Art. 234. (Rep. - SG, No. 1 of 1991, new - SG, No. 107 of 1996) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, am. - SG, No. 7 of 2019) Whoever distributes or holds excise goods without a banderole, when such is required by law, in non-minor cases, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine of up to 10 times the market price of the distributed goods, as well as by deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(2) (New - SG, No. 7 of 2019) Whoever produces or holds for the purpose of distribution alcohol, alcoholic beverages or tobacco products without a proper permit, in non-minor cases, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva, as well as by deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, previous para. 2 - SG, No. 7 of 2019) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7, when the act:
1. has been committed repeatedly;
2. has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance;
3. if the subject of the crime is in large dimensions.
(4) (New - SG, No. 7 of 2019) Whoever, in violation of the established procedure, prepares, holds or conceals objects, materials or instruments, as well as technical equipment, which they know or assume are intended for or have served for the construction of facilities for the production of alcohol, alcoholic beverages or tobacco products under para. 2, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(5) (Previous para. 3 - SG, No. 7 of 2019) The subject of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 234a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever carries out foreign trade activity without a permit, which is required by law or by decree of the Council of Ministers or in violation of such a permit issued, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years, a fine from five to ten thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
Art. 234b. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010, am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever purchases or carries out commercial activity with waste from ferrous or non-ferrous metals without a license, which is required by a normative act, or in violation thereof, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine from two thousand to fifty thousand leva, as well as by deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(2) The waste, subject of the act under para. 1, shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 234c. (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever, by themselves or through another, carries out unlawful connection to an electricity transmission or electricity distribution network, or to a gas transmission, heat transmission, water supply or sewerage system, liquid fuel transmission system, or unlawful interference with commercial measuring devices for electricity, natural gas, liquid fuel, thermal energy or water, or discharged wastewater, thereby creating conditions for incomplete accounting of consumed electricity, natural gas, liquid fuel, thermal energy or water, or of discharged wastewater, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine of up to fifteen thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) If the act under para. 1 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years and a fine of up to twenty thousand leva.
Art. 234d. (New - SG, No. 108 of 2020) (1) Whoever, without a permit, registration or license, which are required by a normative act or act of the European Union, carries out by occupation public transport of passengers, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to three years.
(2) When the act under para. 1 has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission, or through the use of a false or forged document or a document with false content, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to five years and a fine from two to ten thousand leva.
(3) (Declared unconstitutional in the part "regardless of whose property it is" by CCD No. 12 of 2021 - SG, No. 84 of 2021) In the cases under para. 1 and 2, the motor vehicle that served for the commission of the act shall be confiscated in favor of the state, regardless of whose property it is, and when it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 235. (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever, without a proper written permit or with a proper permit, but outside the places, terms, quantity and trees indicated therein, cuts, collects, extracts, takes or transports from the forest fund any trees or part thereof, including cut or fallen ones, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years and by a fine from one thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever conceals, loads, transports, unloads, stores or processes timber illegally extracted by another.
(3) For a crime under para. 1 and 2, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva, if:
1. it has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission;
2. it has been committed in complicity with a forest officer who has taken advantage of their official position;
3. it has been committed through the use of a false or forged document or a document with false content;
4. it has been committed repeatedly;
5. the subject of the crime is in large dimensions.
(4) When the crimes under para. 1 - 3 have been committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group or constitute dangerous recidivism, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to one hundred thousand leva.
(5) When the subject of the crime is in particularly large dimensions and the case is particularly serious, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years and a fine from fifty thousand to five hundred thousand leva.
(6) (New - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In minor cases, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(7) (Previous para. 6 - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) The subject of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 236. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 86 of 1991, am. - SG, No. 85 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever destroys or damages in any way forest trees, young growth, undergrowth, forest culture or forest nursery, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years or by probation, as well as by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and in particularly serious cases - by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
Art. 237. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 86 of 1991) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 85 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever kills or catches large game without proper authorization shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, as well as by deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 86 of 1991, am. - SG, No. 85 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever, without possessing a hunting license, kills or catches small game, specially indicated in the Hunting Act, as well as whoever, even though possessing a hunting license, kills or catches such game at a prohibited time, in a prohibited place or with prohibited means, shall be punished by probation for up to six months or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, as well as by deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(3) The killed or caught game shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 238. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 86 of 1991, am. - SG, No. 55 of 2018) (1) Whoever catches, caught fish and/or other aquatic organisms through the use of explosive, poisonous or stunning substances, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year and a fine from ten thousand to fifteen thousand leva or by probation.
(2) Whoever catches, caught fish and/or other aquatic organisms:
1. with technical devices and equipment for catching with electric current without a valid permit;
2. with fishing nets in objects other than the Black Sea, the Danube River and the objects for which the person has registration under Art. 25 of the Fisheries and Aquaculture Act and has two or more penalty decrees entered into force for administrative violations under the Fisheries and Aquaculture Act;
3. in prohibited places or during low water;
4. in non-commercial waters during the spawning period of fish;
5. of species prohibited for catching,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six months and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva or by probation.
(3) For a crime under para. 1 and 2, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to three years and a fine from fifteen thousand to twenty thousand leva, when:
1. it has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission;
2. it has been committed in complicity with an employee of the Executive Agency for Fisheries and Aquaculture who has taken advantage of their official position;
3. it has been committed repeatedly;
4. the subject of the crime is in large dimensions.
(4) The caught fish, other caught aquatic organisms, as well as the devices and means with which the crime was committed, shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 239. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 86 of 1991) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 85 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) A manager of an enterprise, firm, institution, organization or other official who violates or permits a violation of the rules established by special law for the preservation of fish and other aquatic organisms and for the proper development of fish farming in the country, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years or by probation, as well as by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) The same punishment shall be imposed on any person who releases or discharges into fishery waters polluted waters or substances which, by their quantity or properties, may harm fish and other aquatic organisms.
Art. 240. (1) (Am. and suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) A foreign citizen who enters with a vessel into the territorial waters of the Republic of Bulgaria in the Black Sea and carries out commercial fishing without permission from the relevant border authorities, if not subject to more severe punishment under another law, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years, regardless of liability under the Fisheries and Aquaculture Act.
(2) If this has been committed by a group of foreign citizens who are armed, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) The caught fish, other caught aquatic organisms, as well as the devices and means with which the crime was committed, shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 240a. (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever carries out extraction of underground resources without a granted concession or when the operation of the granted concession for extraction has been suspended under the procedure of Art. 68 of the Underground Resources Act or on the basis of the concluded contract, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to six years and by a fine from one thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever holds, stores, transports, processes or alienates illegally extracted underground resources.
(3) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years and a fine from five thousand to fifty thousand leva, when:
1. the subject of the crime is in large dimensions;
2. the act has been committed in a protected territory or protected zone within the meaning of the Biological Diversity Act;
3. a technical device or road vehicle has been used for the commission of the act;
4. the act has been committed through the use of a document with false content, a false or forged document;
5. the act has been committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance;
6. the act has been committed with the participation of an official who has taken advantage of their official position;
7. the act has been committed by a person who systematically engages in such activity;
8. the act has been committed repeatedly.
(4) The punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years and a fine from ten thousand to one hundred thousand leva, when:
1. the subject of the crime is in particularly large dimensions and the case is particularly serious;
2. the act has been committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
3. the act has been committed under the conditions of dangerous recidivism.
(5) In minor cases under para. 1 and 2, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(6) The subject of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
(7) (Declared unconstitutional in the part "and when it is not the property of the perpetrator" by CCD No. 1 of 2023 - SG, No. 10 of 2023) The vehicle or portable device that served for the transportation or carrying of the subject of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state even when it is not the property of the perpetrator, unless its value manifestly does not correspond to the gravity of the crime.
(8) Whoever prepares, acquires, keeps or conceals objects, materials or instruments which they know are intended for or have served for the extraction of underground resources without a concession, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

### Section III

Crimes against the customs regime (Title amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995)
Art. 241. (Repealed - SG, issue 50 of 1995)
Art. 242. (Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004) Whoever transports across the border of the country goods without the knowledge and permission of the customs authorities, when this is committed:
a) by persons who systematically engage in such activity;
b) (amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004) through the use of a document with false content, of another person's, inauthentic or falsified document;
c) by an official who is in direct connection with the customs service;
d) (amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, issue 26 of 2004, supplemented - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) when potent or toxic substances, explosives, weapons or ammunition for firearms, pyrotechnic products, nuclear material, nuclear facilities or other sources of ionizing radiation or components or precursors for them, determined by law or by an act of the Council of Ministers, are transported
e) (amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975) goods and items for commercial or production purposes in large amounts;
f) (new - SG, issue 62 of 1997) by two or more persons who have conspired in advance;
g) (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002) by a person who acts on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
h) (new - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) through the transport of a specimen of a protected species of wild flora or fauna or a part thereof, or a product.
(amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) shall be punished for qualified smuggling with imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva;
(2) (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000) Whoever transports across the border of the country narcotic substances and/or their analogues without proper authorization shall be punished for high-risk narcotic substances with imprisonment from ten to fifteen years and a fine from one hundred thousand to two hundred thousand leva and for risky narcotic substances - with imprisonment from three to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to one hundred thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000) Whoever transports across the border of the country precursors or equipment and materials for the production of narcotic substances without proper authorization shall be punished with imprisonment from two to ten years and a fine from fifty thousand to one hundred thousand leva.
(4) (New para. 4 - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, para. 3 repealed, former para. 4 - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, former para. 3, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000) When the subject of smuggling under the preceding paragraphs is in especially large amounts and the case is especially serious or when any of the persons under letter "f" of para. 1 is a customs officer, the punishment is: in the cases under para. 1 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from fifty thousand to two hundred thousand leva, and in the cases under para. 2 and 3 - imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years and a fine from two hundred thousand to three hundred thousand leva.
(5) (Former para. 2, amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, former para. 4, amended - issue 89 of 1986, former para. 5, amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, former para. 4, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) In the cases under para. 1, letters "a", "d" and "e", as well as under para. 2, 3 and 4, the court may instead of a fine impose confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
(6) (Former para. 3, amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, former para. 5, amended - issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, former para. 6, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, former para. 5, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000) In minor cases under para. 1, 2 and 3 the punishment is a fine up to one thousand leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
(7) (Former para. 4, amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, former para. 6, amended - issue 89 of 1986, former para. 7, issue 50 of 1995, former para. 6 - SG, issue 21 of 2000) The subject of smuggling shall be forfeited in favor of the state, regardless of whose property it is, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value at the respective state retail prices shall be awarded.
(8) (Former para. 5, amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, former para. 7, amended - issue 89 of 1986, former para. 8, issue 50 of 1995, former para. 7 - SG, issue 21 of 2000, declared unconstitutional in the part "and when it is not the property of the perpetrator" by CCD No. 12 of 2021 - SG, issue 84 of 2021) The vehicle or carrying means that served for the transportation or carrying of the goods, subject of smuggling, shall be forfeited in favor of the state even when it is not the property of the perpetrator unless its value manifestly does not correspond to the gravity of the crime.
(9) (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985, former para. 8, amended - issue 89 of 1986, former para. 9, amended, issue 50 of 1995, former para. 8, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000) For preparation under para. 2, 3 and 4 the punishment is imprisonment up to five years. In these cases para. 7 applies.
Art. 242a. (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, supplemented - SG, issue 26 of 2004) Whoever transports across the state border goods with documents for transit transport and in violation of the established procedure unloads the goods on the territory of the country shall be punished with imprisonment up to six years and a fine from fifty thousand to five hundred thousand leva. The goods and the vehicle that served for the transport shall be forfeited in favor of the state regardless of whose property they are.

### Section IV

Crimes against the monetary and credit system
Art. 243. (1) Whoever produces counterfeit or alters genuine monetary signs with legal tender in the country or abroad shall be punished for counterfeiting of monetary signs with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever counterfeits:
1. tax or postage stamps and
2. bonds issued by the state or other state securities.
3. (new - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) payment instruments.
Art. 244. (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amend. and suppl. - SG, issue 24 of 2005, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017, suppl. - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever puts into circulation counterfeit monetary or other signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2, accepts, acquires, transfers or uses such, knowing that they are counterfeit, or carries them across the border of the country or transports them, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amend. and suppl. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who holds such signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2 in large quantities.
Art. 244a. (New - SG, issue 24 of 2005) (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) Whoever in violation of the established procedure produces, accepts, acquires, carries across the border of the country or transports monetary signs with legal tender in the country or abroad or monetary signs which have not yet been issued but are intended for putting into circulation as legal tender, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(2) Whoever knowingly puts such monetary signs into circulation shall be punished with imprisonment up to eight years.
Art. 245. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever accepts a counterfeit monetary or other sign under Art. 243 and after learning that it is such, knowingly puts it into circulation as genuine, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to three years and with a fine up to one thousand leva.
Art. 246. (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) Preparation for committing a crime under Art. 243 or association for such purpose or for distribution of counterfeit monetary or other signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2 shall be punished with imprisonment up to six years.
(2) (Suppl. - SG, issue 24 of 2005) The accomplice in the association who, before the counterfeiting is completed (if the association was formed for such purpose) or before the distribution of the counterfeit signs begins (if the association was formed for this) or before completing the production of the means under para. 3, withdraws from the act and reports to the authorities, shall not be punished.
(3) (Amend. and suppl. - SG, issue 24 of 2005, amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017, suppl. - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever produces, accepts, acquires, keeps or conceals, carries across the border of the country, transfers, transports, distributes or provides objects, materials or tools, computer programs and data, as well as any other means or elements for protection of monetary signs, which he knows are intended or have been used for producing counterfeit monetary signs or for counterfeiting monetary or other signs or payment instruments under Art. 243, para. 2, shall be punished with imprisonment up to six years.
Art. 247. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, corr. issue 31 of 1982, amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, repeal. - SG, issue 26 of 2010)
Art. 248. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever knows that a crime under Art. 243 and 244 is being committed and does not report it to the authorities shall be punished with probation or with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) The preceding paragraph shall not apply to the spouse, descendants, ascendants, brothers and sisters of the perpetrator and their spouses.
Art. 248a. (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever, in order to obtain credit, presents false information shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years and with a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) (Amend. and suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever presents false information or conceals information in violation of an obligation to provide such, in order to obtain funds from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state, as well as funds belonging to the Bulgarian state with which projects financed with funds from these funds are co-financed.
(3) (Amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amend. and suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) If the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed by a person who manages or represents a legal entity or civil partnership, or by a merchant, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva.
(4) With the punishment under para. 3 shall also be punished the official who authorized the credit or released the funds under para. 2, if he knew that the presented information was false.
(5) (New - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) If as a result of the act under para. 2 funds have been obtained from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state, as well as funds belonging to the Bulgarian state with which projects financed with funds from these funds are co-financed, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years.
Art. 249. (Amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 27 of 2009) Whoever uses a payment instrument or data from a payment instrument without the consent of the holder if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years and with a fine up to twice the amount obtained.
(2) Whoever uses a payment instrument issued in the Republic of Bulgaria or abroad without having coverage for the amount for which the instrument is used shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and with a fine up to twice the amount obtained.
(3) Whoever produces, installs or uses a technical device to acquire information about the content of a payment instrument shall be punished with imprisonment from one to eight years and with a fine up to twice the amount obtained.
(4) The same punishment shall be imposed on a person who stores or provides to another the information under para. 3.
Art. 250. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 89 of 1986, repeal., issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Previous text of Art. 250 - SG, issue 21 of 2000) Whoever transfers amounts abroad by bank transfer using a counterfeit, altered document or a document with false content shall be punished with imprisonment from one to ten years and with a fine up to the amount of twice the transfer.
(2) (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, repeal. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
(Chapter Seven "Crimes against personal property" with Art. 251 - 268 repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 251. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 74 of 2015, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017, amend. - SG, issue 82 of 2023) Whoever fails to fulfill an obligation to declare cash carried across the border of the country and the value of the object of the crime is in particularly large amounts shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years and with a fine in the amount of one fifth of the value of the object of the crime.
(2) (Repeal. - SG, issue 83 of 2019, new - SG, issue 82 of 2023) Whoever fails to fulfill an obligation to declare cash carried across the border of the country and their lawful origin is not established shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years, and the funds shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 252. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 62 of 1997, suppl. - SG, issue 23 of 2009, in force from 01.11.2009, amend. - SG, issue 13 of 2020, in force from 14.02.2020) Whoever without appropriate authorization carries out as an occupation banking, insurance or other financial transactions, provides payment services or issues electronic money for which such authorization is required shall be punished with imprisonment from three to five years and with confiscation up to 1/2 of the perpetrator's property.
(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 62 of 1997) When the activity under para. 1 has caused significant harm to another or significant unlawful income has been obtained, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva, and the court may order confiscation of part or all of the perpetrator's property.
(3) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on whoever, when carrying out banking activity with authorization, uses funds acquired in violation of the established provisions.
Chapter Seven.
CRIMES AGAINST THE FINANCIAL, TAX AND INSURANCE SYSTEMS (TITLE AMEND. - SG, ISSUE 62 OF 1997, AMEND. - SG, ISSUE 51 OF 2000)
Art. 253. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 85 of 1998, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, suppl. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) Whoever performs a financial operation or transaction with property, or conceals the origin, location, movement or actual rights over property which he knows or suspects has been acquired through a crime or other socially dangerous act, shall be punished for money laundering with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004, suppl. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever acquires, receives, holds, uses, converts or assists in any way in the conversion of property which he knows or suspects at the time of receiving it has been acquired through a crime or other socially dangerous act.
(3) (Previous para. 2, suppl. - SG, issue 26 of 2004) The punishment shall be imprisonment from one to eight years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva when the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed:
1. (amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2004) by two or more persons who have conspired in advance, or by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
2. two or more times;
3. by an official within the scope of his service;
4. (new - SG, issue 26 of 2004) by opening or maintaining an account in a financial institution under a fictitious name or under the name of a person who has not given his consent for this;
5. (new - SG, issue 103 of 2020) by an obliged person under Art. 4 of the Law on Measures against Money Laundering or his employee or worker in or on the occasion of fulfilling the obligations under the same law.
(4) (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, previous para. 3, suppl. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from twenty thousand to two hundred thousand leva when the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed with funds or property which the perpetrator knew or suspected were acquired through a serious intentional crime.
(5) (New - SG, issue 85 of 1998, previous para. 3 - SG, issue 21 of 2000, previous para. 4, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) When the funds or property are in particularly large amounts and the case is particularly serious, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva, and the court shall deprive the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(6) (New - SG, issue 85 of 1998, previous para. 4 - SG, issue 21 of 2000, previous para. 5, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2004) The object of the crime or the property into which it has been transformed shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
(7) (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) The provisions of para. 1 - 6 shall also apply when the crime through which the property was acquired does not fall under the criminal jurisdiction of the Republic of Bulgaria.
Art. 253a. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) (1) Preparation for money laundering or association for such purpose shall be punished with imprisonment up to two years or with a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever incites another to money laundering.
(3) Property intended for money laundering shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
(4) A participant in the association under para. 1 who, before the money laundering is completed, ceases his participation in the association and reports it to the authorities shall not be punished.
Art. 253b. (New - SG, issue 85 of 1998, previous text of Art. 253a, amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, suppl. - SG, issue 84 of 2023) An official who violates or fails to fulfill the provisions of the Law on Measures against Money Laundering or the Law on Measures against Financing of Terrorism, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished in non-trivial cases with imprisonment up to three years and with a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
Art. 254. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997, repeal. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
Art. 254a. (New - SG, issue 51 of 2000) (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) An official who, in violation of a budget law or a subordinate act for its implementation, disposes of budget funds or funds with designated purpose not according to their purpose shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years or with probation, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.
(2) If harmful consequences for the state or municipality have resulted from the act under para. 1, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to five years and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.
(3) (Repeal. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
(4) (Amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In the cases under para. 1 the perpetrator shall not be punished if before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the unlawful disposal act is revoked and the unlawfully spent funds are fully restored. This provision shall not apply repeatedly.
Art. 254b. (New - SG, issue 24 of 2005) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever uses not according to purpose received financial funds from funds belonging to the European Union or provided by the European Union to the Bulgarian state shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If an official orders the act under the preceding paragraph to be performed, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years, and the court may deprive the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 255. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997, in force from 06.11.1997, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever evades the establishment or payment of tax obligations in large amounts by:
1. not submitting a declaration;
2. confirming untruth or concealing truth in a submitted declaration;
3. not issuing an invoice or other accounting document;
4. destroying, concealing or not preserving within the legally established periods accounting documents or accounting registers;
5. carrying out or allowing the carrying out of accounting in violation of the requirements of accounting legislation;
6. compiling or using a document with false content, counterfeit or altered document in carrying out economic activity, in keeping accounting or in presenting information to the revenue authorities or public executors;
7. deducting undue tax credit,
(amend. - SG, issue 26 of 2010) shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and with a fine up to two thousand leva.
(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2008, amend. - SG, issue 12 of 2009, in force from 01.01.2010, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 32 of 2009) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the participation of an employee from border police, customs administration, the National Revenue Agency, or a registered auditor, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(3) When the tax obligations are in particularly large amounts, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to eight years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
(4) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the undeclared or unpaid tax obligation is paid into the budget together with the interest, the punishment under para. 1 and 2 shall be imprisonment up to two years and a fine up to five hundred leva, and under para. 3 - imprisonment up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva.
Art. 255a. (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever evades the establishment or payment of tax obligations in large amounts through transformation of a commercial company or other legal entity, through performing a transaction with a commercial enterprise or through performing a transaction with related persons within the meaning of the Tax and Social Security Procedure Code shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine up to ten thousand leva.
(2) When the tax obligations are in particularly large amounts, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to eight years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
(3) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the undeclared or unpaid tax obligation is paid into the budget together with the interest, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva.
Art. 255b. (New - SG, issue 107 of 2014, in force from 01.01.2015) (1) Whoever conceals mandatory insurance contributions for state social security or for health insurance in large amounts by:
1. declaring an insurance income in a smaller amount than the actual one for the insured person;
2. not submitting a declaration;
3. confirming untruth or concealing truth in a submitted declaration;
4. compiling or using a document with false content, counterfeit or altered document in carrying out economic activity, in keeping accounting or in presenting information to the revenue authorities;
5. destroying or concealing within the legally established periods accounting documents, accounting registers or payroll statements;
shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years and with a fine up to two thousand leva.
(2) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the participation of a revenue authority or a registered auditor, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine up to five thousand leva, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(3) When the obligations for mandatory insurance contributions for state social security or for health insurance are in particularly large amounts, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
(4) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the mandatory insurance contributions for state social security or for health insurance are paid into the budget together with the interest, the punishment under para. 1 and 2 shall be imprisonment up to two years and a fine up to five hundred leva, and under para. 3 - imprisonment up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva.
(5) The worker or employee subject to mandatory insurance does not bear criminal liability under para. 1 - 4, including for instigation and aiding.
Art. 256. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 103 of 2020, in force from 01.01.2021) Whoever by using a document with false content or a counterfeit or altered document obtains from the state budget, from the budget of the state social security or from the budget of the National Health Insurance Fund an undue monetary amount in large amounts or gives another person the opportunity to obtain such an amount shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the participation of a person under Art. 255, para. 2 or by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, or if the obtained monetary amount is in particularly large amounts, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(3) (Amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2020, in force from 01.01.2021) If before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the obtained amount is paid into the state budget, into the budget of the state social security or into the budget of the National Health Insurance Fund together with the interest, the punishment under para. 1 shall be imprisonment up to three years and a fine up to one thousand leva, and under para. 2 - imprisonment up to five years and a fine up to three thousand leva.
Art. 257. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997, in force from 06.11.1997, repeal. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006)
Art. 258. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever unlawfully obstructs a revenue authority from fulfilling its legal obligation shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years and a fine from one thousand to two thousand leva.
(2) If the act under para. 1 is committed through force or threat, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva.
Art. 259. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever creates a legal entity with an ideal purpose or establishes a foundation which does not carry out or carries out ostensibly the activity and purpose declared upon registration, in order to receive under its cover credits, to be exempted from taxes, to receive tax reliefs or to receive other property benefit, as well as to carry out prohibited activity, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years, a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 259a. (New - SG, issue 51 of 2000, declared unconstitutional by CCD No. 14 of 2000 - SG, issue 98 of 2000) (1) An official who authorizes the payment of remuneration without all due mandatory insurance contributions having been paid, if the unpaid amount is in large amounts, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
(2) In the cases under para. 1 the perpetrator shall not be punished if before the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court he fully fulfills his obligation together with the due interest.
Art. 260. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, new - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (1) A licensed appraiser who gives a false appraisal or opinion on the value of appraised property and harm in non-trivial cases results from this shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(2) (Amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2008) A registered auditor who certifies a false annual accounting report of a merchant, knowing this, shall be punished with imprisonment up to one year and a fine up to five hundred leva, as well as with deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 260a. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever acquires or disposes for his own account or for the account of a third party, directly or indirectly, of financial instruments by unlawfully using inside information relating to them, and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this, when committed by a person who possesses inside information because:
1. he is a member of the administrative, management or supervisory body of the issuer or participant in the emissions allowances market, or
2. he has equity participation in the capital of an issuer or participant in the emissions allowances market, or
3. he has access to the information through the performance of activity, profession or official duties,
shall be punished for insider dealing with imprisonment up to four years and with a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva, and the court may order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever acquires or disposes for his own account or for the account of a third party, directly or indirectly, of financial instruments by unlawfully using inside information relating to them, acquired outside the cases under para. 1, which he knows is inside information, and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this act.
(3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever, after having submitted an order for a financial instrument, receives inside information relating to the financial instrument and on the basis of it cancels or modifies it and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this.
(4) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever, after having submitted an order or bid for his own account or for the account of a third party, in auction sales of emissions allowances or other auction products based on them which are conducted pursuant to Commission Regulation (EU) 1031/2010 of 12 November 2010 on the timing, administration and other aspects of auctioning of greenhouse gas emission allowances pursuant to Directive 2003/87/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council establishing a scheme for greenhouse gas emission allowance trading within the Community (OJ, L 302/1 of 18 November 2010), receives inside information relating to them and on the basis of it modifies or cancels the order, or modifies or cancels the bid and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this.
(5) Whoever on the basis of inside information incites another person to acquire or dispose of financial instruments, to modify or cancel an order for a financial instrument to which the inside information relates shall be punished with imprisonment up to four years.
