(6) The punishment under para. 5 shall also be imposed on whoever knows that he was incited on the basis of inside information and submits, modifies or cancels an order for a financial instrument to which the information relates, and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this.
(7) If the act under para. 1 - 6 is committed:
1. by two or more persons who have conspired in advance;
2. by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
3. repeatedly,
the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(8) In the cases under para. 1 - 7 the object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and when it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 260b. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever unlawfully discloses to another person inside information which he possesses regarding a financial instrument, except when the disclosure is made in the ordinary performance of activity, profession or official duties, or constitutes market research, and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this, shall be punished for unlawful disclosure of inside information with imprisonment up to two years.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever discloses an incitement when he knows that the incitement is based on inside information, and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this disclosure.
Art. 260v. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 83 of 2019) Whoever concludes transactions or submits orders for trading or performs another type of activity or action by giving false or misleading signals about the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument or related spot commodity contract or sets this price at an unusual or fictitious level, and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this, shall be punished for market manipulation with imprisonment up to four years and with a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva.
(2) Whoever by using a fictitious device or by misleading concludes transactions or submits orders for trading, or performs another type of activity or action which influences the price of one or more financial instruments or related spot commodity contract, and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to four years.
(3) Whoever with the purpose of securing an advantage or obtaining a benefit for himself or for another disseminates information through media or in another way which gives false or misleading signals about the supply, demand or price of a financial instrument or of a related spot commodity contract, or sets for one or more financial instruments or related spot commodity contract a price at an unusual or fictitious level and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this, shall be punished with imprisonment up to four years and with a fine from three to five thousand leva.
(4) Whoever provides false or misleading information or input data or in any other way manipulates the calculation of a benchmark and significant harmful consequences have resulted from this shall be punished with imprisonment from one to four years.
(5) In the cases under para. 1 - 4 the object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and when it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 261. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 262. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 263. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 264. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 265. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 266. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 267. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Art. 268. (Repeal. - SG, issue 10 of 1993)
Chapter Eight.
CRIMES AGAINST THE ACTIVITY OF STATE BODIES, PUBLIC ORGANIZATIONS AND PERSONS PERFORMING PUBLIC FUNCTIONS (TITLE AMEND. - SG, ISSUE 62 OF 1997, AMEND. - SG, ISSUE 43 OF 2005, IN FORCE FROM 01.09.2005)


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Rules of Procedure |

### Section I

Crimes against the order of government
Art. 269. (Am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever uses force or threat with the aim of compelling an authority, a representative of the public, a private bailiff or an assistant private bailiff to perform or omit something in office or related to his function, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years.
(2) When the crime under para. 1 has been committed by participants in a crowd, the instigators and leaders shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.
Art. 270. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) (1) (Previous text of Art. 270 - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 43 of 2005, in force from 01.09.2005, am. - SG, No. 64 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully obstructs an authority, a private bailiff or an assistant-private bailiff from fulfilling his duties, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or a fine from five hundred to two thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) When the duties of the authority are related to control over trafficking of narcotic substances, analogues or precursors, the punishment is imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.
Art. 270a. (New - SG, No. 26 of 1973, rep. - SG, No. 89 of 1986)
Art. 271. (Rep. - SG, No. 99 of 1989)
Art. 272. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004) Whoever arbitrarily leaves a populated place despite a prohibition established by proper administrative procedure, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six months or probation.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004) Whoever, after appropriate warning, continues systematically to violate the legally established measures for administrative supervision taken against him, shall be punished by probation for a term of one year.
Art. 273. (New - SG, No. 102 of 2006) Whoever discloses information establishing affiliation with State Security or the intelligence services of the Bulgarian People's Army, in violation of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of the Documents and on Announcing the Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens with State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to six years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.
Art. 274. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever arbitrarily performs an act which falls within the scope of the office of an official which he does not hold or from which he has been deprived, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or probation.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who arbitrarily performs an act which falls within the scope of the function of a representative of the public with which he has not been entrusted or from which he has been deprived, and thereby unlawfully affects public or personal interests.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever, without having the right, wears uniform clothing or an official badge, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or probation, as well as by public reprimand.
Art. 274a. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. and suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever holds or wears uniform clothing, official badges or inscriptions in violation of the Law on the Ministry of Internal Affairs, the Law on Defence and the Armed Forces of the Republic of Bulgaria, the Law on Execution of Punishments and Detention in Custody or the Law on the Judicial System, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) If the act under para. 1 has been committed with the aim of committing another crime, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years.
(3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever in any way stains, unlawfully destroys or damages uniform clothing, official badges or inscriptions worn or placed in accordance with the Law on the Ministry of Internal Affairs, the Law on Defence and the Armed Forces of the Republic of Bulgaria, the Law on Execution of Punishments and Detention in Custody or the Law on the Judicial System, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year.
Art. 275. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, suppl. - SG, No. 43 of 2005, in force from 01.09.2005, am. - SG, No. 64 of 2007) Whoever, being obliged by law to give assistance to an authority, a private bailiff or an assistant-private bailiff, does not do so after being duly summoned, shall be punished by probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever, when summoned by a competent official in a case dangerous to the life, health or property of someone, refuses to come to the aid which he can give without danger to himself or to another, shall be punished by probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 276. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever counterfeits or puts into circulation counterfeit official authentication marks, such as seals, marks for the quality of precious metals, entrance tickets, transport tickets and other such, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or probation.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever knowingly uses such a counterfeit mark, shall be punished by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever unlawfully removes, destroys or conceals official authentication marks intended for establishing, paying or accounting for values, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or probation.
(4) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever without proper authorization prepares a seal of a state or public organization, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year or probation.
(5) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who removes a seal of a state or public organization with the aim of using it unlawfully.
Art. 277. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 43 of 2005, in force from 01.09.2005, am. - SG, No. 64 of 2007) Whoever knowingly removes or damages a seal placed on lawful grounds by an authority, a private bailiff or an assistant-private bailiff on movable or immovable property as a sign that access to or disposal of it is restricted, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who disposes of property placed under seizure and left to him for safekeeping.
Art. 277a. (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever without appropriate authorization searches for archaeological objects, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years.
(2) Whoever without appropriate authorization carries out or orders to be carried out field archaeological excavations, geophysical or underwater surveys, or not in the legally established manner carries out excavation works on the territory of an immovable cultural property or in its protection zone, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) If in the commission of the acts under para. 1 and 2 technical means or motor vehicles are used, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(4) Whoever orders or permits the carrying out of unlawful activity in a protected territory for the preservation of cultural heritage, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva.
(5) Whoever continues, orders or permits to be continued the activity under para. 4 after it has been stopped by the competent authorities, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from three thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(6) With the punishments under para. 4 and 5 shall also be punished one who organizes or directs the activity, if he knew or assumed that it is being carried out in violation of the Law on Cultural Heritage.
(7) Whoever unlawfully prepares, holds or conceals objects, materials, instruments or computer programs which he knows or assumes are intended for or have served for searching, storing, altering or transporting archaeological objects, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to six years, and the court may also impose deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(8) In the cases under para. 1 - 6 the court may order confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person, and under para. 7 - of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
Art. 278. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever discovers a cultural property and within seven days does not report it to the authorities, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or a fine from five hundred to three thousand leva.
(2) When the cultural property has particularly high scientific or artistic value, the punishment is imprisonment of up to four years or a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(3) If destruction or damage to the cultural property has ensued, when the perpetrator did not want or did not allow this, the punishment is: in the cases under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to four years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva, and in the cases under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva.
(4) The perpetrator shall not be punished when he reports the discovered cultural property even after the period under para. 1, if the consequences under para. 3 have not occurred.
(5) An official who does not request identification and registration of a cultural property, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to two years, and the court may also impose deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(6) Whoever holds an archaeological object which has not been identified and registered in the appropriate manner, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to four years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva, and when the object of the crime represents national wealth, as well as when more than three archaeological objects are held - by imprisonment of up to six years and a fine from three thousand to fifteen thousand leva. The court may also impose confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
(7) The object of the crime shall be seized in favour of the state.
Art. 278a. (New - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever offers for alienation or alienates a cultural property which has not been identified and registered, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from one thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on one who acquires such a cultural property.
(3) When the acts under para. 1 and 2 have been committed repeatedly or constitute dangerous recidivism, or have been committed by order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, as well as when they have been committed with the aim that the object of the crime be taken out across the borders of the country, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from five thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(4) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who without appropriate authorization takes out across the borders of the country a cultural property.
(5) The punishment under para. 1 - 4 shall also be imposed when the object of the crime is a document from the National Archival Fund.
(6) The object of the crime shall be seized in favour of the state, and when it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded.
Art. 278b. (New - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, in force from 10.04.2009) (1) Whoever unlawfully destroys or damages his own cultural property or document from the National Archival Fund, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or a fine from five hundred to two thousand leva, as well as by public reprimand.
(2) An official who unlawfully gives authorization for the destruction, demolition, damage, alteration or export of a cultural property or of a document from the National Archival Fund, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years or a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva, and the court may also impose deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(3) If from the act under para. 2 destruction, demolition, damage, alteration or export of the cultural property has ensued, the punishment is imprisonment from one to six years, a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva and deprivation of a right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
Art. 278b.SUP>1. (New - SG, No. 1 of 2019) (1) Whoever with the aim of obtaining for himself or for another a property benefit falsifies a work of painting, sculpture, graphic art or archaeological objects or items, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) With the punishment under para. 1 shall also be punished a person who offers for sale or puts into circulation as authentic works of painting, sculpture, graphic art or archaeological objects.
(3) A person who gives a false expert opinion which certifies the authenticity of works of painting, sculpture, graphic art or archaeological objects or items, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to one year and a fine from three thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(4) If the act has been committed by a person in the exercise of commercial activity, the punishment is imprisonment of up to three years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva, as well as deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(5) The object of the crime shall be seized in favour of the state.
Art. 278c. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 86 of 1991, previous Art. 278a - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever unlawfully destroys or damages a protected territory or habitat - object of preservation in a protected zone, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation, as well as a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) Whoever destroys or damages exceptionally valuable individual and irreplaceable earth and rock formations and caves declared protected, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) When the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed by negligence, the punishment is probation and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
Art. 278d. (New - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever unlawfully destroys, damages, acquires, holds or alienates a specimen of a protected species of wild flora or fauna, when it does not constitute a minor case, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation, as well as a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) Whoever trades in specimens of protected species of wild flora or fauna or in their parts or products, when it does not constitute a minor case, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) When the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed by negligence, the punishment is probation and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
Art. 278e. (New - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) Whoever unlawfully destroys, damages, holds, acquires or alienates a specimen of European or globally endangered wild vertebrate animals or a specimen of a species under Annex No. 3 to the Law on Biological Diversity, marked with the sign (*), shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years, as well as a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.
Art. 279. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever enters or exits across the border of the country without authorization from the competent authorities or even with authorization, but not through the places designated for this, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to six years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, in force from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2004, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 has been committed repeatedly or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is imprisonment from three to eight years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(3) (Previous para. 2, am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) In the cases under the preceding paragraphs the court instead of a fine may impose confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person.
(4) (Previous para. 3, am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Preparation for a crime under para. 1 and 2 shall be punished by imprisonment of up to three years or probation.
(5) (Previous para. 4 - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) One who enters the country in order to avail himself of the right of asylum in accordance with the Constitution shall not be punished.
Art. 280. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, rep. No. 37 of 1989, new - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever transports across the border of the country individual persons or groups of people without authorization from the competent authorities or even with authorization, but not through the places designated for this, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to eight years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator, if:
1. (am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) a minor or juvenile has been transported across the border of the country;
2. the transportation took place without the knowledge of the person;
3. the person transported across the border is not a Bulgarian citizen;
4. a motor, aircraft or other means of transport has been used;
5. (am. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, suppl. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) the transportation has been organized by a group or organization or has been committed by order and/or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
6. (new - SG, No. 27 of 2009) the transportation has been carried out in a manner dangerous to the life of the transported persons;
7. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) the act has been accompanied by resistance and/or disobedience towards an authority;
8. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) the act has been committed through destruction, undermining or damage to a facility for border protection.
(3) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years, a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator, when the act has been committed with the participation of an official who has taken advantage of his official position.
(4) (Previous para. 3, suppl. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, declared unconstitutional in the part "or has been provided voluntarily" by CCD No. 12 of 2021 - SG, No. 84 of 2021, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 and 2 has been committed repeatedly or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years, a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva and confiscation of part or all of the property of the perpetrator.
(5) (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) In the cases under para. 2, item 5 the vehicle shall be seized in favour of the state, if it is the property of the perpetrator, or a fine in the amount of the market value of the vehicle in leva shall be imposed, when it is not his property.
Art. 281. (1) (Rep. - SG, No. 37 of 1989, new - SG, No. 27 of 2009, previous text of Art. 281, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever in any way unlawfully assists a foreigner to stay or pass through the country in violation of the law, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from three thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment is imprisonment from two to ten years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva, when the act has been:
1. committed through the use of a motor, aircraft or other means of transport;
2. (suppl. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) organized by a group or organization or by order and/or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
3. committed in a manner dangerous to the life of the person;
4. (am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) committed in relation to a minor or juvenile;
5. committed in relation to more than one person;
6. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) committed with the aim that the perpetrator obtain for himself or for another a property benefit;
7. (new - SG, No. 67 of 2023) accompanied by resistance and/or disobedience towards an authority.
(3) (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015, declared unconstitutional in the part "or has been provided voluntarily" by CCD No. 12 of 2021 - SG, No. 84 of 2021, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) If the act under para. 1 and 2 has been committed repeatedly or constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(4) (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) In the cases under para. 2, item 1 the vehicle shall be seized in favour of the state, if it is the property of the perpetrator, or a fine in the amount of the market value of the vehicle in leva shall be imposed, when it is not his property.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section II

Crimes in Office
Art. 282. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) An official who violates or fails to perform his official duties, or exceeds his authority or rights with the purpose of obtaining for himself or for another a benefit or of causing harm to another and from which significant harmful consequences may occur, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years, and the court may also order deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6, or with probation.
(2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) If the act has resulted in significant harmful consequences or has been committed by a person who holds a responsible official position, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years, and the court may also order deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(3) (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) For particularly serious cases under the preceding paragraph, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years, and the court shall order deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(4) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed on an official who has committed the crime with the participation of a person referred to in Art. 142, para. 2, items 6 and 8.
(5) (New - SG, No. 21 of 2000) If the act under the preceding paragraphs is related to the exercise of control over the production, processing, storage, domestic trade, import, export, transit and accounting of narcotic substances and precursors, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to ten years under para. 1 and from three to fifteen years under para. 2.
Art. 282a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) An official who, in the presence of the conditions provided for in a normative act necessary for the issuance of a special permit for the performance of a specific activity, refuses or delays beyond the time limits provided by law its issuance, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years, a fine of up to five hundred leva and deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
Art. 283. (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 1973, am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) An official who uses his official position to obtain for himself or for another an unlawful benefit shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
Art. 283a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) If the crimes under Art. 282 and 283 are related to the privatization, sale, leasing or renting, as well as the contribution to commercial companies of state, municipal and cooperative property, as well as property of legal entities, the punishment shall be:
1. under Art. 282 - deprivation of liberty from three to ten years, a fine from three thousand to five thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7;
2. under Art. 283 - deprivation of liberty from one to three years, a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
Art. 283b. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) An official who obstructs or hinders the exercise by owners of their rights restored under the Law on Restitution of Ownership of Nationalized Immovable Property, under the Law on Restitution of Ownership of Certain Expropriated Property under the Law on Territorial and Settlement Planning, the Law on Planned Construction of Populated Places, the Law on Improvement of Populated Places, the Law on State Property and the Law on Ownership and under the Law on Ownership and Use of Agricultural Land and the Law on Compensation of Owners of Nationalized Property, the Law on Privatization and Post-Privatization Control or through final judicial acts under another law, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from two to six years.
Art. 284. (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) An official who, to the detriment of the state, an enterprise, organization or a private person, communicates to another or publishes information that has been entrusted to him or is accessible to him by virtue of his office and which he knows constitutes an official secret, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years or with probation.
(2) The punishment for an act under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a non-official person working in a state institution, enterprise or public organization, to whom information constituting an official secret has become known in connection with his work.
(3) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 9 of 2021, in force from 06.02.2021) If the act under para. 1 has been committed by an expert, translator or Bulgarian sign language interpreter with respect to information that has become known to him in connection with the task assigned to him, which he is obliged to keep secret, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation.
Art. 284a. (New - SG, No. 102 of 2006) An official from the composition of the Commission for Disclosure of Documents and for Announcing Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens to State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army or from its administration, who discloses information or disseminates a document in violation of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of the Documents and on Announcing Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens to State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army, which have become known to him in that capacity, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to six years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.
Art. 284b. (New - SG, No. 102 of 2006) An official who does not remove from the records a collaborator or undercover employee holding a public office or performing a public activity within the meaning of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of the Documents and on Announcing Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens to State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to six years and a fine of fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.
Art. 284c. (New - SG, No. 27 of 2009) An official who unlawfully authorizes or orders the use of special intelligence means, or applies them, or stores information obtained through them, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years and with a fine of up to five thousand leva.
Art. 285. An official who knowingly allows a person subordinate to him to commit a crime related to his office or work shall be punished with the punishment provided for the crime committed.

### Section III

Crimes against the administration of justice
Art. 286. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever falsely accuses someone of a crime before a competent authority, knowing that the person is innocent, or presents false evidence against them, shall be punished for false accusation with imprisonment from one to six years and with public reprimand.
(2) (Previous para. 3 - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) If the falsely accused person is brought to criminal liability, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to ten years.
Art. 287. (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An official who, during or in connection with the performance of their duties, alone or through another, commits unlawful coercive acts against an accused, witness or expert witness in order to extract a confession, testimony, conclusion or information from them, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years and with deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 287a. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever, with the purpose of misleading a judicial authority:
1. prepares false technical records or alters genuine ones;
2. destroys a record or part of it, collects or arranges data from the record and thereby creates a false impression of the relevant circumstance;
3. uses falsified technical records;
4. unlawfully uses information obtained by special intelligence means, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand leva.
Art. 288. (Amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An authority who fails to perform in a timely manner the duties imposed by their office regarding criminal prosecution, or otherwise obstructs such prosecution with the purpose of sparing another from punishment due by law, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and with deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.
Art. 289. (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever induces an official from the investigative authorities or from the prosecutorial or judicial authorities to violate their official duty in connection with the administration of justice, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years.
Art. 290. (1) Whoever, before a court or before another competent authority, as a witness orally or in writing knowingly confirms an untruth or conceals the truth, shall be punished for perjury with imprisonment up to five years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 9 of 2021, effective from 06.02.2021) The same punishment shall be imposed on an interpreter or interpreter of Bulgarian sign language who, before a court or before another competent authority, in writing or orally knowingly gives a false translation.
Art. 290a. (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) Whoever confirms an untruth or conceals the truth in a written declaration submitted before a court shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
Art. 291. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever, as an expert witness before a court or before another competent authority, orally or in writing knowingly gives a false conclusion, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years and with deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 7.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) If the act under the previous paragraph is committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to one year or probation. The court may also order deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 7.
Art. 292. (1) (Previous text of Art. 292 - SG, issue 89 of 1986) For a crime under Art. 290 and 291, punishability shall cease:
1. when the person, if they tell the truth, would accuse themselves of a crime, and
2. (amended - SG, issue 9 of 2021, effective from 06.02.2021) when the person retracts before the competent authority their false testimony, translation or conclusion before the judgment or decision enters into force and before criminal prosecution has been initiated against them for this.
(2) (New - SG, issue 89 of 1986) The provision of point 2 of the previous paragraph shall also apply in cases under Art. 290a, when the person withdraws their declaration before a decision has been issued in the case in which it was submitted.
Art. 293. (1) (Previous text of Art. 293, supplemented - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever incites another to a crime under Art. 290, 290a and 291 shall be punished with imprisonment up to one year or with probation.
(2) (New - SG, issue 89 of 1986) If two or more persons are incited and the case is particularly serious, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years.
Art. 293a. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever, after being sentenced to fulfill a monetary obligation by a judicial act that has entered into force and for one year from its entry into force does not fulfill their obligation to the creditor, despite having monetary means or property for this, shall be punished with imprisonment up to one year or with probation.
Art. 294. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever assists a person who has committed a crime to escape or to have criminal prosecution against them obstructed or to remain unpunished, without having agreed with that person before they committed the crime itself, shall be punished for personal harboring with imprisonment up to five years, but not with a more severe punishment than that provided for the harbored person.
(2) If this is done with the purpose of material gain, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to five years, but not more severe than that provided for the harbored person.
(3) The above provisions shall not apply to the spouses, descendants, ascendants, brothers and sisters of the harbored person and their spouses.
(4) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009) If the perpetrator is a judge, prosecutor, investigator or police authority, investigating police officer, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years.
Art. 295. (Amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An authority who, with the purpose of sparing another from punishment or delaying the execution of punishment, fails to execute a judgment that has entered into force, if by office they were obliged to do what is necessary for bringing the judgment into execution, shall be punished with imprisonment up to six years, and the court may deprive them of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6, or with probation.
Art. 296. (Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 102 of 2009, effective from 22.12.2009, supplemented - SG, issue 41 of 2015, effective from 06.07.2015) Whoever obstructs or frustrates in any way the execution of a court decision or fails to comply with a protection order against domestic violence or a European Protection Order, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years or a fine up to five thousand leva.
(2) Whoever, with the purpose of obstructing or frustrating the execution of a court decision, destroys, damages, conceals or alienates a thing to which that decision relates, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years or a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime.
(3) (New - SG, issue 60 of 2011) Whoever violates a ban on attending sports events in the country or abroad, during the term of the ban imposed under the procedure of the Law on the Preservation of Public Order during the Conduct of Sports Events, after having been punished for the same act by administrative procedure, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years or with probation.
(4) (New - SG, issue 16 of 2019) If the act under para. 1 is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to five years.
Art. 297. (1) A prisoner who escapes shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
(2) If for the escape the prisoner used undermining, knocking down, destroying walls, doors, windows and others, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to five years.
(3) A prisoner is anyone detained in custody according to the procedure established by law.
Art. 298. (1) Prisoners who have agreed to escape through joint efforts shall be punished with imprisonment up to two years.
(2) If the prisoners proceed to execute the intended escape, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 299. (Amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An official who arbitrarily releases or lets a prisoner escape shall be punished with imprisonment up to five years, and the court may deprive the guilty person of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6.
Art. 300. (Amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amended regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, repealed - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005)

### Section IV

Bribery
Art. 301. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) An official who requests or accepts a gift or any benefit not due to him, or accepts an offer or promise of a gift or benefit, in order to perform or not perform an official act or because he has performed or has not performed such an act, shall be punished for bribery with imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva. (2) (Am. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the official has committed any of the acts under para. 1 in order to violate or because he has violated his duties, when this violation does not constitute a crime, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to eight years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva. (3) (Am. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, am. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) If the official has committed any of the acts under para. 1 in order to commit or because he has committed another crime in connection with his duties, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to ten years and a fine of up to fifteen thousand leva. (4) (Am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) In the cases under the preceding paragraphs, the court shall also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. (5) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a foreign official who requests or accepts a bribe or accepts an offer or promise of a bribe. Art. 302. For bribery committed:
1. (suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) by a person holding a responsible official position, including a judge, lay judge, prosecutor or investigator, or by a police officer, or by an investigating police officer; 2. through extortion by means of abuse of official position; 3. (am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) repeatedly and
4. on a large scale, the punishment shall be:
a) (suppl. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) in the cases of Art. 301, para. 1 and 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years, a fine of up to twenty thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7; b) (am. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) in the cases of Art. 301, para. 3 - imprisonment from three to fifteen years, a fine of up to twenty-five thousand leva and confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person, and the court shall also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. Art. 302a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) For bribery on an especially large scale, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to thirty years, a fine of up to thirty thousand leva, confiscation of all or part of the property of the guilty person and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. Art. 303. (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, rep. - SG, No. 74 of 2015)
Art. 304. (Am. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) Whoever offers, promises or gives a gift or any benefit to an official in order for him to perform or not perform an official act or because he has performed or has not performed such an act, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva. (2) If in connection with the bribery the official has violated his official duties, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to eight years and a fine of up to seven thousand leva, when this violation does not constitute a more severely punishable crime. (3) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on one who offers, promises or gives a bribe to a foreign official. Art. 304a. (New - SG, No. 51 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever offers, promises or gives a bribe to an official holding a responsible official position, including a judge, lay judge, prosecutor or investigator, or to a police officer, or to an investigating police officer, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to ten years and a fine of up to fifteen thousand leva. Art. 304b. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) Whoever requests or accepts a gift or any benefit not due to him, or accepts an offer or promise of a gift or benefit, in order to exercise influence in the decision-making of an official or foreign official in connection with his duties, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years or a fine of up to five thousand leva. (2) Whoever offers, promises or gives a gift or any undue benefit to a person who claims that he can exercise influence under para. 1, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or a fine of up to three thousand leva. Art. 304c. (New - SG, No. 74 of 2015) The punishments under the preceding articles shall also be imposed when, with the consent of the official, the foreign official or the person who claims that he can exercise influence, the gift or benefit is offered, promised or given to another. Art. 305. (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) The punishments for bribery under the preceding articles shall also be imposed on an arbitrator or expert appointed by a court, institution, enterprise or organization, when they commit such acts in connection with their activities, as well as on one who offers, promises or gives such a bribe. (2) The punishments for bribery under the preceding articles shall also be imposed on a defense counsel or attorney, when they commit such acts in order to help resolve a criminal or civil case in favor of the opposing party or to the detriment of the client, as well as on one who offers, promises or gives such a bribe. Art. 305a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever mediates the commission of any of the acts under the preceding articles, if this does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to five thousand leva. Art. 306. (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) One who has offered, promised or given a bribe shall not be punished if he was coerced by the official, arbitrator or expert to do so and if he immediately and voluntarily reported it to the authorities. Art. 307. (Am. - SG, No. 51 of 2000) Whoever deliberately creates a situation or conditions in order to provoke the offering, giving or receiving of a bribe with the aim of harming the one who gave or accepted the bribe, shall be punished for provocation to bribery with imprisonment of up to three years. Art. 307a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The object of the crimes under this section shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and when it is missing, its equivalent value shall be awarded. Chapter Eight "a". CRIMES AGAINST SPORT (NEW - SG, NO. 60 OF 2011)
Art. 307b. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) Whoever by violence, fraud, threat or other unlawful means induces another to influence the course or result of a sports competition administered by a sports organization, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva. Art. 307c. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) (1) Whoever promises, offers or gives to another a benefit not due to him, in order to influence or because he has influenced the course or result of a sports competition administered by a sports organization, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva. (2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on one who requests or accepts any benefit not due to him, or accepts an offer or promise of a benefit with the aim of influencing or because he has influenced the course or result of a sports competition, as well as when with his consent the benefit is offered, promised or given to another. (3) Whoever mediates the commission of any of the acts under para. 1 and 2, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to five thousand leva. (4) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who provides or organizes the offering or giving of the benefit. (5) The perpetrator shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55, if he voluntarily reports to a competent authority a crime committed under para. 1 - 4. Art. 307d. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) (1) The punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva, when the act under Art. 307b or Art. 307c is committed:
1. in relation to a participant in a competition who has not reached 18 years of age; 2. in relation to two or more participants in a competition; 3. in relation to or by a person from a management or supervisory body of a sports organization, sports referee, delegate or other person, during or in connection with the performance of his duties or function; 4. repeatedly. (2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva, when the act under Art. 307b or Art. 307c:
1. is committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group; 2. is committed under the conditions of dangerous recidivism; 3. constitutes an especially serious case; 4. relates to a competition included in a gambling game with bets on the course or results of sports competitions. Art. 307e. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) (1) In the cases under Art. 307b, 307c and 307d, the court may impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. (2) In the cases under Art. 307d, the court may also impose confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person. Art. 307f. (New - SG, No. 60 of 2011) The object of the crime under this chapter shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be awarded. Chapter Nine. DOCUMENT CRIMES
Art. 308. (1) Whoever composes a false official document or alters the content of an official document with the aim that it be used, shall be punished for forgery of a document with imprisonment of up to three years
(2) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 27 of 2009) When the object of the act under para. 1 is certificates of heirs or civil status acts, notarial acts or notarial certifications, Bulgarian or foreign identity documents, documents for completed education or acquired qualifications, driving licenses for vehicles, vehicle registration documents, visa stickers, other documents certifying the transfer or establishment of ownership rights or other real rights, legal capacity, personal or registration data, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to eight years. (3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) The punishment shall be imprisonment of up to ten years, when:
1. (am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017) the act under para. 1 is with the aim of facilitating the commission of a crime under Art. 108a, para. 1, 2, 6 or 7; 2. the act under para. 2 is with the aim of property gain. (4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, former para. 2, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) In minor cases the punishment shall be:
1. (am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) under para. 1 - imprisonment of up to six months or probation; 2. under para. 2 - imprisonment of up to two years; 3. under para. 3 - imprisonment of up to three years. (5) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) For preparation for a crime under para. 1, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year. For preparation for a crime under para. 2 and 3 or for association with the aim of committing any of the crimes provided therein, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to six years. (6) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) A participant in the association who, before the composition of a false official document or the alteration of the content of an official document is completed (if the association was formed with such aim) or before the distribution of false or altered documents begins (if the association was formed for this), withdraws from the act and reports it to the authorities, shall not be punished. (7) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever prepares, holds or conceals objects, materials or instruments, which he knows or assumes are intended or have served for the composition or alteration of a document under para. 2 and 3, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years. Art. 309. (1) Whoever himself or through another composes a false private document or alters the content of a private document and uses it to prove that a right or obligation or a legal relationship exists or does not exist or has been terminated or changed, shall be punished for forgery of a document with imprisonment of up to two years. (2) If the crime has as its object securities, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years. (3) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017) When the act under para. 1 is committed in order to prove that rights over shares, shares in a commercial company or part or a whole commercial enterprise as a totality of rights, obligations and factual relationships exist or do not exist or have been terminated or changed, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years. (4) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017) When by an act under para. 3 damages on an especially large scale have been caused, constituting an especially serious case, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to ten years. The court may impose confiscation of part or all of the property of the guilty person and deprive him of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. (5) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017) In minor cases under para. 1 and 2, the punishment shall be probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and under para. 3 - imprisonment of up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva. Art. 310. (1) (Am. and suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) If the crime under Art. 308, para. 1 and 309, para. 1 and 2, is committed by an official within the scope of his duties, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to five years, and in the cases under Art. 308, para. 2 and 3 - imprisonment of up to twelve years, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6. (2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) In minor cases the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation. Art. 311. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) An official who, within the scope of his duties, composes an official document in which he certifies false circumstances or statements, with the aim that this document be used as evidence for these circumstances or statements, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years, and the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, point 6. (2) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) In minor cases the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to one year or probation. Art. 312. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) A physician who provides someone with a false certificate about the state of his health, when not acting as an official, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to two years or with probation. (2) (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Under the same conditions, a veterinarian who issues a document with false content about the health condition of an animal shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or with probation. Art. 313. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever confirms a falsehood or conceals the truth in a written declaration or communication sent electronically, which by virtue of law, decree or resolution of the Council of Ministers is given before an authority to certify the truth of certain circumstances, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva. (2) (New - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 50 of 1995, am. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) When the act under para. 1 is committed with the aim of avoiding payment of due taxes, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to three years or a fine of up to one thousand leva. (3) (Former para. 2 - am., SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on one who confirms a falsehood or conceals the truth in a private document or communication sent electronically, in which by express provision of law, decree or resolution of the Council of Ministers he is specially obliged to certify the truth, and uses this document as evidence for the falsely certified circumstances or statements. (4) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever in connection with a public offering of securities in a prospectus or review of economic condition uses false favorable data or conceals unfavorable such data, which are of essential importance in making a decision to acquire securities, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to five hundred leva. Art. 313a. (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, am. - SG, No. 99 of 1989, rep., No. 10 of 1993, former Art. 313b - No. 10 of 1993) (1) Whoever in a declaration under Art. 4, para. 2 of the Law on the Property of the Bulgarian Communist Party, the Bulgarian Agrarian People's Union, the Fatherland Front, the Dimitrov Communist Youth Union, the Union of Active Fighters Against Fascism and Capitalism and the Bulgarian Trade Unions confirms a falsehood or conceals the truth with the aim of thwarting wholly or partially the confiscation of unlawfully held state property, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to eight years. (2) Whoever after being requested refuses to give a declaration under Art. 4, para. 2 of the law specified in para. 1, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to six years. (3) In the cases under para. 1 and 2, the court may also impose deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. (4) The perpetrator under para. 1 and 2 shall not be punished if in revealing the truth he would accuse of a crime himself, his spouse, his descendants, ascendants, brothers or sisters. Art. 313b. (New - SG, No. 102 of 2006) (1) Whoever destroys, conceals, alters or damages a document of State Security or of the intelligence services of the Bulgarian People's Army, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to six years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva. (2) The same punishment shall also be imposed on one who holds a document in violation of the Law on Access to and Disclosure of the Documents and on Declaring Affiliation of Bulgarian Citizens to State Security and the Intelligence Services of the Bulgarian People's Army. Art. 314. (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever intentionally causes false circumstances or statements to be entered in an official document composed according to the established procedure on the basis of an application by a private person, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to two years or with probation. Art. 315. (1) Whoever composes a document by filling in a sheet bearing the signature of the issuer with content that does not correspond to the will of the signatory, shall be punished in accordance with the distinctions in Art. 308 and 309. (2) In accordance with the same distinctions, one who by fraud induces another to sign a document with content that does not correspond to the will of the signatory shall also be punished. Art. 316. The punishment provided by the preceding articles of this chapter shall also be imposed on one who knowingly uses a false or altered document, a document with false content or one under the preceding article, when he cannot be held criminally liable for the composition itself. Art. 317. (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever unlawfully uses a document, knowing that the issuer signed it without intention to be bound by it, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to two years or with probation. Art. 318. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever unlawfully uses an official document issued for another person, with the aim of deceiving an official or representative of the public, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to two years or with probation, or with a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva. Art. 319. (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever destroys, conceals or damages another's document or one not exclusively belonging to him with the aim of causing harm to another or obtaining a benefit for himself or another, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years or with probation. Chapter Nine "a". COMPUTER CRIMES (NEW - SG, NO. 92 OF 2002)
Art. 319a. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever unlawfully gains access to an information system or parts thereof, in non-minor cases shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years and with a fine of up to three thousand leva. (2) (Am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed by two or more persons who have conspired in advance to commit such an act, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva. (3) (Suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed repeatedly or in relation to data for creating an electronic signature, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to seven years and a fine of up to twenty thousand leva. (4) (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the acts under para. 1 - 3 are committed in relation to information constituting a state or other secret protected by law or against an information system that is part of critical infrastructure, the punishment shall be from four to eight years imprisonment, if not subject to more severe punishment. (5) (Am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If serious consequences have resulted from the act under para. 4, the punishment shall be from five to twelve years. Art. 319b. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever unlawfully adds, copies, uses, modifies, transfers, deletes, damages, impairs, conceals, destroys computer data in an information system or stops access to such data, in non-minor cases shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva. (2) (Am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If by the act under para. 1 significant damages have been caused or other serious consequences have occurred, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to seven years and a fine of up to twenty thousand leva. (3) (Am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed with the aim of property gain, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to seven years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva. (4) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is committed through a computer program, password, access code or other access data to an information system or to part thereof, intended to affect more than one information system, and the consequences under para. 2 have occurred, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to eight years and a fine of up to twenty thousand leva. (5) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) The punishment shall be imprisonment from nine to twelve years and a fine of up to thirty thousand leva, when the act under para. 1:
1. is committed by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group; 2. is committed against an information system that is part of critical infrastructure. Art. 319c. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever commits an act under Art. 319b in relation to data that is given by virtue of law, electronically or on an electronic or other medium, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to seven years and with a fine of up to five thousand leva. (2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 is with the aim of thwarting the fulfillment of an obligation, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to seven years and a fine of up to seven thousand leva. Art. 319d. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever introduces a computer virus into an information system or computer network, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to seven years and a fine of up to five thousand leva. (2) (New - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on one who introduces another computer program that is intended to disrupt the operation of an information system or computer network or to discover, erase, delete, modify or copy computer data without permission, when such is required, insofar as the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime. (3) (Former para. 2, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If from the act under para. 1 and 2 significant damages have occurred or it is committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to nine years and a fine of up to twenty thousand leva. (4) (New - SG, No. 53 of 2022) If the act under para. 1 and 2 is committed against an information system or computer network that is part of critical infrastructure, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twelve years and a fine of up to twenty thousand leva. Art. 319e. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, am. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022, suppl. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever creates, obtains for himself or another, imports, exports, transfers, transports, provides or otherwise distributes computer programs, passwords, codes or other similar access data to an information system or part thereof with the aim of committing a crime under Art. 171, para. 3, Art. 319a, Art. 319b, Art. 319c or Art. 319d, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years. (2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) When by the act under para. 1 personal data, classified information or other secret protected by law has been disclosed, insofar as the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, the punishment shall be imprisonment from four to seven years. (3) (New - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) For an act under para. 1, committed with a mercenary aim or by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group, or if by it significant damages have been caused or other serious consequences have occurred, the punishment shall be imprisonment of up to nine years. Art. 319f. (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 85 of 2017, suppl. - SG, No. 53 of 2022) Whoever in providing information services violates the provisions of Art. 6, para. 2, point 5 of the Electronic Document and Electronic Identification Services Act, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years and a fine of up to five thousand leva, if not subject to more severe punishment. Chapter Ten. CRIMES AGAINST ORDER AND PUBLIC PEACE
Art. 320. (1) (Former text of Art. 320 - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) Whoever openly incites the commission of a crime through preaching before a multitude of people through mass media or in another similar manner, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years, but not with more severe punishment than that provided for the crime itself. (2) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002, am. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, rep. - SG, No. 84 of 2023)
(3) (New - SG, No. 67 of 2023) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed when the act under para. 1 is committed on racist, xenophobic or related to sexual orientation motives. Art. 320a. (New - SG, No. 41 of 1985, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, am. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, am. - SG, No. 101 of 2017, am. - SG, No. 84 of 2023) Whoever threatens to commit a crime under Art. 330, Art. 333, Art. 334, Art. 336a, Art. 340, Art. 341a, Art. 341b, Art. 341c, Art. 342, para. 3, Art. 344, Art. 349, Art. 350, Art. 352, para. 1, Art. 356e, Art. 356k, Art. 356l or Art. 356m and this threat could cause reasonable apprehension for its realization, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to two years. Art. 321. (1) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever forms or leads an organized criminal group, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years. (2) (Am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever participates in such a group, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years. (3) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997, am. - SG, No. 21 of 2000, am. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, suppl. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, am. and suppl. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) When the group is armed or created with a mercenary aim or with the aim of committing crimes under Art. 142, Art. 142a, Art. 143a, Art. 243, 244, 253, 280, 337, Art. 339, para. 1 - 4, Art. 354a, para. 1 and 2 and Art. 354b, para. 1 - 4 or an official participates in it, the punishment shall be:
1. under para. 1 - imprisonment from five to fifteen years; 2. under para. 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years. (4) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) A participant in the group shall not be punished if he voluntarily surrenders to the authorities and discloses everything known to him about the group, before a crime has been committed by him or by it. (5) (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) A participant in the group who voluntarily surrenders to the authorities, discloses everything known to him about the group and thereby substantially facilitates the detection and proof of crimes committed by it, shall be punished under the conditions of Art. 55. (6) (New - SG, No. 92 of 2002) Whoever conspires with one or more persons to commit crimes in the country or abroad, for which imprisonment of more than three years is provided and which aim to obtain property gain or to exercise unlawful influence over the activity of an authority or local self-government, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to six years. Art. 321a. (New - SG, No. 62 of 1997) (1) Whoever participates in the leadership of an organization or group that through the use of force or instilling fear concludes transactions or extracts benefits, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to eight years. (2) Whoever participates in such an organization or group, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to five years. (3) Property acquired by the organization, by the group or by the participants in them from this activity shall be confiscated in favor of the state, if the persons from whom it was acquired or their heirs are unknown. (4) In the cases under the preceding paragraphs, the provision of Art. 321, para. 4 and 5 shall apply. Art. 322. (Am. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever does not prevent the commission of an obviously serious crime, if he could have done so without significant difficulty and without danger to himself or another, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to one year or with probation. Art. 323. (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever arbitrarily, not according to the procedure established by law, exercises his own or another's actual or presumed right disputed by another, shall be punished in non-minor cases with imprisonment of up to five years and a fine of up to one thousand leva. (2) (Am. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, am. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) Whoever arbitrarily occupies immovable property from the possession of which he has been removed by due procedure, shall be punished with imprisonment of up to three years and a fine of up to five hundred leva. (3) The perpetrator shall not be punished if after a warning from the respective state authority he immediately restores the original factual situation. (4) The provision of the preceding paragraph shall not apply if the perpetrator after the restoration commits the same act again.(5) (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) When the act under para. 1 is committed by force or threat, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to 6 years. Art. 323a. (New - SG, issue 27 of 1973, amend. issue 28 of 1982, corr. issue 31 of 1982, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever constructs a building on arable land, agricultural land or pasture, without having the right to do so, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to two years and a fine from one thousand to three thousand leva. (2) In case of repeated commission of the crime under para. 1, as well as in case of continuation of the construction after it has been stopped by the competent authorities, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to three years and a fine from two to five thousand leva, as well as public reprimand. Art. 324. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 1 of 1991) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever practices a profession or trade without having the relevant legal capacity shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to one year or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva. (2) (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, suppl. - SG, issue 83 of 1998, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 76 of 2005, effective from 01.01.2007, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006) When the profession or trade is related to public health care, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva. The same punishment shall be imposed on a physician, dentist or master pharmacist who practices their profession in violation of the established procedure. (3) (New - SG, issue 89 of 1986, amend. - issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) If the act under the previous paragraph is committed repeatedly, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. Art. 325.
