(1) (Amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever commits indecent acts grossly violating public order and expressing obvious disrespect for society shall be punished for hooliganism with deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation, as well as public reprimand. (2) When the act is accompanied by resistance against an authority or a representative of the public performing duties to protect public order, or when by its content it is distinguished by exceptional cynicism or audacity, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to five years. (3) (New - SG, issue 95 of 2016) When the act under para. 1 or 2 is committed while operating a motor vehicle, the punishment is: under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty for up to three years and deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 7; under para. 2 - deprivation of liberty for up to five years and deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 7. (4) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, previous para. 3, suppl. - SG, issue 95 of 2016) When the act under the previous paragraphs is committed repeatedly, the punishment is: under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty for up to three years; under para. 2 and 3 - deprivation of liberty from one to five years. (5) (Previous para. 3, amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, previous para. 4, amend. - SG, issue 95 of 2016) When the act under para. 1 - 3 constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to six years. Art. 325a. (New - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever organizes or participates in conducting animal fights, breeds, trains or provides animals for fights, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to six years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva. (2) (Amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) The punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to six years and a fine from six thousand to fifty thousand leva, when:
1. the act is committed repeatedly; 2. the act is committed by a veterinarian or technician, by a person carrying out an activity or profession related to breeding animals or caring for them; 3. a minor or juvenile is present at the fights; 4. the fights are recorded for the purpose of distribution. (3) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on whoever organizes or participates in betting on animal fights. (4) The perpetrator shall not be punished if he voluntarily reports to a competent authority the crime committed under para. 1 and 3. (5) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 1 - 3 constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from two to seven years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva. Art. 325b. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.07.2011) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever, by showing cruelty to a vertebrate animal, unlawfully causes it death, serious or permanent injury, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty from one to four years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva. (2) (Amend. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) The punishment is from two to four years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva, when the act under para. 1 is committed:
1. by a person who carries out an activity or profession related to breeding animals or caring for them; 2. in a manner or by means dangerous to the life of people or animals, in a particularly painful manner for the animal or with particular cruelty; 3. in a public place or in the presence of a minor or juvenile; 4. repeatedly. (3) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) When the act under para. 2 and 3 constitutes dangerous recidivism, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from two to five years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva. Art. 325c. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.07.2011) (1) Whoever does not provide sufficient care for a vertebrate animal under his supervision, as a result of which it causes medium or serious bodily injury to a person, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to three years or probation and a fine of up to five thousand leva. (2) In the cases under para. 1, if death has resulted, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine of up to ten thousand leva. Art. 326. (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 41 of 1985, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, previous text of Art. 326 - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever transmits by radio, telephone or in another manner false calls or misleading signals for help, accident or alarm, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to two years. (2) (New - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amend. - SG, issue 28 of 2020, effective from 24.03.2020) If the act under para. 1 has resulted in significant harmful consequences, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to five years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva. Art. 327. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 60 of 2011) (1) Whoever organizes a game of chance not in accordance with the procedure established by law shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to six years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva. (2) The punishment is deprivation of liberty from one to eight years and a fine from two thousand to fifty thousand leva, when the act under para. 1:
1. is committed under conditions of dangerous recidivism; 2. constitutes a particularly serious case; 3. involves betting on the development or result of a sports competition administered by a sports organization. (3) Whoever participates in a game of chance organized not in accordance with the procedure established by law shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to one year or probation. (4) When the act under para. 3 is committed repeatedly, the punishment is deprivation of liberty for up to three years and a fine from five hundred to five thousand leva. (5) The money or things that are the subject of the game of chance shall be confiscated in favor of the state, and if they are missing or have been alienated, their equivalent value shall be awarded. (6) The perpetrator under para. 3 shall not be punished if he voluntarily reports to a competent authority the crime committed under para. 1 and 2. Art. 328. (Amend. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, issue 26 of 2010)
Art. 329. (Amend. - SG, issue 95 of 1975) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, declared unconstitutional by DCC No. 13 of 2022 - SG, issue 79 of 2022) An adult able-bodied person who for a prolonged period of time does not engage in socially useful work, while receiving non-labor income in an impermissible or immoral manner, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for up to two years or probation. (2) (Amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, effective from 01.01.2005, amend. regarding entry into force - SG, issue 26 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2004, declared unconstitutional by DCC No. 13 of 2022 - SG, issue 79 of 2022) Whoever systematically engages in begging shall be punished with probation for a term of up to two years. Chapter Eleven. GENERALLY DANGEROUS CRIMES

### Section I

Crimes committed in a generally dangerous manner or with generally dangerous means
Art. 330. (1) Whoever sets fire to a building, inventory, goods, agricultural or other products, forest, machines, mine or other property of significant value shall be punished for arson with imprisonment from one to eight years.
(2) The punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years:
1. if the fire posed a danger to someone's life;
2. if there was a danger that it would spread to other properties such as those mentioned above;
3. if the burned property has historical, scientific or artistic value or if objects having historical, scientific or artistic value are kept in the burned premises;
4. (new - SG, issue 92 of 2002) if the arson was committed by a person acting on the order of or in execution of a decision of an organization or group under Art. 321a or an organized criminal group;
5. (new - SG, issue 26 of 2004) if the burned forest or other property of significant value is located in a protected territory;
6. (new - SG, issue 67 of 2023) if the arson was committed with racist or xenophobic motives.
(3) If in the cases under the preceding paragraph significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years, and if death has resulted to someone, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not allow this, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
Art. 331. (1) Whoever by negligence sets fire to another's property under the preceding article shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
(2) (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever sets fire to stubble, as a result of which causes a fire in the forest fund, shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years and a fine from five hundred to five thousand leva.
(3) (Former para. 2 - SG, issue 92 of 2002) If death or significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 332. For arson the perpetrator shall not be punished if on his own initiative he extinguished the fire immediately, before significant damages occurred.
Art. 333. When the objects under Art. 330 are damaged or destroyed by explosion, the punishments provided for in that article shall be imposed accordingly.
Art. 334. (1) Whoever causes a flood and thereby endangers the life or property of another shall be punished with imprisonment from three to twelve years.
(2) If in this case significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and if death has resulted to someone, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not allow this, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to fifteen years.
Art. 335. (1) Whoever causes a flood by negligence and thereby endangers the life or property of another shall be punished with imprisonment up to three years.
(2) If death or significant damages have resulted, the punishment is imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 336. (Repealed - SG, issue 41 of 1985)
Art. 336a. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever creates a danger to the life of another or for causing serious or medium bodily injury, or significant property damage, by violating the established rules:
1. uses an explosive substance, radioactive material, biological, chemical or nuclear weapon or other nuclear device in an aircraft against, or on a ship, or on a fixed platform located on the continental shelf;
2. releases, pours, discharges or unloads from an aircraft, from a ship or from a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, an explosive substance, radioactive material, biological, chemical or nuclear weapon or other nuclear device;
3. releases, pours, discharges or unloads from an aircraft, from a ship or from a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, oil, liquefied natural gas, dangerous or harmful substance, outside the cases under item 2;
4. uses an aircraft or ship,
shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever unlawfully transports on an aircraft or on a ship weapons, dangerous substance, material, information technology or other object provided for in an international treaty that has entered into force for the Republic of Bulgaria.
(3) When by an act under para. 1 and 2 serious or medium bodily injury has been caused to another, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years.
(4) When by an act under para. 1 and 2 death has been caused to another, the punishment is imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years or life imprisonment.
(5) In the cases under para. 1 - 4 the court shall also impose deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
Art. 336b. (New - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever with a terrorist purpose delivers, places, activates or detonates a substance or other explosive device or explosive materials in a place for public use, an object of the public transport system or an infrastructure object and from this significant damages, medium or serious bodily injury have resulted, shall be punished with imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and when death has been caused - with imprisonment from fifteen to thirty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without replacement.
Art. 337. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 41 of 1985, former text of Art. 337, amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011, supplemented - SG, issue 47 of 2016, supplemented - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever produces, processes, modifies, repairs, develops, researches, stores, trades, transports, imports or exports explosive substances, firearms, non-firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, ammunition and pyrotechnic products or destroys cluster munitions, anti-personnel mines and other products within the meaning of the Law on Implementation of the Convention on Cluster Munitions and the Convention on the Prohibition of the Use, Stockpiling, Production and Transfer of Anti-Personnel Mines and on Their Destruction, without having the right to do so by law or without a permit issued by the competent authority, when such is required, or performs this not in accordance with the permit given to him, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) The punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years, if the act was committed:
1. by an official who took advantage of his official position;
2. repeatedly, in not insignificant cases.
(3) (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) When the object of the crime is in large amounts, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
(4) (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) When the object of the crime is in especially large amounts and the case is especially serious, the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(5) (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) For preparation for a crime under para. 1 - 4 the punishment is imprisonment up to two years.
Art. 338. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever while holding, carrying, sending or working with explosive substances, firearms, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic products, does not take the necessary safety measures and especially the measures provided for in the proper regulations, ordinances or instructions, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to five years or with a fine from five hundred to three thousand leva.
(2) (New - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever provides explosive substances, weapons, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic products to a person who has not reached 18 years of age shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years and with a fine up to five thousand leva.
(3) (Former para. 2, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If from the acts under para. 1 and 2 have resulted causing to one or more persons medium or serious bodily injury, or death, or significant damage to property, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not allow this, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years, and in especially serious cases the punishment is imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(4) (New - SG, issue 47 of 2016) When the object of the crime is cluster munitions, anti-personnel mines and other products within the meaning of the Law on Implementation of the Convention on Cluster Munitions and the Convention on the Prohibition of the Use, Stockpiling, Production and Transfer of Anti-Personnel Mines and on Their Destruction, the punishment is:
1. in the cases under para. 1 - imprisonment from two to eight years;
2. in the cases under para. 2 - imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine up to ten thousand leva;
3. in the cases under para. 3 - imprisonment from five to twelve years, and in especially serious cases - imprisonment from six to fifteen years.
Art. 339. (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 41 of 1985, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 84 of 2023) Whoever acquires in any manner researches, holds or transfers to another explosive substances, firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic products, without having proper authorization for this, shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) If it concerns explosive substances, firearms, chemical, biological or nuclear weapons, ammunition for firearms or pyrotechnic products in large quantity, the punishment is imprisonment from three to ten years.
(3) Whoever alienates or transfers explosive substances, firearm, chemical, biological or nuclear weapon or pyrotechnic products to a person who does not have authorization for their acquisition shall be punished with imprisonment from two to eight years.
(4) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed on whoever alienates or transfers to another ammunition for a firearm, without the latter having authorization to carry the respective firearm.
(5) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who without appropriate authorization takes found explosive substances, firearm, chemical, biological or nuclear weapon, ammunition for a firearm or pyrotechnic products.
(6) (New - SG, issue 47 of 2016) When the object of the crime is cluster munitions, anti-personnel mines and other products within the meaning of the Law on Implementation of the Convention on Cluster Munitions and the Convention on the Prohibition of the Use, Stockpiling, Production and Transfer of Anti-Personnel Mines and on Their Destruction, the punishment is:
1. in the cases under para. 1 - imprisonment from three to ten years;
2. in the cases under para. 2 - imprisonment from five to twelve years;
3. in the cases under para. 3, 4 and 5 - imprisonment from three to ten years.
Art. 339a. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever without proper authorization, which is required by law, produces, uses, sells or holds special technical means intended for covert collection of information, shall be punished with imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) (Former para. 3, amended - SG, issue 27 of 2009, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) If the act under para. 1 was committed by an official in connection with his service, the punishment is imprisonment from two to eight years.
(3) (Former para. 2 - SG, issue 27 of 2009) The special technical means shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 339b. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amended - SG, issue 38 of 2007, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) Whoever produces, carries, holds, acquires or transfers to another dual-use items or technologies, defined by law or by an act of the Council of Ministers with the purpose that they be used for carrying out transactions in violation of prohibitions, restrictions or sanctions imposed by the Security Council of the United Nations, by the Organization for Security and Co-operation in Europe or by the European Union, or arising from an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, shall be punished with imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine up to two hundred thousand leva.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section II

Crimes against transport and communications
Art. 340. (1) (Am. - SG, issue 95 of 1975) Whoever damages rolling railway stock or railway, aircraft, automobile, electric transport vehicle (trolleybus, tram and other such intended for mass transport) or facilities or accessories thereto, tunnel, bridge or retaining wall along roads, or damages or allows to be damaged, run aground or sunk a ship and thereby creates danger to the life of another or for significant damage to another's property, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(2) (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975) Whoever destroys an aircraft in operation, or causes damage to it which renders it unfit for flight or is of a nature to endanger its safety in flight, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to twenty years.
(3) (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on whoever destroys a ship or causes damage to the ship or its cargo which is of a nature to endanger the safety of the ship during navigation, or destroys a fixed platform, including when located on the continental shelf, or causes damage to it which is of a nature to endanger its safety.
(4) (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on whoever, by using a device, substance or weapon, destroys or causes significant property damage to the facilities of an airport or to an aircraft located on its territory which is not in operation, or interrupts the activities of the airport services, where this endangers or is of a nature to endanger the safety of the airport.
(5) (Previous para. 2, am. - SG, issue 95 of 1975, previous para. 3 - SG, issue 101 of 2017) If in the cases under the preceding paragraphs there has resulted:
a) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from eight to fifteen years;
b) (suppl. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, am. - SG, issue 153 of 1998) death of one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under letter "a" have occurred, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 341. (Am. - SG, issue 95 of 1975, am. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) When the act under Art. 340, para. 1 - 4 has been committed by negligence and from it have resulted:
a) significant property damage;
b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons regardless of whether consequences under the preceding letter have occurred;
c) death of one or more persons regardless of whether consequences under letters "a" and "b" have occurred,
the punishment is: under letter "a" - imprisonment up to three years; under letter "b" - imprisonment up to six years; under letter "c" - imprisonment from one to ten years.
Art. 341a. (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975) (1) Whoever places in an aircraft a device or substance which can destroy it or cause damage to it which renders it unfit for flight or creates danger to its safety in flight, if not subject to more severe punishment, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years.
(2) Whoever endangers the safety of an aircraft in flight by:
a) destroying or damaging an installation or facility for air navigation control;
b) communicating information or giving a signal which he knows to be false, placing a false sign or removing or moving a sign intended to ensure traffic safety,
shall be punished by imprisonment from three to fifteen years.
(3) Whoever commits violence against a person located on board an aircraft in flight, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safety of that aircraft and does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(4) (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) The punishment under para. 3 shall also be imposed on whoever, by using a device, substance or weapon, commits violence against a person located on the territory of an airport, if the act is of a nature to endanger the health or life of those around and does not constitute a more serious crime.
(5) (Previous para. 4, am. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) When in the cases under the preceding paragraphs there has resulted medium or serious bodily injury or death of one or more persons, the punishments provided in Art. 340, para. 5 shall be imposed respectively.
(6) (Previous para. 5 - SG, issue 101 of 2017) When the act under para. 1 has been committed by negligence and consequences under Art. 341 have resulted, the punishments provided in that article shall be imposed respectively.
Art. 341b. (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975) (1) (Am. and suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) Whoever unlawfully seizes an aircraft located on the ground or in flight, or a ship located in the maritime spaces or in the inland waterways of the Republic of Bulgaria or on the high seas, or on a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, or establishes control over such means, shall be punished by imprisonment up to ten years.
(2) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed with force or threat, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years.
(3) (New - SG, issue 84 of 2023) The punishments under the preceding paragraphs shall also be imposed on whoever unlawfully seizes other means for public transport or carriage of goods.
(4) (Previous para. 3 - SG, issue 84 of 2023) If from the act under the preceding paragraphs there has resulted:
a) (suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) significant damage to the aircraft, to the ship or to the platform;
b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under the preceding letter have occurred;
c) (am. - SG, issue 153 of 1998, am. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) death of one or more persons regardless of whether the consequences under letters "a" and "b" have occurred,
the punishment is: under letters "a" and "b" - imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and under letter "c" - imprisonment from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment without commutation, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 - 10.
Art. 341c. (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, rep. - SG, issue 41 of 1985, new - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever places on a ship located in the exclusive economic zone of the Republic of Bulgaria or on the high seas a device or substance which can destroy it or damage it or damage the cargo of the ship, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safe navigation of the ship, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to twelve years.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever places on a fixed platform located on the continental shelf a device or substance which can destroy it or is of a nature to endanger its safety.
(3) Whoever endangers the safe navigation of a ship by:
1. destroying, damaging or disrupting the operation of maritime or river navigation facilities;
2. communicating information or giving a signal which he knows to be false, placing a false sign or removing or moving a sign intended to ensure traffic safety,
shall be punished by imprisonment from three to fifteen years.
(4) Whoever commits violence against a person located on board a ship, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safe navigation of the ship and does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to ten years.
(5) The punishment under para. 4 shall also be imposed on whoever commits violence against a person located on board a fixed platform located on the continental shelf, if the act is of a nature to endanger the safety of the platform and does not constitute a more serious crime.
(6) If in the cases under para. 1 - 5 there has resulted:
1. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from eight to fifteen years;
2. death of one or more persons, the punishment is imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
(7) When the act under para. 1 has been committed by negligence and there have resulted:
1. significant property damage;
2. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons regardless of whether consequences under point 1 have occurred;
3. death of one or more persons regardless of whether consequences under points 1 and 2 have occurred,
the punishment is:
under point 1 - imprisonment from five to ten years;
under point 2 - imprisonment from eight to fifteen years;
under point 3 - imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 342. (Am. - SG, issue 95 of 1975, am. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) (Am. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever while operating rolling railway stock, aircraft, motor vehicle, watercraft, combat or special machine violates the traffic rules, thereby allowing the causing of bodily injury or death to another, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years or probation.
(2) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) The same punishment shall be imposed also on a worker or employee in transport who violates the rules for operation or the requirements for good quality of repair of the rolling stock, of the roads or of the facilities, thereby allowing the causing of bodily injury or death to another.
(3) (Previous para. 2, am., - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) If by acts under the preceding paragraphs there have been intentionally caused death, bodily injury or significant property damage to another, the punishment is:
a) when causing significant property damage - imprisonment from one to ten years;
b) when causing medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without property damage - from three to twelve years;
c) (Am. - SG, issue 85 of 1998) when causing death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under letters "a" and "b" - imprisonment from ten to twenty years, and in particularly serious cases - from fifteen to twenty years or life imprisonment.
(4) (Previous para. 3, am. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 89 of 1986, am. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In the cases under paragraph 3, letters "a" and "b" the court shall deprive the guilty person of the rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7. In the cases under letter "c" the deprivation of these rights is for life.
Art. 343. (Am. - SG, issue 95 of 1975, am. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982) (1) When by acts under the preceding article by negligence there have been caused:
a) (am. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) significant property damage, the punishment is imprisonment up to one year or probation;
b) (am. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) serious or medium bodily injury, regardless of whether the consequences under letter "a" have occurred, the punishment is imprisonment up to four years for serious bodily injury and up to three years or probation for medium bodily injury;
c) (am. - SG, issue 60 of 2012, in force from 08.09.2012, am. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) death, regardless of whether the consequences under letter "a" have occurred, the punishment is imprisonment from three to eight years, and in particularly serious cases - imprisonment from five to twelve years.
(2) (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 86 of 2005, in force from 29.04.2006, am. - SG, issue 95 of 2016) The criminal proceedings shall be terminated if the injured person requests this:
1. for crimes under para. 1, letter "a";
2. for crimes under para. 1, letter "b", when criminal proceedings against the perpetrator have not been terminated on this ground during the last five years.
(3) (Suppl. - SG, issue 21 of 2000, previous para. 2 - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, suppl. - SG, issue 60 of 2012, in force from 08.09.2012, suppl. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) If the act has been committed in a state of intoxication or after consumption of narcotic substances or their analogues or the person without medical reasons refuses to be tested for alcohol and/or narcotic substances or their analogues or from it has resulted bodily injury or death of more than one person, or the perpetrator has fled from the scene of the incident, or has been operating without having the necessary qualification when such is required by law, as well as after exceeding the permitted speed in a populated area by more than 50 km/h or if the act has been committed after passing a red signal of the traffic light, or on a pedestrian crossing the punishment is:
a) (am. - SG, issue 60 of 2012, in force from 08.09.2012, am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) for serious or medium bodily injury - imprisonment from one to six years, and in particularly serious cases - imprisonment from two to ten years;
b) (am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015, am. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) for death - imprisonment from five to fifteen years, and in particularly serious cases - imprisonment from ten to twenty years.
(4) (Previous para. 3 - SG, issue 92 of 2002) The punishment under letter "b" of the preceding paragraph shall be imposed also when there has occurred death of one or more persons and bodily injury to one or more persons.
(5) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) In the cases under para. 3 and 4 the court may confiscate in favour of the state the aircraft, motor vehicle, watercraft or special machine which has served for the commission of the crime and is property of the perpetrator, and when the perpetrator is not the owner - to award its equivalent value.
Art. 343a. (1) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, previous text of Art. 343a - SG, issue 92 of 2002) If the perpetrator after the act under the preceding article has done everything depending on him to provide assistance to the injured person or to the injured persons, the punishment is:
a) (am. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) under para. 1, letter "b" - imprisonment up to three years for serious bodily injury and imprisonment up to two years or probation for medium bodily injury;
b) under para. 1, letter "c" - imprisonment up to four years;
c) under para. 2, letter "a", when medium or serious bodily injury has been caused to more than one person - imprisonment up to four years, and in particularly serious cases - up to six years;
d) under para. 2, letter "b", when death of more than one person has occurred - imprisonment from two to ten years, and in particularly serious cases - from three to twelve years.
(2) (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 86 of 2005, in force from 29.04.2006) For a crime under para. 1, letter "a" the criminal proceedings shall be terminated if the injured person requests this.
Art. 343b. (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever operates a motor vehicle with a concentration of alcohol in his blood above 1.2 per thousand, established in the proper manner, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from two hundred to one thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever operates a motor vehicle with a concentration of alcohol in his blood above 0.5 per thousand, established in the proper manner, after having been convicted by a final judgment for the act under para. 1, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand five hundred leva.
(3) (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015, suppl. - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever operates a motor vehicle after consumption of narcotic substances or their analogues, established in the proper manner, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand five hundred leva.
(4) (New - SG, issue 74 of 2015) If the act under para. 3 has been committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand five hundred leva.
(5) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) In the cases under para. 1 - 4 the court shall confiscate in favour of the state the motor vehicle which has served for the commission of the crime and is property of the perpetrator, and when the perpetrator is not the owner - to award its equivalent value.
(6) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) Whoever operates a motor vehicle and refuses to undergo a check in the proper manner for establishing the consumption of alcohol and/or narcotic substances or their analogues, after having been punished for any of these acts by administrative procedure, shall be punished by imprisonment up to six months and a fine of two thousand leva.
(7) (New - SG, issue 67 of 2023) The perpetrator shall not be punished if the refusal under para. 6 is due to reasons beyond his control.
Art. 343c. (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995) (1) (Am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever operates a motor vehicle during the term of serving the punishment deprivation of the right to operate a motor vehicle, after having been punished for the same act by administrative procedure, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years and a fine from two hundred to one thousand leva.
(2) (Am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever within a one-year period from his punishment by administrative procedure for operating a motor vehicle without the corresponding driving licence commits such an act, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to three years and a fine from five hundred to one thousand two hundred leva.
(3) (New - SG, issue 95 of 2016) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on a person who operates a motor vehicle during the term of serving a compulsory administrative measure for temporary withdrawal of the driving licence for a motor vehicle.
Art. 343d. (New - SG, issue 50 of 1995, am. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In all cases under Art. 343, 343a, 343b and 343c, para. 1 the court shall order also deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, point 7 and may order deprivation of the right under point 6.
Art. 344. (1) (Am. - SG, issue 95 of 1975, am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) Whoever removes or moves a sign or signal intended to ensure the safety of traffic of railway and water transport and electric transport, places such a false sign or gives a false signal and thereby exposes to danger the life or property of someone, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to seven years.
(2) (Am. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 74 of 2015) For the act under para. 1 affecting signs of automobile transport, the punishment is imprisonment up to two years or a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
Art. 345. (Am. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 95 of 2016) (1) Whoever uses a plate with a registration number issued for another motor vehicle, or a plate not issued by the respective authorities, shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year or a fine from five hundred to one thousand leva.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on whoever operates a motor vehicle which is not registered in the proper manner.
Art. 345a. (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000) (1) Whoever in violation of the established procedure for this erases or falsifies an identification number of a motor vehicle, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
(3) The punishment is imprisonment up to three years or a fine up to three thousand leva, if numbers on parts of a motor vehicle have been erased or falsified.
Art. 346. (1) (Am. - SG, issue 107 of 1996, am. - SG, issue 62 of 1997) Whoever unlawfully takes another's motor vehicle from the possession of another without his consent with the intention to use it, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years.
(2) (Am. - SG, issue 89 of 1986, corr. - SG, issue 90 of 1986, am. - SG, issue 107 of 1996, am. - SG, issue 62 of 1997) The punishment is imprisonment from one to ten years, if:
1. there has resulted damage to the vehicle or it has been abandoned without supervision, or
2. the act has been committed in a state of intoxication or more than twice, or repeatedly, or
3. the taking has been committed under the conditions of Art. 195, para. 1, points 1 - 6;
4. (new - SG, issue 26 of 2004) the taking has been committed with the purpose of property gain from the return of the motor vehicle.
(3) (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on a person who offers assistance for the return of the taken vehicle in exchange for receiving property gain.
(4) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 62 of 1997, previous para. 3 - SG, issue 26 of 2004) In the cases under the preceding paragraphs the court shall order deprivation of the right to operate a motor vehicle.
(5) (Previous para. 3 - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 62 of 1997, previous para. 4 - SG, issue 26 of 2004) If for the taking of the vehicle or for preserving possession over it force or threat has been used, the punishment is imprisonment from three to twelve years and deprivation of the right to operate a motor vehicle, and the court shall order also confiscation of not less than 1/2 of the property of the perpetrator.
(6) (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, previous para. 5 - SG, issue 26 of 2004) The punishment under the preceding paragraph shall be imposed also when the act has been committed by a person specified in Art. 142, para. 2, points 6 and 8, or by order of an organization or group, or when an attempt has been made to take the motor vehicle across the border of the country or when its serial and registration numbers have been altered.
Art. 346a. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) If until the conclusion of the judicial investigation in the first instance court the taken motor vehicle is returned, the punishment is:
1. in the cases under Art. 346, para. 1 - imprisonment up to five years;
2. in the cases under Art. 346, para. 2, point 4, if the return has been made before receiving the property gain - imprisonment up to eight years.
Art. 346b. (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, previous text of Art. 346a - SG, issue 26 of 2004, am. - SG, issue 7 of 2019) Whoever unlawfully enters another's motor vehicle without the consent of the owner, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.
Art. 347. (1) Whoever damages telegraph, telephone or telex equipment or line, television or radio equipment or electrification equipment or line and thereby interrupts or obstructs communications, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years.
(2) (Am. - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, am. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, am. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) If the act has been committed by negligence, the punishment is imprisonment up to one year or probation, or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 348. (Am. - SG, issue 10 of 1993) Whoever:
a) constructs, holds or uses radio equipment which transmits into the ether, without having written permission for this;
b) uses radio equipment which transmits into the ether, without having previously registered it with full identification data or uses it for purposes not permitted by the permission given to him;
c) without prior written permission arbitrarily alters the registered data of the radio equipment which transmits into the ether;
d) (am. - SG, issue 92 of 2002) obstructs or interferes with the operation of a radio communication, radio broadcasting, television or radio relay station or radio transmission centre, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and the radio equipment shall be confiscated in favour of the state.
Art. 348a. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) (1) Whoever by fraud or other unlawful means uses a telecommunications network, facility or service to generate or redirect in his own or another's interest directed transmission of signals, written text, images, sound, data or messages of any kind via conductor, radio waves, optical or other transmission medium, shall be punished by imprisonment up to six years and a fine up to ten thousand leva.
(2) When the act under para. 1 has been committed:
1. by two or more persons who have conspired in advance for its commission, when it does not constitute a minor case;
2. by using an unregistered telecommunications device;
3. repeatedly,
the punishment is imprisonment up to eight years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(3) (Am. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) In minor cases under para. 1 the punishment is imprisonment up to one year or probation.
Art. 348b. (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, previous text of Art. 348a - SG, issue 26 of 2004) When the property damage and bodily injury under Art. 343 have been caused to a spouse, ascendant, descendant, brother or sister, the criminal prosecution shall be initiated by complaint of the injured person. In these cases the perpetrator may be deprived by administrative procedure of the right to operate a motor vehicle even when a complaint has not been filed or the filed complaint has been withdrawn.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section III

Crimes against public health and against the environment (Title amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004)
Art. 349. (1) Whoever intentionally places or mixes a substance dangerous to life or health in a well, spring, water supply or other facility intended for common use, from which or with which drinking water is drawn, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to eight years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) If the act has resulted in serious bodily injury, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years, and if death has resulted, the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 41 of 1985) In accordance with the differences in the preceding paragraphs, the person who, with the purpose of infecting people, spreads causative agents of an epidemic disease shall also be punished.
Art. 349a. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997) (1) Whoever violates rules established for the taking and provision of human organs or tissues for transplantation shall be punished by imprisonment from one to three years.
(2) The punishment shall be imprisonment from three to five years if the act has been committed for profit.
Art. 350. (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004) (1) Whoever prepares food or beverages intended for common use in such a way that substances dangerous to health are created or get into them, as well as the person who sells, offers for sale or otherwise puts into circulation such food or beverages, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years.
(2) Whoever violates rules regulating the extraction, production, processing, storage or trade of animals, raw materials, food or beverages intended for common use, and thereby endangers the health or life of another, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.
(3) If the act under para. 1 and 2 has resulted in medium bodily injury to another - the punishment shall be imprisonment up to six years, if serious bodily injury to another has resulted - imprisonment from one to eight years, and if death has resulted - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.
Art. 350a. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) Whoever in violation of the law produces or places on the market food, animal feed or veterinary medicinal products, or beverages, and thereby endangers the life or health of another, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.
Art. 351. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) For acts under Art. 349 and 350, committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to two years or probation.
(2) If in this case death has resulted for someone, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to five years.
Art. 352. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991, amended - SG, issue 85 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever pollutes or allows to be polluted the soil, air, water streams, basins, underground waters, territorial or internal sea waters or sea waters in zones defined by an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates, and thereby makes them dangerous for people or animals and plants or unfit for their use for cultural-domestic, health, agricultural and other economic purposes, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004) The same punishment shall be imposed on an official who has allowed, in the design, construction or operation of drainage or irrigation systems, the necessary measures not to be taken to prevent dangerous pollution of the water-supplying zones for drinking water supply or the raising of the level of underground waters in populated and resort places.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) When the acts under para. 1 or 2 have caused:
1. death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva;
2. non-negligible harm to the environment, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(4) (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) If the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.
Art. 352a. (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 85 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever pollutes or allows to be polluted with petroleum products or derivatives territorial or internal sea waters or sea waters in zones defined by an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva. When the act has been committed by a ship's captain, the court shall also order deprivation of the right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 7.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed on the person who pollutes or allows to be polluted the waters under para. 1 with harmful liquid substances in bulk, defined in an international agreement in which the Republic of Bulgaria participates.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) When the act under para. 1 or 2 has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years and a fine from two thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
(4) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993) A ship's captain or other vessel's captain who does not immediately report to the nearest port the discharge into waters specified in para. 1 of petroleum products or derivatives, or of other substances dangerous to people, animals or plants, shall be punished by a fine up to five hundred leva.
(5) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) A captain or other person from the command staff of a ship who does not fulfill the obligation to record in the ship's documents an operation with substances dangerous to people, animals or plants, or enters false information in them about such operations, or refuses to present these documents to the relevant officials, shall be punished by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
Art. 353. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 86 of 1991, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) An official who puts into operation or orders to be put into operation an enterprise or thermal power plant before the necessary purification facilities have been put into operation, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on an official who does not fulfill his obligations for the construction of purification facilities, as well as for ensuring the serviceability and continuous proper operation of such facilities, as a result of which they could not come into operation fully or partially or have ceased their operation.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) If the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(4) (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002) In minor cases the punishment shall be: under para. 1 and 2 - a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, and under para. 3 - a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, imposed by administrative procedure.
Art. 353a. (New - SG, issue 86 of 1991, amended - SG, issue 85 of 1997) An official who, within the scope of his official duties, conceals or discloses false information about the state of the environment and its components - air, water, soil, sea spaces - and this results in non-negligible harm to the environment, life and human health, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years and a fine from one hundred to one thousand leva.
Art. 353b. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever manages waste not in the established manner and thereby creates danger to the life or health of another or for causing non-negligible harm to the environment, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(2) When the act under para. 1 has caused:
1. death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva;
2. non-negligible harm to the environment, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(3) Whoever violates or does not fulfill his obligations to ensure the serviceability and proper functioning of an installation or facility for disposal or recovery of waste and thereby causes death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to twenty years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva, and if non-negligible harm to the environment has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(4) When the acts under para. 1 - 3 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years and a fine from two thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
Art. 353v. (New - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever manages hazardous waste not in the established manner, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years and a fine from two thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(2) When the act under para. 1 creates danger to the life or health of another or for causing non-negligible harm to the environment, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(3) When the act under para. 1 has caused death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years and a fine from fifteen thousand to fifty thousand leva, and if non-negligible harm to the environment has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva.
(4) An official who violates or does not fulfill his obligations for the management of hazardous waste shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years.
(5) When the acts under para. 1 - 3 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years and a fine from three thousand to twenty thousand leva.
Art. 353g. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever in violation of the established manner transports waste across the border of the country, when the act committed does not constitute a minor case, shall be punished by imprisonment up to four years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) Whoever in violation of international treaties to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party transports across the border of the country hazardous waste, toxic chemical substances, biological agents, toxins and radioactive substances, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) When the acts under para. 1 or 2 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to two years or probation.
Art. 353d. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever stores not in the established manner hazardous substances or mixtures and thereby creates danger to the life or health of another or for causing non-negligible harm to the environment, shall be punished by imprisonment up to four years and a fine from two thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) Whoever not in the established manner puts into operation or orders to be put into operation an enterprise or installation for the functioning of which hazardous substances or mixtures are used, and thereby creates danger to the life or health of another or for causing non-negligible harm to the environment, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to five years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on the person who not in the established manner puts into operation or orders to be put into operation an enterprise or installation whose functioning may create danger to the life or health of another or for causing non-negligible harm to the environment.
(4) If in the cases under para. 2 and 3 death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from eight to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to thirty thousand leva, and if non-negligible harm to the environment has been caused, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from fifteen thousand to thirty thousand leva.
(5) When the acts under para. 1 - 4 have been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to two years or probation.
Art. 353e. (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) (1) Whoever in violation of the established manner produces, uses, distributes, imports or exports across the border of the country substances that deplete the ozone layer, shall be punished by imprisonment up to four years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(2) When the act under para. 1 has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to one year or probation.
Art. 353zh. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004, previous Art. 353g - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever in violation of the law constructs a water intake facility or facility for the use of surface or underground waters, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years and a fine from five thousand to fifteen thousand leva.
Art. 353z. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004, previous Art. 353d - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) Whoever in violation of the law uses mineral water for economic activity, shall be punished by imprisonment up to one year and a fine up to five thousand leva.
Art. 354. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever without proper authorization produces, acquires, possesses, alienates or transfers to another a strongly acting or poisonous substance that is not a narcotic substance placed under a permit regime, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) If the crime under para. 1 is committed systematically, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to three years and a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(3) The object of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
(4) (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever violates rules established for the production, acquisition, storage, accounting, dispensing, transport or carrying of substances under para. 1, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years or probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 354a. (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) (1) Whoever without proper authorization produces, processes, acquires or possesses narcotic substances or their analogues with the purpose of distribution, or distributes narcotic substances or their analogues, shall be punished for high-risk narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment from two to eight years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva, and for risky narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva. When the object of the crime is precursors or equipment or materials for the production of narcotic substances or their analogues, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 26 of 2010) When the narcotic substances or their analogues are in large quantities, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and a fine from ten thousand to fifty thousand leva. Whoever without proper authorization in a public place acquires or possesses with the purpose of distribution, or distributes narcotic substances or their analogues, as well as when the narcotic substances or their analogues are in particularly large quantities or the act has been committed:
1. by a person acting on the order or in execution of a decision of an organized criminal group;
2. by a doctor or pharmacist;
3. by an educator, teacher, head of an educational institution or official at or on the occasion of the performance of his service;
4. under the conditions of dangerous recidivism,
the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years and a fine from twenty thousand to one hundred thousand leva.
(3) Whoever without proper authorization acquires or possesses narcotic substances or their analogues, shall be punished:
1. for high-risk narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment from one to six years and a fine from two thousand to ten thousand leva;
2. for risky narcotic substances or their analogues - by imprisonment up to five years and a fine from one thousand to five thousand leva.
(4) Whoever violates rules established for the production, acquisition, storage, accounting, dispensing, transport or carrying of narcotic substances, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years and a fine up to five thousand leva, and the court may also order deprivation of the rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
(5) In minor cases under para. 3 and 4 the punishment shall be a fine up to one thousand leva.
(6) In the cases under para. 1 - 5 the object and means of the crime shall be confiscated in favor of the state.
Art. 354b. (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever incites or assists another to use narcotic substances or their analogues, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to eight years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000) When the act under para. 1 has been committed:
1. with respect to a minor, juvenile or insane person;
2. with respect to more than two persons;
3. (supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) by a doctor, pharmacist, educator, teacher, head of an educational institution or official in places of deprivation of liberty at or on the occasion of the performance of his service;
4. (amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) in a public place;
5. (amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006, amended - SG, issue 67 of 2023) through the mass media;
6. (amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) under the conditions of dangerous recidivism,
the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from twenty thousand to fifty thousand leva, and the court in the cases of item 3 shall order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
(3) (New - SG, issue 21 of 2000, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever gives to another a narcotic substance or its analogue in quantities that may cause death and such results, shall be punished by imprisonment from fifteen to twenty years and a fine from one hundred thousand to three hundred thousand leva.
(4) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, previous para. 3 - SG, issue 21 of 2000, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever systematically provides premises to different persons for the use of narcotic substances or organizes the use of such substances, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to ten years and a fine from five thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(5) (Amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, previous para. 4 - SG, issue 21 of 2000, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) A doctor who in violation of the established manner knowingly prescribes to another narcotic substances or their analogues or medicines that contain such substances, shall be punished by imprisonment up to five years and a fine up to three thousand leva, and the court may also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
(6) (Previous para. 5 - SG, issue 21 of 2000, amended - SG, issue 26 of 2004) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years, a fine up to five thousand leva and deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, items 6 and 7.
Art. 354v. (New - SG, issue 95 of 1975, amended - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended - SG, issue 21 of 2000) (1) (Amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever sows or cultivates plants of opium poppy and coca bush or plants of the genus of cannabis in violation of the rules established in the Law on Control of Narcotic Substances and Precursors, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to five years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever organizes, leads or finances an organized criminal group for the cultivation of plants under para. 1 or for the extraction, production or processing of narcotic substances, shall be punished by imprisonment from ten to twenty years and a fine from fifty thousand to two hundred thousand leva.
(3) (Supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) Whoever participates in an organized criminal group under the preceding paragraph, shall be punished by imprisonment from three to ten years and a fine from five thousand to ten thousand leva.
(4) (Supplemented - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) A participant in an organized criminal group who has voluntarily reported to the authorities all facts and circumstances known to him about the activity of the criminal group shall not be punished.
(5) In minor cases under para. 1 the punishment shall be imprisonment up to one year and a fine up to one thousand leva.
Art. 355. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 23 of 2020, amended - SG, issue 28 of 2020, effective from 24.03.2020) Whoever violates an ordinance, rules or measures issued against the spread or occurrence of a contagious disease in humans, shall be punished by imprisonment up to three years and a fine from one thousand to ten thousand leva.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005, amended - SG, issue 23 of 2020) If the act has been committed during an epidemic, pandemic or state of emergency related to deaths, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to five years and a fine from ten to fifty thousand leva.
(3) (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, amended - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amended - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) Whoever violates an ordinance issued for the prevention of food poisoning, shall be punished by probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 356. (1) Whoever violates the established construction, sanitary or fire safety rules in the design, management or execution of construction works and thereby endangers the life of another, shall be punished by imprisonment up to two years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 103 of 2004, effective from 01.01.2005) If the violation of the rules under the preceding paragraph has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment up to one year or probation.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Раздел IV

Other Generally Dangerous Crimes (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985)
Art. 356a. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985, amend. - SG, issue 21 of 2000, suppl. - SG, issue 26 of 2004, amend. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, effective from 27.05.2011) For preparation for a crime under Art. 330, 333, 334, 340, 341a, 341b, Art. 342, para. 3, Art. 344, 349, 350, Art. 352, para. 1, Art. 353zh, 353z, Art. 354, 354a and 354v, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to eight years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.
Art. 356b. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985) (1) A foreign citizen who on the territory of the republic prepares to commit abroad an act under Art. 242, para. 2 and 3 or any of the acts specified in Art. 356a, shall be punished by imprisonment of up to five years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.
(2) If an organization or group has been created for the same purpose, the punishment shall be imprisonment from one to six years, and for the organizers and leaders - imprisonment from three to eight years, but not more than the punishment provided for the respective crime.
Art. 356v. (New - SG, issue 41 of 1985, repealed - SG, issue 21 of 2000)

### Section V

Crimes in the Use of Atomic Energy for Peaceful Purposes (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985, corr. - SG, issue 80 of 1985)
Art. 356g. (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) An official who orders or permits the commencement or performance of an activity without a permit provided for in the Law on the Use of Atomic Energy for Peaceful Purposes being issued or before such permit is issued, or in deviation from the permit, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to two years or by probation or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
(2) If the act under the preceding paragraph has been committed repeatedly or has created an immediate danger to the life or health of another, the punishment shall be imprisonment for up to three years.
Art. 356d. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985, amend. - SG, issue 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, issue 92 of 2002, amend. - SG, issue 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) An official who appoints or permits a person without the necessary qualification to work with nuclear material, nuclear facilities or other sources of ionizing radiation shall be punished by imprisonment for up to one year or by probation or by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 356e. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) (1) (Amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) Whoever destroys or damages a nuclear facility, nuclear material or a source of ionizing radiation and thereby creates a danger to the life or health of another, to damage to the environment or to the occurrence of significant property damage, shall be punished by imprisonment from five to fifteen years.
(2) If in the cases under the preceding paragraph there has followed:
a) (new - SG, issue 101 of 2017) damage to the environment or significant property damage, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years;
b) (former item "a" - SG, issue 101 of 2017) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, the punishment shall be imprisonment from eight to fifteen years;
c) (suppl. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, issue 153 of 1998, former item "b" - SG, issue 101 of 2017) death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under item "a", the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 356zh. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) When by the act under the preceding article by negligence there have been caused:
a) significant property damage;
b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under item "a";
c) death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under items "a" and "b",
the punishment shall be: under item "a" - imprisonment for up to five years; under item "b" - imprisonment for up to eight years; under item "c" - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.
Art. 356z. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) (1) Whoever violates the rules for nuclear or radiation safety, admitting that bodily injury or death of another may follow, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to three years.
(2) Whoever violates the rules for nuclear or radiation safety and intentionally causes significant property damage, bodily injury or death of another shall be punished:
a) for causing significant property damage - by imprisonment from five to fifteen years;
b) for causing medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under item "a" - by imprisonment from five to twenty years;
c) (suppl. - SG, issue 50 of 1995, amend. - SG, issue 153 of 1998) for causing death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under items "a" and "b" - by imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
Art. 356i. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985) When by an act under the preceding article by negligence there have been caused:
a) significant property damage;
b) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under item "a";
c) death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under items "a" and "b",
the punishment shall be: under item "a" - imprisonment for up to five years; under item "b" - imprisonment for up to eight years; under item "c" - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.
Art. 356y. (New - SG, issue 79 of 1985, amend. - SG, issue 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) In the cases under Art. 356e, para. 2, Art. 356zh, items "b" and "c", Art. 356z, para. 2 and Art. 356i, items "b" and "c", the court shall deprive the guilty person of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6 and 7.
Art. 356k. (New - SG, issue 26 of 2004) (1) (Suppl. - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) Whoever without proper authorization produces, processes, modifies, neutralizes, receives, acquires in any manner, possesses, uses, disperses, destroys, buries, transports, carries, moves, sends or transfers to another nuclear material or sources of ionizing radiation or components therefor, shall be punished by imprisonment from one to six years.
(2) If in the cases under para. 1 there has followed:
1. (new - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) danger of causing damage to the environment or significant property damage, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years;
2. (new - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, suppl. - SG, issue 101 of 2017) damage to the environment or significant property damage, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years;
3. (former point 1 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) danger to the life or health of another, the punishment shall be imprisonment from two to eight years;
4. (former point 2 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not admit this, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to ten years;
5. (former point 3 - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) death of one or more persons, when the perpetrator did not intend and did not admit this, the punishment shall be imprisonment from eight to fifteen years and a fine from ten thousand to twenty thousand leva.
(3) (New - SG, issue 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) When the act under para. 1 has been committed by negligence, the punishment shall be imprisonment for up to three years.
Art. 356l.
