(New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever unlawfully commits an act directed against a nuclear facility or constituting interference with the operation of a nuclear facility, intending or admitting that death, serious or medium bodily injury, significant property damage or damage to the environment may follow, through exposure to radioactive radiation or release of radioactive substances, shall be punished by imprisonment for up to five years.
(2) If by the act under para. 1 there have been caused:
1. significant property damage or damage to the environment, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to fifteen years;
2. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under point 1, the punishment shall be imprisonment from five to twenty years;
3. death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under point 1, the punishment shall be imprisonment from ten to twenty years, life imprisonment or life imprisonment without commutation.
(3) When by an act under para. 2 by negligence there have been caused:
1. significant property damage or damage to the environment;
2. medium or serious bodily injury to one or more persons with or without the consequences under point 1;
3. death of one or more persons with or without the consequences under points 1 and 2,
the punishment shall be:
under point 1 - imprisonment for up to five years;
under point 2 - imprisonment for up to eight years;
under point 3 - imprisonment from three to fifteen years.
Art. 356m. (New - SG, issue 101 of 2017) (1) Whoever by use of force or threat demands that nuclear material or another source of ionizing radiation be provided to him, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by imprisonment from two to ten years and confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.
(2) If in the cases of para. 1 the perpetrator threatens that:
1. he will use nuclear material or another source of ionizing radiation to:
a) cause death, serious or medium bodily injury;
b) cause significant property damage or damage to the environment;
2. he will take nuclear material by theft or robbery;
3. he will commit a crime under Art. 356l, in order to compel another to perform, omit or endure something contrary to his will,
the punishment shall be imprisonment from three to twelve years and confiscation of up to one half of the property of the guilty person.
Chapter Twelve.
CRIMES AGAINST THE DEFENSE CAPABILITY OF THE REPUBLIC, AGAINST INFORMATION CONSTITUTING A STATE SECRET, AND AGAINST FOREIGN CLASSIFIED INFORMATION (TITLE AMEND. - SG, ISSUE 26 OF 2004)


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Раздел I

Crimes against information constituting a state secret and against foreign classified information (Title amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2004)
Art. 357. (1) (Repealed, former para. 2, amd. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) Whoever discloses information constituting a state secret which has been entrusted to him or has become known to him by service or work, as well as whoever discloses such information being aware that harm to the interests of the Republic of Bulgaria may result therefrom, if not subject to a more severe punishment, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.
(2) (Former para. 3 - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2010) If particularly severe consequences for the security of the state have occurred or may occur from the act, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
(3) (New - SG, No. 26 of 2004) The punishment under para. 1 and 2 shall also be imposed on whoever discloses foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party.
Art. 357а. (New - SG, No. 10 of 1993, repealed - SG, No. 26 of 2004)
Art. 357б. (New - SG, No. 41 of 2001, repealed - SG, No. 26 of 2004)
Art. 358. (1) (Amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amd. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever loses documents, publications or materials containing information constituting a state secret, or foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to two years or by probation.
(2) If particularly severe consequences have occurred or may occur from the act, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty up to five years.
Art. 359. (1) (Amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2004, amd. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever causes information constituting a state secret, or foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, to be disclosed through negligence, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to two years or by probation.
(2) (Amd. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) If this has occurred due to non-compliance with the normatively established requirements for protection of classified information, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty up to three years.
Art. 360. (Amd. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) Whoever discloses information of a military, economic or other nature which is not a state secret, but the disclosure of which is prohibited by law, order or other administrative instruction, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to one year or probation.

### Раздел II

Crimes against performance of military service
Art. 361. (1) (Repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
(2) (Amd. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, amd. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, repealed - SG, No. 20 of 2012, in force from 10.06.2012)
Art. 362. (Repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 363. (Repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 364. (1) Whoever evades military service during mobilization shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years.
(2) (Repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
(3) (Amd. - SG, No. 153 of 1998, amd. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007) If the act under para. 1 is committed in wartime, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 365. (Amd. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 366. (Amd. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG, No. 20 of 2012, in force from 10.06.2012) Whoever violates his obligations under the military registration of Bulgarian citizens and equipment outside the cases provided for in the special laws on military registration shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to two years and by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, and in wartime - by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years and by a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 367. (1) Whoever fails to comply with orders related to the preparation and conduct of mobilization, or obstructs its normal conduct, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years, and in particularly serious cases - by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
(2) (Amd. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amd. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amd. - SG, No. 92 of 2002, amd. - SG, No. 103 of 2004, in force from 01.01.2005) If the act under the preceding paragraph is committed through negligence, the punishment shall be probation or a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva.
Art. 368. (Amd. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amd. - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. and suppl. - SG, No. 20 of 2012, in force from 10.06.2012) Whoever knowingly violates his obligations in the medical examination of military personnel, reservists and reserves, or in the inspection of equipment in connection with military registration, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to eight years, and in wartime - by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.

### Раздел II "а"

Crimes against performance of peacetime alternative service (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 368а. (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 368б. (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 368в. (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 368г. (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 368д. (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 368е. (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)
Art. 368ж. (New - SG, No. 132 of 1998, repealed - SG, No. 38 of 2007, in force from 01.12.2007, amd. regarding entry into force - SG, No. 89 of 2007)

### Section III

Other Crimes
Art. 369. (1) Whoever during wartime fails to fulfill their obligations in connection with anti-aircraft, anti-atomic and anti-chemical defense shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.
(2) If serious consequences have resulted from the act, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to eight years.
Art. 370. Whoever during wartime as a labor mobilized person absconds or fails to fulfill their obligations in connection with the work assigned to them shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
Chapter Thirteen.
MILITARY CRIMES
Art. 371. (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) The following persons shall bear responsibility for crimes under this chapter:
a) servicemembers under the Defense and Armed Forces of the Republic of Bulgaria Act;
b) generals, officers and persons from the sergeant and enlisted ranks from other ministries and departments;
c) (amend. - SG, No. 27 of 2009, amend. - SG, No. 53 of 2014) civil servants in the Ministry of Internal Affairs, when committed in wartime or in combat conditions or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country or in connection with combat operations;
d) (amend. - SG, No. 20 of 2012, effective 10.06.2012) reservists in the performance of active service in the voluntary reserve and persons in wartime service;
e) persons not mentioned in this article for complicity in crimes under this chapter.

### Section I

Crimes Against Subordination and Military Honor
Art. 372. (1) Whoever fails to execute or refuses to execute an order of their superior shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.
(2) If this is committed by a group of persons or before formation, or in a demonstrative manner, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to five years.
(3) For crimes under the preceding paragraphs in especially serious cases, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
Art. 373. The punishment under the respective paragraphs of the preceding article shall also be imposed for failure to execute or refusal to execute a lawful demand of a military official performing military service duties.
Art. 374. Whoever openly expresses dissatisfaction against an order or command of their superior shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.
Art. 375. Whoever threatens their superior or a military official performing military service duties with bodily injury or with murder shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
Art. 376. (1) Whoever resists their superior or a military official performing military service duties shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on one who compels such person to violate their duties.
(3) If the act under the preceding paragraphs is committed with a weapon or by a group of persons, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from two to eight years, and in especially serious cases - deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years.
Art. 377. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever commits an act of violence against their superior, as well as against a military official during or on the occasion of performing military service duties, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to ten years.
(2) If serious consequences have resulted from the act, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years.
Art. 378. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Whoever insults or slanders a person specified in Art. 371, letters "a", "b", "c" and "d" shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year and public reprimand.
(2) When the act under the preceding paragraph is committed by a subordinate against a superior, by a superior against a subordinate, as well as by a military official or against a military official during or on the occasion of performing military service duties, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years and public reprimand.
(3) The punishment under para. 2 shall also be imposed on one who in the absence of their superior says or does something of a nature to impair their honor or dignity.
Art. 379. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Whoever causes light bodily injury to a person specified in Art. 371, letters "a", "b", "c" and "d", if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.
(2) For causing light bodily injury by a superior to a subordinate, by a military official during or on the occasion of performing military service duties or to more than one person, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
Art. 379a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever commits a violent or indecent act grossly violating military order and expressing manifest disrespect for the military honor and dignity of a servicemember, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years.

### Section II

Absence from Military Service
Art. 380. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) Whoever without permission leaves their unit or place of service or fails to report for service upon assignment, transfer, secondment, from leave, medical or correctional facility for a period from one to three days shall be punished for absence from military service by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.
(2) The punishment under the preceding paragraph shall also be imposed on one who absents themselves for less than one day, if the act is committed systematically, in a group or if the person has been convicted of absence from military service.
Art. 381. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever absents themselves from military service for more than three days or if the act under para. 1 of the preceding article is committed systematically, repeatedly or in a group, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
Art. 382. Whoever absents themselves with the intention to permanently avoid fulfillment of their military service obligation shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
Art. 383. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever absents themselves from military service duties by simulating illness, falsifying a document or by another fraudulent means, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years.
(2) Whoever absents themselves from military service duties by causing health disorder shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
(3) If the act under the preceding paragraphs constitutes an especially serious case, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
Art. 384. (Repealed - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982)
Art. 385. Whoever refuses to perform military service duties or systematically avoids their performance shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
Art. 386. (1) (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986) Whoever absents themselves from military service with the intention to leave the territory of the country without permission of the competent authorities shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
(2) (Former para. 1 - SG, No. 89 of 1986) Whoever absents themselves from military service by leaving the territory of the country without permission of the competent authorities or fails to return to it within the specified time, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(3) (Former para. 2, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) For preparation under the preceding paragraphs, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

### Section III

Official Crimes
Art. 387. (1) Whoever abuses power or official position, fails to perform official duties or exceeds authority and harmful consequences result therefrom, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) If serious consequences have resulted from the act or it is committed systematically by a superior towards a subordinate, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
(3) (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) If the act under the preceding paragraphs has been committed with the purpose that the perpetrator obtain for himself or for another a property benefit or cause harm to another, the punishment shall be: deprivation of liberty from one to five years under para. 1, from three to eight years under para. 2, and in particularly serious cases - from three to ten years, and the court shall also impose deprivation of right under Art. 37, para. 1, item 6.
(4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, former para. 3 - No. 89 of 1986) When the harmful consequences have been caused by negligence, the punishment shall be: under para. 1 - deprivation of liberty for up to two years, under para. 2 - deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
Art. 388. (1) (Former text of Art. 388, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) Whoever by negligence violates or fails to perform official duties and harmful consequences result therefrom, if the act committed does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to two years.
(2) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) If serious consequences have resulted from the act, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

### Section IV

Crimes against guard, post, patrol, internal and border service
Art. 389. (1) Whoever intentionally or by negligence violates the statutory rules of guard, post or patrol service or orders issued on the basis of these rules, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.
(2) If the crime has been committed at a facility of particularly important state or military significance, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(3) If the crime has been accompanied by the occurrence of harmful consequences, for the prevention of which the guard, post or patrol was established, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
(4) If the act under para. 2 and 3 constitutes a particularly serious case, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
Art. 390. With the punishment under the respective paragraphs of the preceding article shall also be punished a duty person who violates the established rules on observation or notification in connection with ensuring anti-aircraft, anti-atomic, anti-chemical or sanitary defense, as well as defense of water space.
Art. 391. (1) A person from the daily duty who intentionally or by negligence violates the statutory rules of internal service, shall be punished in non-trivial cases by deprivation of liberty for up to six months.
(2) If the crime has been accompanied by the occurrence of harmful consequences, for the prevention of which the duty was established, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to two years.
(3) If the act constitutes a particularly serious case, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
Art. 392. (1) A person from the composition of the border duty who intentionally or by negligence violates the rules on protection of the state border, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to three years.
(2) If the act has consisted in unauthorized abandonment or failure to occupy the guarded object or section of the state border, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
(3) For the crimes under the preceding paragraphs in particularly serious cases, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years.

### Section V

Other military crimes
Art. 393. (Amend. - SG, No. 26 of 2004) Whoever discloses in any manner information of a military nature constituting a state secret, or foreign classified information received under an international treaty to which the Republic of Bulgaria is a party, if not subject to more severe punishment, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
Art. 394. (Repealed - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982)
Art. 395. Whoever abandons or loses items given to him for personal use in service, shall be punished in non-trivial cases by deprivation of liberty for up to one year.

### Section VI

Military crimes committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country (Title suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006)
Art. 396. (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) For military crimes committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country, the punishment shall be:
1. under Art. 387, para. 3, first sentence, 388, para. 1 and 391 - deprivation of liberty for up to five years;
2. under Art. 372, para. 1, 373 in connection with Art. 372, para. 1, 374, 375, 380 and 387, para. 3, second sentence, 388, para. 2 - deprivation of liberty for up to eight years;
3. under Art. 381, 385, 386, para. 2, 387, para. 1 - deprivation of liberty from three to ten years;
4. under Art. 373, in connection with Art. 372, para. 2 and 3, 387, para. 2, 389, para. 2 and 3, and 390 - deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years.
Art. 397. (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 153 of 1998, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) For the crimes under Art. 372, para. 2 and 3, 376, 377, para. 2, 382, 383, para. 3, committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty from five to twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 153 of 1998, suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) For the crime under Art. 101, para. 2 and 386, para. 1, committed in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country, the punishment shall be deprivation of liberty of twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 397a. (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, effective 01.07.1982) (1) (Suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Whoever in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country unauthorizedly leaves the place of service or fails to appear for service for not more than one day, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.
(2) (Suppl. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, effective 13.10.2006) Whoever in wartime or in combat situation or during participation in a mission or operation outside the country by negligence fails to appear for service for more than one day, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for up to five years.

### Section VII

Crimes in Connection with Military Operations
Art. 398. (1) A commander who:
a) does not destroy or render unserviceable the means of warfare entrusted to him or does not take appropriate measures for their destruction or rendering unserviceable when they are threatened by imminent danger of capture by the enemy, or
b) although he has not received an order for military operations, but when it was necessary to act, does not timely cause the issuance of such an order or in urgent circumstances does not act on his own initiative, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years, insofar as the act does not constitute a more serious crime.
(2) If the act is committed through negligence, the punishment is deprivation of liberty up to five years.
Art. 399. (Amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) A commander of a sinking warship who, without having fulfilled his official duty to the end, abandons the ship, as well as a person from the ship's crew who abandons the ship without the order of his commander, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 400. (Amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever unauthorizedly leaves the battlefield during combat or surrenders into captivity due to fear or cowardice, or refuses during combat to act with weapons, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty of twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 401. Whoever, being in captivity, voluntarily participates in work that has immediate military significance, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.
Art. 402. Whoever, being in captivity, as a senior treats another prisoner of war cruelly, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.
Art. 403. Whoever, being in captivity, in order to obtain an advantage or to secure lenient treatment from the enemy, commits acts to the detriment of another prisoner of war, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years.
Art. 404. Whoever robs, steals, misappropriates, damages, destroys or unlawfully takes property of the population located in the area of military operations, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years, and in particularly serious cases - by deprivation of liberty from ten to fifteen years.
Art. 405. Whoever on the battlefield takes things from a wounded person, prisoner or killed person with the intention to unlawfully misappropriate them, if the act does not constitute a more serious crime, shall be punished for marauding by deprivation of liberty from three to fifteen years, and in particularly serious cases - by deprivation of liberty from ten to fifteen years.
Supplementary Provision
Art. 406. (1) (Amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998, amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) For the crimes under this chapter for which a punishment of deprivation of liberty of more than ten years or life imprisonment without substitution is provided, the court shall also order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 6, 7, 9 and 10.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 75 of 2006, effective from 13.10.2006) For the crimes under Art. 386 and 393, the court may order deprivation of rights under Art. 37, para. 1, points 9 and 10.
(3) (Para. 3 deleted, former para. 4, amended - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, issue 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006)
(4) (New - SG, issue 28 of 1982, effective from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, issue 86 of 2005, effective from 29.04.2006)
Chapter Fourteen.
CRIMES AGAINST PEACE AND HUMANITY

### Section I

Crimes Against Peace
Art. 407. Whoever conducts in any manner propaganda for war shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to eight years.
Art. 408. Whoever directly or indirectly, through press, word, radio or by other means seeks to provoke an armed attack by one state upon another, shall be punished for incitement to war by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
Art. 409. (Amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) Whoever plans, prepares or wages aggressive war shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from fifteen to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.

### Section II

Crimes Against the Laws and Customs of Warfare
Art. 410. Whoever in violation of the rules of international law for the conduct of war:
a) commits or orders to be committed against wounded, sick, shipwrecked persons or medical personnel murder, torture or inhuman treatment, including biological experiments, causes or orders to be caused to such persons severe suffering, mutilation or other injury to health;
b) commits or orders to be committed significant destruction or misappropriation of medical materials or installations,
(amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 411. Whoever in violation of the rules of international law for the conduct of war:
a) commits or orders to be committed against prisoners of war murder, torture or inhuman treatment, including biological experiments, causes or orders to be caused to such persons severe suffering, mutilation or other injury to health;
b) compels a prisoner to serve in the armed forces of the enemy state or
c) deprives a prisoner of his right to be tried by a regular court and by regular procedure,
(amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 412. Whoever in violation of the rules of international law for the conduct of war:
a) commits or orders to be committed against the civilian population murders, torture, inhuman treatment, including biological experiments, causes or orders to be caused severe suffering, mutilation or other serious injury to health;
b) takes or orders to be taken hostages;
c) commits or orders to be committed unlawful deportations, persecutions or detentions;
d) compels a civilian to serve in the armed forces of an enemy state;
e) deprives a civilian of his right to be tried by a regular court and by regular procedure;
f) unlawfully or arbitrarily commits or orders to be committed destruction or misappropriation of property on a large scale,
(amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 413. Whoever without having the right wears the emblem of the Red Cross or Red Crescent or whoever abuses the flag or emblem of the Red Cross or Red Crescent, or the color designated for vehicles for medical evacuation, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to two years.
Art. 414. (1) Whoever in violation of the rules of international law for the conduct of war destroys, damages or renders unserviceable cultural or historical monuments and objects, works of art, buildings and facilities with cultural, scientific or other humanitarian purpose, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to ten years.
(2) The same punishment shall be imposed on whoever steals, unlawfully misappropriates or conceals objects referred to in the preceding paragraph, or imposes in respect of such objects contribution or confiscation.
Art. 415. (1) (Supplemented - SG, issue 62 of 1997, amended and supplemented - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever in violation of the rules of international law for the conduct of war uses or orders to be used nuclear, chemical, bacteriological, biological or toxic weapons or prohibited means or methods of warfare, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from three to ten years.
(2) (Amended - SG, issue 153 of 1998) If particularly serious consequences have resulted therefrom, the punishment is deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 415a. (New - SG, issue 92 of 2002) Whoever undertakes military preparations for the use of nuclear, chemical, bacteriological, biological or toxic weapons as a method of warfare shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to six years.


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

### Section III

Destruction of groups of the population (genocide) and apartheid (Title suppl. - SG, No. 95 of 1975)
Art. 416. (1) Whoever, with the intent to destroy, in whole or in part, a particular national, ethnic, racial or religious group:
a) causes death, serious bodily injury or permanent mental disorder to a person belonging to such a group;
b) places the group in such living conditions that lead to its complete or partial physical destruction;
c) undertakes measures aimed at preventing births within such a group;
d) forcibly transfers children from one group to another,
(amend. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) shall be punished for genocide by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years or by life imprisonment without substitution.
(2) (Former Art. 417 - SG, No. 95 of 1975) Whoever commits preparation for genocide shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from two to eight years.
(3) (Former Art. 418 - SG, No. 95 of 1975) Whoever publicly and directly incites to genocide shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to eight years.
Art. 417. (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975) Whoever, with the intent to establish or maintain domination or systematic oppression of one racial group of people over another racial group of people:
a) causes death or serious bodily injury to one or more persons from that group of people or
b) imposes living conditions of a nature to cause complete or partial physical destruction of a racial group of people,
(amend. - SG, No. 153 of 1998) shall be punished for apartheid by deprivation of liberty from ten to twenty years or life imprisonment without substitution.
Art. 418. (New - SG, No. 95 of 1975) Whoever with the intent under the preceding article:
a) unlawfully deprives of liberty members of a racial group of people or subjects them to forced labour;
b) puts into effect measures to prevent the participation of a racial group of people in the political, social, economic and cultural life of the country and to deliberately create conditions that hinder the full development of such a group of people, in particular by depriving its members of the fundamental freedoms and rights of citizens;
c) puts into effect measures to divide the population by racial criteria through the creation of reserves and ghettos, through the prohibition of mixed marriages between members of different racial groups or through the expropriation of land property belonging to them;
d) deprives of fundamental rights and freedoms organizations or persons because they oppose apartheid,
shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from five to fifteen years.
Additional provisions (Title amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011)
Art. 419. In accordance with the distinctions under the preceding articles, whoever knowingly permits his subordinate to commit a crime provided for in this chapter shall also be punished.
Art. 419a. (New - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, No. 67 of 2023) (1) Whoever publicly justifies, denies or grossly trivializes a committed crime under this chapter and the act is committed in a manner that may create a danger of violence being exercised or hatred being created against individuals or groups of people united by race, colour, religion, origin, national or ethnic affiliation, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty from one to five years.
(2) The punishment under para. 1 shall also be imposed when the crime under this chapter was committed during and in connection with the National Socialist regime.
(3) Whoever incites another to a crime under para. 1 and 2 shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to one year.
Transitional provisions
Art. 420. This code enters into force on 1 May 1968 and repeals:
1. The Criminal Code of 1951,
2. The Law for the Protection of Peace of 1951,
3. The Decree for strengthening the fight against persons deviating from socially useful labour and leading an anti-social, parasitic way of life.
Art. 421. Persons sentenced to deprivation of the right to be electors or eligible or of the right to receive a certain pension (Art. 28, points 1 and 4 of the Criminal Law of 1951) shall be released from that punishment from the day of entry into force of this code.
Art. 422. The death penalty of persons sentenced for crimes for which such a penalty is not provided for under this code shall be replaced by deprivation of liberty for a term of twenty years. This provision enters into force from the day of promulgation of this code.
Art. 423. The measures imposed before the entry into force of this code under the Decree for strengthening the fight against persons deviating from socially useful labour and leading an anti-social, parasitic way of life shall be executed according to the procedure established by it, with the maximum term of these measures under Art. 1, para. 1, letter "b" being reduced to three years.
Art. 424. (Amend. - SG, No. 92 of 1969, amend. - SG, No. 95 of 1975, amend. - SG, No. 3 of 1977, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1979, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 1 of 1991) (1) (Amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, suppl. - SG, No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) For the acts under Art. 135, para. 5, Art. 218b, Art. 225b, para. 3, Art. 228, para. 3, Art. 231, para. 2, Art. 232, para. 3, Art. 242, para. 6, Art. 352a, para. 5 and Art. 353, para. 4, the provisions of the Law on Administrative Violations and Penalties shall apply.
(2) (Amend. - SG, No. 3 of 1977, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1979, amend. - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982; suppl. - SG, No. 62 of 1997) The penal decrees shall be issued:
a) (amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) under Art. 135, para. 5 - by the Minister of Health;
b) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, SG, No. 10 of 1993; suppl. - SG No. 62 of 1997, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, No. 14 of 2015) under Art. 225b, para. 3 - by the Minister of Finance; under Art. 228, para. 3 and Art. 231, para. 2 - by the Minister of Economy or by the Chairman of the State Agency for Metrological and Technical Surveillance;
c) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011, amend. - SG, No. 14 of 2015) under Art. 232, para. 3 - by the Minister of Economy, by the Minister of Finance or by the Chairman of the State Agency for Metrological and Technical Surveillance;
d) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 51 of 2000, amend. - SG, No. 75 of 2006, in force from 13.10.2006) under Art. 242, para. 6 - by the Minister of Finance;
e) (amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 93 of 2009, in force from 25.12.2009, amend. - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011) under Art. 352a, para. 5 and Art. 353, para. 4 - by the Minister of Environment and Water, and when the violation is in connection with pollution of marine waters - by the Minister of Environment and Water or by the Minister of Transport, Information Technology and Communications.
f) (amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986; repealed - SG, No. 10 of 1993)
g) (new - SG, No. 89 of 1986; repealed - SG, No. 1 of 1991)
(3) (Amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986) In the cases under the preceding paragraph, the penal decrees may also be issued by officials designated by the heads of the respective departments.
(4) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, repealed - SG, No. 33 of 2011, in force from 27.05.2011)
(5) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend. - SG, No. 10 of 1993) The administrative penalty fine under Art. 218b shall be imposed by a penal decree by the mayor, on the basis of materials sent to him by the prosecutor, or on an act from the administration of the enterprise, the institution or from the control bodies. When the violation is established by the bodies of the Ministry of Internal Affairs, the penal decree shall be issued by the Minister of Internal Affairs or by a person authorized by him.
(6) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, amend. - SG, No. 86 of 2005, in force from 29.04.2006) With respect to military personnel, as well as officers and sergeants and rank-and-file personnel from other departments, the administrative penalties provided for in this code shall be imposed by the respective commanders and chiefs having the right to impose disciplinary penalties on them. In this case, appeals against the penal decrees shall be considered by a military court.
(7) (New - SG, No. 28 of 1982, in force from 01.07.1982, repealed - SG, No. 86 of 2005, in force from 29.04.2006)
(8) (New - SG, No. 89 of 1986, amend., SG, No. 10 of 1993, amend. - SG, No. 92 of 2002) In minor cases of violations under Art. 225b, para. 3, established at the time of their commission, the authorized control bodies may impose on the spot a fine from one hundred to three hundred leva according to the procedure of Art. 39, para. 2 of the Law on Administrative Violations and Penalties.
Art. 425. (New - SG, No. 10 of 1993, repealed - SG, No. 51 of 1999)
Art. 426. (New - SG, No. 50 of 1995) Persons who, on the basis of the former Art. 343b, second sentence, have been deprived of the right to drive a motor vehicle forever, may restore their right not earlier than three years from serving the sentence of deprivation of liberty and after passing an examination for acquiring the right to drive a motor vehicle.
AMENDMENTS TO OTHER LAWS


This excerpt sits under: | Constitution | Regulations |

#### § 1. In the Law on Bulgarian Citizenship

In Art. 26 the words "and in particularly important cases - with deprivation of liberty" shall be deleted.

#### § 2. In the Law on Forests

1. In Art. 42, para. 1 after the words "cut down or fallen" the words "when the amount of damage is below one hundred leva" shall be added.
Paragraph 2 of the same article shall be repealed.
2. In Art. 43 the words "paragraph 1" and the words at the end "and in the cases under para. 2 of the same article - with deprivation of liberty up to three years or with correctional labour" shall be deleted.
3. In Art. 45, para. 1 after the words "forest nursery" the words "when the amount of damage is below one hundred leva" shall be added.
Paragraph 2 of the same article shall be repealed.
4. In Art. 52, para. 2 the words "with deprivation of liberty up to three years or with correctional labour" shall be replaced with the words "with a fine up to five hundred leva".

#### § 3. In the Law on Protection of Plants from Diseases and Pests

In Art. 10, para. 1 after the words "quarantine of plants" the words "insofar as the act committed does not constitute a crime" shall be added, and the words "with deprivation of liberty up to five years, and in minor cases" shall be deleted.

#### § 4. In the Law on Customs

1. In Art. 51, para. 1 after the words "according to the established procedure" the words "insofar as the act committed does not constitute a crime" shall be added.
2. Paragraph 2 of Art. 51 shall be amended as follows:
"The goods, object of smuggling, as well as the vehicles and carrying means, which served primarily for their transportation or carrying across the border, regardless of whose property they are, shall be forfeited to the state, and if they are missing or have been alienated, their equivalent value at the respective state retail prices shall be awarded".
3. In Articles 52, 53, 54 and 59 the words "shall be confiscated" shall be replaced with the words "shall be forfeited to the state", and in Art. 61 the words "to confiscation" shall be replaced with the words "to forfeiture to the state".
4. Articles 55 and 57 shall be repealed.

#### § 5. In the Law on Transactions with Foreign Currency Values and Foreign Currency Control

1. In Art. 37 and 38, para. 2 the words "under Art. 240" shall be replaced with the words "under Art. 250".

#### 2. Article 39 shall be amended as follows

"For the acts under Art. 250, para. 3 of the Penal Code the provisions of Chapter XXVIII of the Code of Criminal Procedure shall apply.
The penal decrees shall be issued by the Minister of Finance or by officials designated by him.
If the value of the object of the act is up to twenty leva, a fine in double amount shall be imposed by the body that established the violation, or by another duly authorized person. The imposition of the fine shall be subject to appeal before the Ministry of Finance.

#### § 6. In the Law on Rents

In Art. 37 the words "with deprivation of liberty up to one year or" shall be deleted.

#### § 7. In the Law on Protection of Air, Waters and Soil from Pollution

Articles 18 and 19 shall be repealed.

#### § 8. In the Law on Planned Development of Populated Places

In Art. 67, para. 1 the words "with imprisonment up to one year" shall be replaced with the words "with a fine up to one thousand leva".

#### § 9. In the Law on Hunting

#### Article 21 shall be amended as follows

"Whoever kills or catches without proper authorization large game, such as deer, doe, wild goat, fallow deer, roe deer, bear, wild boar, shall be punished under Art. 237, para. 1 of the Penal Code.
Whoever, without possessing a hunting license, kills or catches hare, marten, squirrel, capercaillie, pheasant, partridge or chukar, as well as one who, even if possessing a hunting license, kills or catches such game in prohibited time, in prohibited place or with prohibited means, shall be punished under Art. 237, para. 2 of the Penal Code.
Whoever, without possessing a hunting license, kills or catches wild goose, wild duck, bustard, coot, weasel, pigeon, turtle dove, quail and all other types of useful game, subject to hunting, shall be punished with a fine up to fifty leva.
With the punishment under the previous paragraph shall also be punished one who, even if possessing a hunting license, kills or catches such game in prohibited time, in prohibited place or with prohibited means.
The killed or caught game shall be forfeited to the state, and if it is missing or has been alienated, its equivalent value shall be paid".

2. Articles 24 and 35 shall be repealed.

In Art. 14 the words "deprivation of liberty" shall be deleted.
